## I / 塞斯蒂纳的故事

===Original===
"Armatz de fust e de fer e d'acier,

Mos ostal seran bosc, fregz, e semdier,

E mas cansos sestinas e descortz,

E mantenrai los frevols contra 'ls fortz."

===Chinese===
"我身披木与铁与钢的铠甲，

我的居所将是森林、寒冷与道路，

我的歌是塞斯蒂纳与争辩曲，

我将护佑弱者对抗强者。"

===Original===
The first novel.——Alianora of Provence, coming in disguise and in adversity to a certain clerk, is by him conducted across a hostile country; and in that troubled journey are made manifest to each the snares which had beguiled them aforetime.

===Chinese===
第一篇传奇。——普罗旺斯的艾丽亚诺拉（Alianora），乔装潦倒来到一位教士面前，由他引领穿越敌国；在这段艰险旅途中，彼此得以洞见从前将他们引入歧途的种种陷阱。

===Original===
In this place we have to do with the opening tale of the Dizain of Queens. I abridge, as afterward, at discretion; and an initial account of the Barons' War, among other superfluities, I amputate as more remarkable for veracity than interest. The result, we will agree at outset, is that to the Norman cleric appertains whatever these tales may have of merit, whereas what you find distasteful in them you must impute to my delinquencies in skill rather than in volition.

===Chinese===
此处所述乃《十女王传》（Dizain of Queens）的开篇故事。我如后文一样，酌情删繁就简；关于男爵战争的初始叙述及其他冗余之处，因所述虽属确凿却乏味可厌，一并割爱。结果，我们在开篇便达成共识：这些故事若有可取之处，当归功于那位诺曼教士；而其中令诸位感到不适之处，务必归咎于我的技艺不精，而非用心不诚。

===Original===
Within the half hour after de Giars' death (here one overtakes Nicolas mid-course in narrative) Dame Alianora thus stood alone in the corridor of a strange house. Beyond the arras the steward and his lord were at irritable converse.

===Chinese===
在德·雅尔（de Giars）死后半小时内（此处叙事追上了尼古拉斯的讲述），艾丽亚诺拉夫人独自伫立于一所陌生宅邸的走廊中。帷幔彼端，管家正与主人恼怒地交谈。

===Original===
First, "If the woman be hungry," spoke a high and peevish voice, "feed her. If she need money, give it to her. But do not annoy me."

===Chinese===
首先传来一个尖厉而不耐烦的声音："若这女人饿了，就喂她。若她需要钱，就给她。但别来烦我。"

===Original===
"This woman demands to see the master of the house," the steward then retorted.

===Chinese===
"这女人要求面见宅邸的主人，"管家反驳道。

===Original===
"O incredible Boeotian, inform her that the master of the house has no time to waste upon vagabonds who select the middle of the night as an eligible time to pop out of nowhere. Why did you not do so in the beginning, you dolt?" The speaker got for answer only a deferential cough, and very shortly continued: "This is remarkably vexatious. *Vox et praeterea nihil*——which signifies, Yeck, that to converse with women is always delightful. Admit her." This was done, and Dame Alianora came into an apartment littered with papers, where a neat and shriveled gentleman of fifty-odd sat at a desk and scowled.

===Chinese===
"噢，不可思议的波奥提亚人（Boeotian）！告诉她，宅邸的主人没有闲暇去搭理那些偏偏选在半夜三更从不知何处冒出来的流浪汉。你当初为何不这样打发她，你这蠢货？"说话者得到的回答不过是一声恭敬的咳嗽，随即他很快又说："这实在令人恼火。*Vox et praeterea nihil*（空有声音，别无他物）——叶克（Yeck），此话的意思是，与女子交谈总是令人愉快的。让她进来。"门开了，艾丽亚诺拉夫人走进一间堆满纸张的房间，一位年过五旬、整洁而清瘦的绅士坐在书桌前，满脸阴云。

===Original===
He presently said, "You may go, Yeck." He had risen, the magisterial attitude with which he had awaited her entrance cast aside. "Oh, God!" he said; "you, madame!" His thin hands, scholarly hands, were plucking at the air.

===Chinese===
他随即说道："你可以退下了，叶克。"他已站起身来，此前等候她进门时那副威严的架势荡然无存。"噢，天哪！"他说，"是您，夫人！"他那双纤细的、书生的手在空中无助地抓挠着。

===Original===
Dame Alianora had paused, greatly astonished, and there was an interval before she said, "I do not recognize you, messire."

===Chinese===
艾丽亚诺拉夫人驻足不动，大为惊异，隔了好一会儿才说："我不认得您，骁骑爵士（Messire）。"

===Original===
"And yet, madame, I recall very clearly that some thirty years ago the King-Count Raymond Bérenger, then reigning in Provence, had about his court four daughters, each one of whom was afterward wedded to a king. First, Meregrett, the eldest, now regnant in France; then Alianora, the second and most beautiful of these daughters, whom troubadours hymned as the Unattainable Princess. She was married a long while ago, madame, to the King of England, Lord Henry, third of that name to reign in these islands."

===Chinese===
"然而，夫人，我记得清清楚楚，大约三十年前，普罗旺斯在位的国王-伯爵雷蒙·贝伦杰（Raymond Bérenger）身边有四位公主，后来每人都嫁给了国王。先是长女梅雷格蕾特（Meregrett），如今是法兰西的王后；继而是次女、诸女中最美丽的艾丽亚诺拉，吟游诗人称颂她为'不可企及的公主'。她很久以前便嫁给了英格兰国王亨利大人（Lord Henry），乃此岛国第三位以亨利为名的君主。"

===Original===
Dame Alianora's eyes were narrowing. "There is something in your voice," she said, "which I recall."

===Chinese===
艾丽亚诺拉夫人的双眸眯了起来。"您的声音里有某种东西，"她说，"我记得。"

===Original===
He answered: "Madame and Queen, that is very likely, for it is a voice which sang a deal in Provence when both of us were younger. I concede with the Roman that I have somewhat deteriorated since the reign of Cynara. Yet have you quite forgotten the Englishman who made so many songs of you? They called him Osmund Heleigh."

===Chinese===
他答道："夫人，也是王后陛下，这极有可能，因为这副嗓子在普罗旺斯唱过不少歌，那时你我都还年轻。我承认，按那位罗马人的说法，自我爱上茜娜拉（Cynara）的时代以来，我已经衰老了许多。然而，您当真忘了那个为您谱了那么多歌的英格兰人？他们叫他奥斯蒙德·黑利（Osmund Heleigh）。"

===Original===
"He made the Sestina of Spring which won the violet crown at my betrothal," the Queen said; and then, with eagerness: "Messire, can it be that you are Osmund Heleigh?" He shrugged assent. She looked at him for a long time, rather sadly, and demanded if he were the King's man or of the barons' party.

===Chinese===
"他在我的订婚礼上谱写了那首《春之塞斯蒂纳》（Sestina of Spring），赢得了紫罗兰花冠，"王后说；继而急切地追问："骁骑爵士，莫非您就是奥斯蒙德·黑利？"他耸肩以示承认。她久久地凝视他，目光中有几分悲凉，然后问他究竟是国王的人还是男爵一派。

===Original===
The nervous hands were raised in deprecation. "I have no politics," Messire Heleigh began, and altered it, gallantly enough, to, "I am the Queen's man, madame."

===Chinese===
那双紧张的手抬起以示否认。"我不涉政事，"黑利骁骑爵士开口道，随即改口——改得颇为殷勤——"我是王后陛下的人，夫人。"

===Original===
"Then aid me, Osmund," she said.

===Chinese===
"那就帮我吧，奥斯蒙德，"她说。

===Original===
He answered with a gravity which singularly became him, "You have reason to understand that to my fullest power I will aid you."

===Chinese===
他以一种与他格外相称的庄重口吻答道："您有理由相信，我将竭尽全力相助。"

===Original===
"You know that at Lewes these swine overcame us." He nodded assent. "Now they hold the King, my husband, captive at Kenilworth. I am content that he remain there, for he is of all the King's enemies the most dangerous. But, at Wallingford, Leicester has imprisoned my son, Prince Edward. The Prince must be freed, my Osmund. Warren de Basingbourne commands what is left of the royal army, now entrenched at Bristol, and it is he who must liberate my son. Get me to Bristol, then. Afterward we will take Wallingford." The Queen issued these orders in cheery, practical fashion, and did not admit opposition into the account, for she was a capable woman.

===Chinese===
"您知道在刘易斯（Lewes），那些猪猡打败了我们。"他点头表示知晓。"如今他们将我的丈夫——国王——囚禁在凯尼尔沃思（Kenilworth）。我倒情愿他留在那儿，因为他是所有国王之敌中最危险的一个。但莱斯特（Leicester）将我的儿子爱德华王子（Prince Edward）囚于沃灵福德（Wallingford）。王子必须获救，我的奥斯蒙德。沃伦·德·贝辛伯恩（Warren de Basingbourne）统率着王军残部，现驻守布里斯托尔（Bristol），由他来解救我的儿子。那么，护送我到布里斯托尔去。之后我们再拿下沃灵福德。"王后以欢快而务实的方式发布了这些命令，不容许任何异议，因为她是个果决的女人。

===Original===
"But you, madame?" he stammered. "You came alone?"

===Chinese===
"可是您呢，夫人？"他结结巴巴地说，"您是独自来的？"

===Original===
"I come from France, where I have been entreating——and vainly entreating——succor from yet another monkish king, the holy Lewis of that realm. Eh, what is God about when He enthrones these whining pieties! Were I a king, were I even a man, I would drive these smug English out of their foggy isle in three days' space! I would leave alive not one of these curs that dare yelp at me! I would——" She paused, anger veering into amusement. "See how I enrage myself when I think of what your people have made me suffer," the Queen said, and shrugged her shoulders. "In effect, I skulked back in disguise to this detestable island, accompanied by Avenel de Giars and Hubert Fitz-Herveis. Tonight some half-dozen fellows——robbers, thorough knaves, like all you English——attacked us on the common yonder and slew the men of our party. While they were cutting de Giars' throat I slipped away in the dark and tumbled through many ditches till I spied your light. There you have my story. Now get me an escort to Bristol."

===Chinese===
"我从法兰西来，在那里我一直在恳求——徒劳地恳求——那个王国里又一位僧侣般国王的援助，即圣路易（Lewis）。唉，上帝高坐云端，面对那些呜呜咽咽的虔信者究竟在做什么！假若我是一国之君，哪怕是堂堂男儿，我三天之内便将这些自鸣得意的英格兰人从他们的雾霭小岛上赶出去！我要叫那些竟敢对我狂吠的杂种一个不留地活不成！我要——"她住了口，怒气转为好笑。"瞧，一想到你们英格兰人让我受的苦，我就忍不住发火，"王后说着，耸了耸肩。"总而言之，我乔装潜回了这座可恨的岛，随行的是阿韦内尔·德·雅尔（Avenel de Giars）和休伯特·菲茨-赫维斯（Hubert Fitz-Herveis）。今晚大约五六个家伙——强盗，彻头彻尾的恶棍，跟你们所有英格兰人一样——在那边的荒地上袭击了我们，杀了我们的人。趁他们割雅尔的喉咙时，我在黑暗中溜走了，跌跌撞撞过了许多沟渠，直到看见了您的灯光。这就是我的经历。现在，给我一支护卫队去布里斯托尔。"

===Original===
It was a long while before Messire Heleigh spoke. Then, "These men," he said——"this de Giars and this Fitz-Herveis——they gave their lives for yours, as I understand it——*pro caris amicis*. And yet you do not grieve for them."

===Chinese===
黑利骁骑爵士过了好一会儿才开口。"这些人，"他说——"这个德·雅尔和这个菲茨-赫维斯——据我所知，他们为你献出了生命——*pro caris amicis*（为了亲爱的朋友）。然而你并不为他们悲伤。"

===Original===
"I shall regret de Giars," the Queen acknowledged, "for he made excellent songs. But Fitz-Herveis?——foh! the man had a face like a horse." Again her mood changed. "Many persons have died for me, my friend. At first I wept for them, but now I am dry of tears."

===Chinese===
"我会怀念德·雅尔的，"王后承认道，"因为他写了极好的歌。但菲茨-赫维斯？——呸！那人的脸跟马一样。"她的情绪又变了。"有许多人曾为我而死，我的朋友。起初我为他们哭泣，如今我的泪已流干。"

===Original===
He shook his head. "Cato very wisely says, 'If thou hast need of help, ask it of thy friends.' But the sweet friend that I remember was a clean eyed girl, joyous and exceedingly beautiful. Now you appear to me one of those ladies of remoter times——Faustina, or Jael, or Artemis, the King's wife of Tauris——they that slew men, laughing. I am somewhat afraid of you, madame."

===Chinese===
他摇了摇头。"加图（Cato）极为明智地说过：'若你需要帮助，就向朋友求助。'但我记忆中那位甜蜜的朋友是个目光清澈的少女，欢快而绝美。如今您在我看来，是那些久远时代的女子——福斯蒂娜（Faustina）或雅亿（Jael）或阿耳忒弥斯（Artemis），陶里斯国王之妻——她们笑着杀人。我有些怕您，夫人。"

===Original===
She was angry at first; then her face softened. "You English!" she said, only half mirthful. "Eh, my God! you remember me when I was a high hearted young sorceress. Now the powers of the Apsarasas have departed from me, and time has thrust that Alianora, who was once the Unattainable Princess, chin deep in misery. Yet even now I am your Queen, messire, and it is not yours to pass judgment upon me."

===Chinese===
她先是愤怒，继而面色柔和下来。"你们英格兰人！"她说，半是打趣。"唉，我的上帝！您记得的还是那个意气风发的年轻女巫。如今阿布萨罗（Apsarasas）的法力已离我而去，时间将那个曾是'不可企及的公主'的艾丽亚诺拉推入齐颈深的苦难。但即便如此，我仍是您的王后，骁骑爵士，由不得您来对我评头论足。"

===Original===
"I do not judge you," he returned. "Rather I cry with him of old, *Omnia incerta ratione*! and I cry with Salomon that he who meddles with the strife of another man is like to him that takes a hound by the ears. Yet listen, madame and Queen. I cannot afford you an escort to Bristol. This house, of which I am in temporary charge, is Longaville, my brother's manor. Lord Brudenel, as you doubtless know, is of the barons' party and——scant cause for grief!——is with Leicester at this moment. I can trust none of my brother's people, for I believe them to be of much the same opinion as those Londoners who not long ago stoned you and would have sunk your barge in Thames River. Oh, let us not blink the fact that you are not overbeloved in England. So an escort is out of the question. Yet I, madame, if you so elect, will see you safe to Bristol."

===Chinese===
"我没有评判您，"他答道。"我不过是像古人那样感叹：*Omnia incerta ratione*（万事皆无定论）！我也像所罗门（Salomon）那样说，插手他人争端的人好比抓住猎犬的耳朵。然而请听我说，夫人兼王后。我无法为您安排一支去布里斯托尔的护卫队。这座宅邸——我暂时代管——是朗阿维尔（Longaville），我兄长的庄园。布鲁德尔勋爵（Lord Brudenel），您想必知道，属男爵一派，而且——没什么好伤心的——此刻正追随莱斯特。我不能信任我兄长的任何手下，因为他们与不久前向您投石、险些将您的船凿沉在泰晤士河里的那些伦敦人想法如出一辙。哦，我们不要讳言您在英格兰并不太受爱戴。因此护卫队是不可能的。然而，夫人，如果您愿意，我将亲自护送您安全抵达布里斯托尔。"

===Original===
"You? Singly?" the Queen demanded.

===Chinese===
"就您一个人？"王后追问道。

===Original===
"My plan is this: Singing folk alone travel whither they will. We will go as jongleurs, then. I can yet manage a song to the viol, I dare affirm. And you must pass as my wife."

===Chinese===
"我的计划是这样的：唯有卖艺之人才能通行无阻。那么，我们就扮作行吟艺人。我敢说，我还能拉着手摇琴唱支歌。而您必须假扮我的妻子。"

===Original===
He said this with simplicity. The plan seemed unreasonable, and at first Dame Alianora waved it aside. Out of the question! But reflection suggested nothing better; it was impossible to remain at Longaville, and the man spoke sober truth when he declared any escort other than himself to be unprocurable. Besides, the lunar madness of the scheme was its strength; that the Queen would venture to cross half England unprotected——and Messire Heleigh on the face of him was a pasteboard buckler——was an event which Leicester would neither anticipate nor on report credit. There you were! these English had no imagination. The Queen snapped her fingers and said: "Very willingly will I be your wife, my Osmund. But how do I know that I can trust you? Leicester would give a deal for me; he would pay any price for the pious joy of burning the Sorceress of Provence. And you are not wealthy, I suspect."

===Chinese===
他说这话时态度坦率。这计划似乎荒诞不经，艾丽亚诺拉夫人起初挥手否决。绝无可能！但思来想去也想不出更好的办法；留在朗阿维尔是不可能的，而他所言——除了他自己，无人可充任护卫——乃不争的事实。况且，这疯狂至极的计划正是其优势所在；王后竟敢只身穿越半个英格兰——而黑利骁骑爵士看上去不过是一面纸板盾牌——此等事件莱斯特既不会预料到，即便听到报告也不会相信。就是这样！这些英格兰人毫无想象力。王后打了个响指，说道："我非常乐意做您的妻子，我的奥斯蒙德。但我怎能确信我信任您呢？莱斯特会为我开出大价钱；为了一把火烧掉普罗旺斯女巫的虔诚快意，他什么都肯付。而且我猜您并不富裕。"

===Original===
"You may trust me, *mon bel esper*,"——his eyes here were those of a beaten child——"because my memory is better than yours." Messire Osmund Heleigh gathered his papers into a neat pile. "This room is mine. Tonight I keep guard in the corridor, madame. We will start at dawn."

===Chinese===
"您大可信任我，*mon bel esper*（我最美好的希望），"——此刻他的眼睛像一个受了委屈的孩子——"因为我的记性比您好。"奥斯蒙德·黑利骁骑爵士将他的纸张归拢成整齐的一摞。"这间房归我。今晚我在走廊里守夜，夫人。我们天亮就出发。"

===Original===
When he had gone, Dame Alianora laughed contentedly. "*Mon bel esper!* my fairest hope! The man called me that in his verses——thirty years ago! Yes, I may trust you, my poor Osmund."

===Chinese===
他走后，艾丽亚诺拉夫人满意地笑了。"*Mon bel esper!* 我最美好的希望！此人在他的诗中这样称呼过我——三十年前！是的，我可以信任你，我可怜的奥斯蒙德。"

===Original===
So they set out at cockcrow. He had procured for himself a viol and a long falchion, and had somewhere got suitable clothes for the Queen; and in their aging but decent garb the two approached near enough to the appearance of what they desired to be thought. In the courtyard a knot of servants gaped, nudged one another, but openly said nothing. Messire Heleigh, as they interpreted it, was brazening out an affair of gallantry before the countryside; and they esteemed his casual observation that they would find a couple of dead men on the common exceedingly diverting.

===Chinese===
于是他们在鸡鸣时分出发。他弄到了一把手摇琴和一柄长弯刀，还从不知何处为王后找到了合适的衣裳；二人穿着虽旧却体面的衣装，看上去与他们希望被认作的身份相去不远。院子里一群仆人目瞪口呆，互相推搡，但谁也不明说。在他们看来，黑利骁骑爵士不过是厚颜无耻地在乡间搞一桩风流韵事；他们觉得他随口说的那句"在荒地上会找到两具死尸"着实有趣。

===Original===
When the Queen asked him the same morning, "And what will you sing, my Osmund? Shall we begin the practise of our new profession with the Sestina of Spring?"——old Osmund Heleigh grunted out: "I have forgotten that rubbish long ago. *Omnis amans, amens*, saith the satirist of Rome town, and with reason."

===Chinese===
那天早上王后问他："您打算唱什么，我的奥斯蒙德？我们践行新行当，是否先来一首《春之塞斯蒂纳》？"——老奥斯蒙德·黑利哼了一声说："那堆废话我老早忘了。*Omnis amans, amens*（每个恋人都是疯子），罗马城的讽刺诗人如是说，此言不虚。"

===Original===
Followed silence.

===Chinese===
继而是一阵沉默。

===Original===
One sees them thus trudging the brown, naked plains under a sky of steel. In a pageant the woman, full-veined and comely, her russet gown girded up like a harvester's might not inaptly have prefigured October; and for less comfortable November you could nowhere have found a symbol more precise than her lank companion, humorously peevish under his white thatch of hair, and constantly fretted by the sword tapping at his ankles.

===Chinese===
但见二人跋涉在褐黄裸露的平原上，头顶是铁灰色的天空。若在庆典游行中，这妇人丰腴健美，赭色裙裾像收割女工那样束起，恰如十月的化身；而那个阴郁寡欢的月份——十一月，再没有比她身旁这位消瘦的同伴更贴切的象征了：白发如茅草覆顶，诙谐而易怒，腰间佩剑不时敲打脚踝，令他烦躁不已。

===Original===
They made Hurlburt prosperously and found it vacant, for the news of Falmouth's advance had driven the villagers hillward. There was in this place a child, a naked boy of some two years, lying on a doorstep, overlooked in his elders' gross terror. As the Queen with a sob lifted this boy the child died.

===Chinese===
他们顺利抵达赫尔伯特（Hurlburt），发现村中空无一人，因为法尔茅斯（Falmouth）逼近的消息已驱使村民逃往山中。这里有一个孩子——一个赤身裸体的两岁左右的男孩——躺在门阶上，在大人们惊恐万状的奔逃中被遗忘了。当王后呜咽着抱起这孩子时，他已死了。

===Original===
"Starved!" said Osmund Heleigh; "and within a stone's throw of my snug home!"

===Chinese===
"饿死的！"奥斯蒙德·黑利说，"而且离我温暖的家不过一箭之遥！"

===Original===
The Queen laid down the tiny corpse, and, stooping, lightly caressed its sparse flaxen hair. She answered nothing, though her lips moved.

===Chinese===
王后放下那小小的尸体，俯身轻轻抚了抚那稀疏的亚麻色头发。她什么也没说，尽管嘴唇微微翕动。

===Original===
Past Vachel, scene of a recent skirmish, with many dead in the gutters, they were overtaken by Falmouth himself, and stood at the roadside to afford his troop passage. The Marquess, as he went by, flung the Queen a coin, with a jest sufficiently high flavored. She knew the man her inveterate enemy, knew that on recognition he would have killed her as he would a wolf; she smiled at him and dropped a curtsey.

===Chinese===
经过瓦谢尔（Vachel）——此处不久前刚发生过一场小规模战斗，沟渠中满是尸体——他们被法尔茅斯本人追上了，便站在路旁让他的部队先行。侯爵经过时扔给王后一枚硬币，说了句相当粗俗的玩笑话。她知道此人是她的死敌，知道一旦认出她便会像杀狼一样杀掉她；她朝他微微一笑，行了个屈膝礼。

===Original===
"This is remarkable," Messire Heleigh observed. "I was hideously afraid, and am yet shaking. But you, madame, laughed."

===Chinese===
"这可了不起，"黑利骁骑爵士评论道。"我怕得要命，现在还在发抖。而您，夫人，竟笑了。"

===Original===
The Queen replied: "I laughed because I know that some day I shall have Lord Falmouth's head. It will be very sweet to see it roll in the dust, my Osmund."

===Chinese===
王后答道："我笑，是因为我知道总有一天我会得到法尔茅斯勋爵的首级。看它在尘土中滚动，那将是很甜蜜的事，我的奥斯蒙德。"

===Original===
Messire Heleigh somewhat dryly observed that tastes differed.

===Chinese===
黑利骁骑爵士不咸不淡地说，人各有所好。

===Original===
At Jessop Minor befell a more threatening adventure. Seeking food at the Cat and Hautbois in that village, they blundered upon the same troop at dinner in the square about the inn. Falmouth and his lieutenants were somewhere inside the house. The men greeted the supposed purveyors of amusement with a shout; and one of these soldiers——a swarthy rascal with his head tied in a napkin——demanded that the jongleurs grace their meal with a song.

===Chinese===
在杰索普·迈诺尔（Jessop Minor），他们遭遇了更险恶的境况。在村中"猫与号角"（Cat and Hautbois）客栈寻找食物时，他们撞见法尔茅斯那支部队正在客栈周围的广场上用餐。法尔茅斯和他的副官们在屋内某处。士兵们高声招呼这两位被认为是以卖艺为生的人；其中一个黝黑的无赖——脑袋上缠着餐巾——命令行吟艺人为他们的饭局唱支歌助兴。

===Original===
Osmund tried to put him off with a tale of a broken viol.

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德试图以手摇琴坏了为由搪塞过去。

===Original===
But, "Haro!" the fellow blustered; "by blood and by nails! you will sing more sweetly with a broken viol than with a broken head. I would have you understand, you hedge thief, that we gentlemen of the sword are not partial to wordy argument." Messire Heleigh fluttered inefficient hands as the men-at-arms gathered about them, scenting some genial piece of cruelty. "Oh, you rabbit!" the trooper jeered, and caught at Osmund's throat, shaking him. In the act this rascal tore open Messire Heleigh's tunic, disclosing a thin chain about his neck and a handsome locket, which the fellow wrested from its fastening. "Ahoi!" he continued. "Ahoi, my comrades, what sort of minstrel is this, who goes about England all hung with gold like a Cathedral Virgin! He and his sweetheart"——the actual word was grosser——"will be none the worse for an interview with the Marquess."

===Chinese===
然而那家伙咆哮道："天杀的！以血以指甲起誓！断了琴的歌声总比断了脑袋的强。我让你明白，你这田埂上的贼，我们执剑的绅士们不喜欢费口舌讲道理。"黑利骁骑爵士徒劳地挥动着他那双无力的手，周围的甲兵围拢过来，嗅到了某种令人愉快的残酷气息。"噢，你这胆小鬼！"骑兵嘲笑着，一把掐住奥斯蒙德的脖子摇晃他。挣扎间这恶棍扯开了黑利骁骑爵士的束腰外衣，露出他脖颈上一条细金链和一个精美的挂坠盒，那人一把将挂坠盒扯了下来。"喂！"他接着嚷道。"喂，伙计们，这是什么行吟艺人，浑身挂满金饰，活像个大教堂里的圣母像！他和他的小妞儿"——实际用的词更加粗鄙——"去见侯爵一面也不亏。"

===Original===
The situation smacked of awkwardness, because Lord Falmouth was familiar with the Queen, and to be brought specifically to his attention meant death for two detected masqueraders. Hastily Osmund Heleigh said:

===Chinese===
局面颇为棘手，因为法尔茅斯勋爵认识王后，一旦被引到他面前，两个被识破的假扮者必死无疑。奥斯蒙德·黑利急忙说道：

===Original===
"Messire, the locket contains the portrait of a lady whom in my youth I loved very greatly. Save to me, it is valueless. I pray you, do not rob me of it."

===Chinese===
"骁骑爵士，这挂坠盒里装的是一位我年轻时深爱的女子的画像。它对我之外的人毫无价值。恳求您，不要夺走它。"

===Original===
But the trooper shook his head with drunken solemnity. "I do not like the looks of this. Yet I will sell it to you, as the saying is, for a song."

===Chinese===
但那骑兵带着醉意的庄重摇了摇头。"我看这玩意儿不顺眼。不过我可以把它卖给你，俗话说得好，换一首歌。"

===Original===
"It shall be the king of songs," said Osmund——"the song that Arnaut Daniel first made. I will sing for you a Sestina, messieurs——a Sestina in salutation of Spring."

===Chinese===
"那将是歌中之王，"奥斯蒙德说——"阿尔诺·达尼埃尔（Arnaut Daniel）首创的那首歌。我为诸位唱一首塞斯蒂纳，先生们——一首致敬春天的塞斯蒂纳。"

===Original===
The men disposed themselves about the dying grass, and presently he sang.

===Chinese===
士兵们在枯草上随意散坐，不久他便唱了起来。

===Original===
Sang Messire Heleigh:

===Chinese===
黑利骁骑爵士唱道：

===Original===
"Awaken! for the servitors of Spring

						Proclaim his triumph! ah, make haste to see

						With what tempestuous pageantry they bring

						The victor homeward! haste, for this is he

						That cast out Winter and all woes that cling

						To Winter's garments, and bade April be!

===Chinese===
"醒来吧！春天的侍从们宣告他的凯旋！啊，快来观看他们以何等狂风暴雨般的盛大游行将胜利者迎回！快，因为他正是那驱逐了冬天以及一切附着于冬天衣袍上的忧愁、命四月降临的主宰！

===Original===
"And now that Spring is master, let us be

						Content, and laugh, as anciently in spring

						The battle-wearied Tristan laughed, when he

						Was come again Tintagel-ward, to bring

						Glad news of Arthur's victory——and see

						Ysoude, with parted lips, that waver and cling.

===Chinese===
"如今春天已为君主，让我们心满意足地欢笑吧，如同往昔春天里饱经战阵的特里斯坦（Tristan）那样欢笑——他重返丁塔格尔（Tintagel），带回亚瑟王（Arthur）大捷的喜讯——看哪，伊索尔德（Ysoude）双唇微启，犹疑而依恋。

===Original===
"Not yet in Brittany must Tristan cling

						To this or that sad memory, and be

						Alone, as she in Cornwall; for in spring

						Love sows against far harvestings——and he

						Is blind, and scatters baleful seed that bring

						Such fruitage as blind Love lacks eyes to see!"

===Chinese===
"特里斯坦在布列塔尼（Brittany）尚不可沉溺于这样或那样的悲伤记忆中，如她在康沃尔（Cornwall）那般孤寂；因为在春天，爱播下的是与遥远收获相悖的种子——而他是盲目的，撒下有害的种子，结出的果实连盲目的爱也无眼去看见！"

===Original===
Osmund paused here for an appreciable interval, staring at the Queen. You saw his flabby throat a-quiver, his eyes melting, saw his cheeks kindle, and youth seeping into the lean man like water over a crumbling dam. His voice was now big and desirous.

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德在这里停顿了好一会儿，凝视着王后。你看见他松弛的喉咙在颤抖，他的双目渐渐柔化，面颊泛起红晕，青春如水漫过坍塌的堤坝，重新渗入这个消瘦之人的体内。他的声音此刻洪亮而充满渴望。

===Original===
Sang Messire Heleigh:

===Chinese===
黑利骁骑爵士唱道：

===Original===
"Love sows, but lovers reap; and ye will see

						The loved eyes lighten, feel the loved lips cling,

						Never again when in the grave ye be

						Incurious of your happiness in spring,

						And get no grace of Love there, whither he

						That bartered life for love no love may bring.

===Chinese===
"爱播种，恋人收获；而你们将看见心爱之人的明眸闪耀，感受心爱之人的双唇依恋——一旦入了坟墓，你们便再也不能对春天的幸福无动于衷，在那里也得不到爱的恩泽——因为以生命换取爱情的人，到了爱无法带到之处，也得不到爱。

===Original===
"No braggart Heracles avails to bring

						Alcestis hence; nor here may Roland see

						The eyes of Aude; nor here the wakening spring

						Vex any man with memories: for there be

						No memories that cling as cerements cling,

						No force that baffles Death, more strong than he.

===Chinese===
"纵然有赫拉克勒斯（Heracles）这等英雄也无法将阿尔刻提斯（Alcestis）从此处带回；罗兰（Roland）在此也望不见奥德（Aude）的眼眸；苏醒的春天在此也无法以记忆搅扰任何人：因为没有哪段记忆像裹尸布那样缠人不放，没有哪种力量能挫败死神——他比一切力量都强大。

===Original===
"Us hath he noted, and for us hath he

						An hour appointed; and that hour will bring

						Oblivion.——Then, laugh! Laugh, dear, and see

						The tyrant mocked, while yet our bosoms cling,

						While yet our lips obey us, and we be

						Untrammeled in our little hour of spring!

===Chinese===
"他已注视了我们，为我们定下了一个时辰；那时辰将带来遗忘。——那么，笑吧！笑吧，亲爱的，嘲笑这暴君吧，趁我们的胸膛尚在跳动，趁我们的双唇听从使唤，趁我们在短暂的春日时光里自由自在！

===Original===
"Thus in the spring we jeer at Death, though he

						Will see our children perish and will bring

						Asunder all that cling while love may be."

===Chinese===
"就这样我们在春天里嘲弄死神，尽管他将目睹我们的子女消亡，将拆散一切相爱之人尚能相守的情缘。"

===Original===
Then Osmund put the viol aside and sat quite silent. The soldiery judged, and with cordial frankness stated, that the difficulty of his rhyming scheme did not atone for his lack of indecency, but when the Queen of England went among them with Messire Heleigh's faded green hat she found them liberal. Even the fellow with the broken head admitted that a bargain was proverbially a bargain, and returned the locket with the addition of a coin. So for the present these two went safe, and quitted the Cat and Hautbois fed and unmolested.

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德把手摇琴放到一旁，静静地坐着。士兵们品评了一番，然后坦率地表示，他那艰深的押韵方案并不足以弥补他歌中缺乏淫秽之辞的缺憾，但当英格兰王后拿着黑利骁骑爵士那顶褪色的绿帽子走到他们中间时，他们倒很大方。连那个破了脑袋的家伙也承认买卖就是买卖（a bargain is proverbially a bargain），归还了挂坠盒，还外加了一枚硬币。于是二人暂时安然无恙，吃饱了从"猫与号角"客栈离开，没有受到骚扰。

===Original===
"My Osmund," Dame Alianora said, presently, "your memory is better than I had thought."

===Chinese===
"我的奥斯蒙德，"艾丽亚诺拉夫人过了一会儿说，"您的记性比我以为的要好。"

===Original===
"I remembered a boy and a girl," he returned. "And I grieved that they were dead."

===Chinese===
"我记得一个男孩和一个女孩，"他答道，"我为他们已经死了而悲伤。"

===Original===
Afterward they plodded on toward Bowater, and the ensuing night rested in Chantrell Wood. They had the good fortune there to encounter dry and windless weather and a sufficiency of brushwood, with which Osmund constructed an agreeable fire. In its glow these two sat, eating bread and cheese.

===Chinese===
此后他们步履沉重地向博沃特（Bowater）进发，次夜在尚特雷尔林（Chantrell Wood）中歇息。他们很走运，遇到了干燥无风的天气，还有充足的灌木柴，奥斯蒙德用这些生起了一堆令人惬意的篝火。两人坐在火光中，吃着面包和奶酪。

===Original===
But talk languished at the outset. The Queen had complained of an ague, and Messire Heleigh was sedately suggesting three spiders hung about the neck as an infallible corrective for this ailment, when Dame Alianora rose to her feet. "Eh, my God!" she said; "I am wearied of such ungracious aid! Not an inch of the way but you have been thinking of your filthy books and longing to be back at them! No; I except the moments when you were frightened into forgetfulness——first by Falmouth, then by the trooper. O Eternal Father! afraid of a single dirty soldier!"

===Chinese===
但谈话起初便无精打采。王后抱怨患了疟疾，黑利骁骑爵士正不动声色地建议将三只蜘蛛挂在脖子上作为治此症的万灵药方时，艾丽亚诺拉夫人霍然起身。"唉，我的上帝！"她说，"我厌倦了这种毫无情义的援助！一路上没有一寸不在想你那些肮脏的书，巴不得回去守着它们！不——除了你被吓得忘了一切的那几个瞬间——先是法尔茅斯，然后是那个骑兵。哦，天父啊！竟怕区区一个肮脏的士兵！"

===Original===
"Indeed, I was very much afraid," said Messire Heleigh, with perfect simplicity; "*timidus perire*, madame."

===Chinese===
"我确实非常害怕，"黑利骁骑爵士坦率地说，"*timidus perire*（怯者赴死），夫人。"

===Original===
"You have not even the grace to be ashamed! Yet I am shamed, messire, that Osmund Heleigh should have become the book-muddled pedant you are. For I loved young Osmund Heleigh."

===Chinese===
"您竟连羞愧都没有！可我倒替您感到羞耻，骁骑爵士，奥斯蒙德·黑利竟变成了您这样一个书呆子式的迂腐学究。因为我爱过年轻时的奥斯蒙德·黑利。"

===Original===
He also had risen in the firelight, and now its convulsive shadows marred two dogged faces. "I think it best not to recall that boy and girl who are so long dead. And, frankly, madame and Queen, the merit of the business I have in hand is questionable. It is you who have set all England by the ears, and I am guiding you toward opportunities for further mischief. I must serve you. Understand, madame, that ancient folly in Provence yonder has nothing to do with the affair. Count Manuel left you: and between his evasion and your marriage you were pleased to amuse yourself with me——"

===Chinese===
他也在火光中站了起来，摇曳的阴影投在两张执拗的脸上。"我认为最好不要去回忆那对已故去多年的男孩与女孩。坦率地说，夫人兼王后，我所做之事的正当性值得怀疑。是您搅得整个英格兰鸡犬不宁，而我却在引导您走向继续制造灾祸的机会。我必须为您效劳。请您明白，夫人，普罗旺斯那段久远的荒唐事与此事无关。曼努埃尔伯爵（Count Manuel）离开了您：在他出走与您再婚之间，您乐于拿我消遣——"

===Original===
"You were more civil then, my Osmund——"

===Chinese===
"那时候您还更有礼貌些，我的奥斯蒙德——"

===Original===
"I am not uncivil, I merely point out that this old folly constitutes no overwhelming obligation, either way. I cry *nihil ad Andromachen*! For the rest, I must serve you because you are a woman and helpless; yet I cannot forget that he who spares the wolf is the sheep's murderer. It would be better for all England if you were dead. Hey, your gorgeous follies, madame! Silver peacocks set with sapphires! Cloth of fine gold——"

===Chinese===
"我并非不礼貌，我只是指出这桩旧日的荒唐事并不构成任何一方压倒性的义务。我高呼*nihil ad Andromachen*（此事与安德洛玛刻无关）！至于其余，我必须为您效劳，因为您是女人，孤弱无依；然而我不能忘记，放过狼的人便是羊的谋杀者。您若死了，对全英格兰都好。哎，您那些华丽的荒唐事啊，夫人！镶着蓝宝石的银孔雀！金线织锦——"

===Original===
"Would you have me go unclothed?" Dame Alianora demanded, pettishly.

===Chinese===
"难道您要我赤身裸体不成？"艾丽亚诺拉夫人嗔怒地反问。

===Original===
"Not so," Osmund retorted; "again I say to you with Tertullian, 'Let women paint their eyes with the tints of chastity, insert into their ears the Word of God, tie the yoke of Christ about their necks, and adorn their whole person with the silk of sanctity and the damask of devotion.' I say to you that the boy you wish to rescue from Wallingford, and make King of England, is freely rumored to be not verily the son of Sire Henry but the child of tall Manuel of Poictesme。I say to you that from the first you have made mischief in England. And I say to you——"

===Chinese===
"并非如此，"奥斯蒙德反驳道，"我再次引用德尔图良（Tertullian）的话对您说：'让女人以贞洁的色彩涂画双眼，在耳中放入上帝的话语，在颈上套上基督的轭，以圣洁的丝绸和虔敬的锦缎装饰全身。'我对您说，您想从沃灵福德救出来并拥立为英格兰国王的那个孩子，坊间纷纷传言并非亨利大人（Sire Henry）的亲生骨肉，而是波伊克泰姆（Poictesme）高大的曼努埃尔（Manuel）之子。我对您说，您从一开始就在英格兰制造祸端。我还要对您说——"

===Original===
But Dame Alianora was yawning quite frankly. "You will say to me that I brought foreigners into England, that I misguided the King, that I stirred up strife between the King and his barons. Eh, my God! I am sufficiently familiar with the harangue. Yet listen, my Osmund: They sold me like a bullock to a man I had never seen. I found him a man of wax, and I remoulded him. They asked of me an heir for England: I provided that heir. They gave me England as a toy; I played with it. I was the Queen, the source of honor, the source of wealth——the trough, in effect, about which swine gathered. Never since I came into England, Osmund, has any man or woman loved me; never in all my English life have I loved man or woman. Do you understand, my Osmund?——the Queen has many flatterers, but no friends. Not a friend in the world, my Osmund! And so the Queen made the best of it and amused herself."

===Chinese===
但艾丽亚诺拉夫人毫不掩饰地打了个哈欠。"您要对我说，我把外国人带进了英格兰，误导了国王，挑拨了国王与他的男爵之间的争端。唉，我的上帝！这番训诫我早已听腻了。但请听我说，我的奥斯蒙德：他们像卖牛犊一样把我卖给一个素未谋面的男人。我发现他不过是个蜡做的人，便将他重新塑造。他们要我为英格兰诞下一位继承人：我给了他们那个继承人。他们把英格兰当玩具给了我；我便玩了起来。我是王后，是荣耀之源、财富之源——说到底，不过是猪群围绕的食槽。自从我来到英格兰，奥斯蒙德，没有人爱过我；在我整个英格兰的日子里，我也没有爱过任何人。你明白吗，我的奥斯蒙德？——王后有很多谄媚者，但没有朋友。这世上一个朋友都没有，我的奥斯蒙德！所以王后便随遇而安，聊以自娱。"

===Original===
Somewhat he seemed to understand, for he answered without asperity:

===Chinese===
他似乎有些理解了，因为他回答时并无尖刻：

===Original===
"*Mon bel esper*, I do not find it anywhere in Holy Writ that God requires it of us to amuse ourselves; but upon many occasions we have been commanded to live righteously. We are tempted in divers and insidious ways. And we cry with the Psalmist, 'My strength is dried up like a potsherd.' But God intends this, since, until we have here demonstrated our valor upon Satan, we are manifestly unworthy to be enregistered in God's army. The great Captain must be served by proven soldiers. We may be tempted, but we may not yield. O daughter of the South! we must not yield!"

===Chinese===
"*Mon bel esper*，我在《圣经》各处都找不到上帝要求我们以自娱为务；但多处经文明白地命令我们走正道。我们以各种狡诈的方式受到试探。我们与《诗篇》作者一同呼喊：'我的精力枯干，如同瓦片。'但上帝自有其意旨，因为，除非我们在此地上演了对抗撒旦的英勇事迹，我们便显然不配被录入上帝的军队。伟大的统帅需要经过考验的士兵。我们可以受试探，但不可屈服。南方的女儿啊！我们不可屈服！"

===Original===
"Again you preach," Dame Alianora said. "That is a venerable truism."

===Chinese===
"您又在布道了，"艾丽亚诺拉夫人说。"这不过是古老的陈词滥调。"

===Original===
"Ho, madame," he returned, "is it on that account the less true?"

===Chinese===
"呵，夫人，"他答道，"难道因此它就不那么真实了吗？"

===Original===
Pensively the Queen considered this. "You are a good man, my Osmund," she said, at last, "though you are very droll. Ohimé! it is a pity that I was born a princess! Had it been possible for me to be your wife, I would have been a better woman. I shall sleep now and dream of that good and stupid and contented woman I might have been." So presently these two slept in Chantrell Wood.

===Chinese===
王后沉思了片刻。"你是个好人，我的奥斯蒙德，"她终于说道，"尽管你非常滑稽。唉！可惜我生来是公主！若我有可能成为你的妻子，我本可以做个更好的女人。我现在要睡了，去梦见那个我本可以成为的善良、愚笨而知足的女人。"于是不久，二人在尚特雷尔林中入睡了。

===Original===
Followed four days of journeying. As Messer Dante had not yet surveyed Malebolge, Osmund Heleigh and Dame Alianora lacked a parallel for that which they encountered; their traverse discovered England razed, charred, and depopulate——picked bones of an island, a vast and absolute ruin about which passion-wasted men skulked like rats. Messire Heleigh and the Queen traveled without molestation; malice and death had journeyed before them on this road, and had swept it clear.

===Chinese===
此后又走了四天。但丁（Dante）先生尚未勘察过马勒博尔杰（Malebolge），奥斯蒙德·黑利和艾丽亚诺拉夫人找不到与他们所见相仿的景象；他们所经过之处，英格兰已被夷为焦土、人口凋零——一座岛屿被剔尽的骨骸，一片辽阔而彻底的废墟，被战火摧残的男人们像老鼠一样在其中窜行。黑利骁骑爵士与王后一路未受侵扰；恶意与死亡已先他们一步走过这条路，把一切清扫干净。

===Original===
At every trace of these hideous precessors Osmund Heleigh would say, "By a day's ride I might have prevented this." Or, "By a day's ride I might have saved this woman." Or, "By two days' riding I might have fed this child."

===Chinese===
每看到这些可怕的先行者留下的痕迹，奥斯蒙德·黑利便说："若早来一天，我或许能阻止这一切。"或者说："若早来一天，我或许能救下这个女人。"又说："若早来两天，我或许能喂饱这个孩子。"

===Original===
The Queen kept Spartan silence, but daily you saw the fine woman age. In their slow advance every inch of misery was thrust before her for inspection; meticulously she observed and evaluated her handiwork. Enthroned, she had appraised from a distance the righteous wars she set afoot; trudging thus among the debris of these wars, she found they had unsuspected aspects. Bastling the royal army had recently sacked. There remained of this village the skeletons of two houses, and for the rest a jumble of bricks, rafters half-burned, many calcined fragments of humanity, and ashes. At Bastling, Messire Heleigh turned to the Queen toiling behind.

===Chinese===
王后保持着斯巴达人般的沉默，但你每天都看到这杰出的妇人在衰老。在缓慢的行进中，每一寸苦难都被推到她眼前供她审视；她一丝不苟地观察并评估自己的杰作。端坐王座时，她曾从远处审视自己发动的那些正义之战；如今跋涉在这些战争的废墟之间，她发现它们有着出人意料的面目。巴斯特灵（Bastling）刚刚被王军洗劫过。这个村庄只剩下两座房屋的骨架，其余不过是一堆砖头、半烧焦的椽木、许多烧焦的人体碎片，以及灰烬。在巴斯特灵，黑利骁骑爵士回头望向在身后艰难行走的王后。

===Original===
"Oh, madame!" he said, in a dry whisper, "this was the home of so many men!"

===Chinese===
"噢，夫人！"他用干涩的低语说，"这里是多少人的家啊！"

===Original===
"I burned it," Dame Alianora replied. "That man we passed just now I killed. Those other men and women——my folly slew them all. And little children, my Osmund! The hair like flax, blood-dabbled!"

===Chinese===
"是我烧的，"艾丽亚诺拉夫人答道。"我们刚才路过的那个人是我杀的。那些男人和女人——是我的愚蠢杀了他们所有人。还有小孩子，我的奥斯蒙德！亚麻色的头发，沾满了血！"

===Original===
"Oh, madame!" he wailed, in the extremity of his pity.

===Chinese===
"噢，夫人！"他悲号道，悲悯到了极处。

===Original===
For she stood with eyes shut, all gray. The Queen demanded: "Why have they not slain me? Was there no man in England to strangle the proud wanton? Are you all cowards here?"

===Chinese===
她闭目而立，满脸灰败。王后质问道："他们为何不杀了我？难道英格兰就没有一个人敢扼住这个骄纵的荡妇的喉咙？你们都是些懦夫吗？"

===Original===
He said: "I detect only one coward in the affair. Your men and Leicester's men also ride about the world, and draw sword and slay and die for the right as they see it. And you and Leicester contend for the right as ye see it. But I, madame! I! I, who sat snug at home spilling ink and trimming rosebushes! God's world, madame, and I in it afraid to speak a word for Him! God's world, and a curmudgeon in it grudging God the life He gave!" The man flung out his soft hands and snarled: "*We are tempted in divers and insidious ways.* But I, who rebuked you! behold, now, with how gross a snare was I entrapped!"

===Chinese===
他说："在这桩事中，我只发现一个懦夫。您的人和莱斯特的人也都纵横天下，拔剑厮杀，为他们心目中的正义而死。您和莱斯特各为心目中的正义而争。但您看我，夫人！我！我窝在温暖的家中泼墨挥毫、修剪玫瑰丛！这是上帝的世界，夫人，而我在其中竟不敢为他说一句话！这是上帝的世界，而我在其中竟是一个吝啬上帝赐予之生命的吝啬鬼！"那人伸出他那双柔软的手，咆哮道："*我们以各种狡诈的方式受到试探。*而我——那个斥责过您的人！看看吧，现在我陷入了何等粗陋的陷阱！"

===Original===
"I do not understand, my Osmund."

===Chinese===
"我不明白，我的奥斯蒙德。"

===Original===
"I was afraid, madame," he returned, dully. "Everywhere men fight, and I am afraid to die."

===Chinese===
"我害怕了，夫人，"他沉闷地答道。"四处都有人在战斗，而我害怕死。"

===Original===
So they stood silent in the ruins of Bastling.

===Chinese===
于是他们在巴斯特灵的废墟中默默伫立。

===Original===
"Of a piece with our lives," Dame Alianora said at last. "All ruin, my Osmund."

===Chinese===
"和我们的命运一模一样，"艾丽亚诺拉夫人终于开口。"全是废墟，我的奥斯蒙德。"

===Original===
But Messire Heleigh threw back his head and laughed, new color in his face. "Presently men will build here, my Queen. Presently, as in legend was reborn the Arabian bird, arises from these ashes a lordlier and more spacious town."

===Chinese===
但黑利骁骑爵士仰头大笑，脸上泛起新的血色。"不久人们就会在这里重建，我的王后。不久，正如传说中不死鸟（Arabian bird）浴火重生，从这些灰烬中将升起一座更宏伟、更广阔的城镇。"

===Original===
They went forward. The next day chance loosed upon them Gui Camoys, lord of Bozon, Foliot, and Thwenge, who, riding alone through Poges Copse, found there a man and a woman over their limited supper. The woman had thrown back her hood, and Camoys drew rein to stare at her. Lispingly he spoke the true court dialect.

===Chinese===
他们继续前行。第二天，命运将居伊·卡穆瓦（Gui Camoys）——博宗（Bozon）、福利奥（Foliot）与斯温吉（Thwenge）的领主——引到了他们面前。他独骑穿过波日树林（Poges Copse）时，发现一男一女正在那里吃着简陋的晚餐。女子掀开了兜帽，卡穆瓦勒住缰绳盯着她看。他用娇揉造作的宫廷腔调说话。

===Original===
"*Ma belle*," said this Camoys, in friendly condescension, "*n'estez vous pas jongleurs*?"

===Chinese===
"*Ma belle*（美人），"卡穆瓦以友好的俯就之态说道，"*n'estez vous pas jongleurs*（你们不正是行吟艺人吗）？"

===Original===
Dame Alianora smiled up at him. "*Ouais, messire; mon mary faict les chançons*——" She paused, with dilatory caution, for Camoys had leaped from his horse, giving a great laugh.

===Chinese===
艾丽亚诺拉夫人朝他嫣然一笑。"*Ouais, messire; mon mary faict les chançons*（是呀，骁骑爵士；我的丈夫谱写歌曲）——"她迟疑地停住了，因为卡穆瓦已跳下马来，放声大笑。

===Original===
"A prize! ho, an imperial prize!" Camoys shouted. "A peasant woman with the Queen's face, who speaks French! And who, madame, is this? Have you by any chance brought pious Lewis from oversea? Have I bagged a brace of monarchs?"

===Chinese===
"好一个猎物！嚯，一件帝王级的猎物！"卡穆瓦喊道。"一个有着王后面容的村妇，居然说法语！这位，夫人，是谁？您该不会碰巧从海外把虔诚的路易带来了吧？我逮到的可是一对君王？"

===Original===
Here was imminent danger, for Camoys had known the Queen some fifteen years. Messire Heleigh rose, his five days' beard glinting like hoarfrost as his mouth twitched.

===Chinese===
此时危险迫在眉睫，因为卡穆瓦认识王后已有大约十五年了。黑利骁骑爵士站起身来，五天未刮的胡茬如白霜般闪烁，嘴唇微微颤抖。

===Original===
"I am Osmund Heleigh, messire, younger brother to the Earl of Brudenel."

===Chinese===
"我是奥斯蒙德·黑利，骁骑爵士，布鲁德尔伯爵（Earl of Brudenel）的幼弟。"

===Original===
"I have heard of you, I believe——the fellow who spoils parchment. This is odd company, however, Messire Osmund, for Brudenel's brother."

===Chinese===
"我想我听说过你——那个糟蹋羊皮纸的家伙。不过，奥斯蒙德骁骑爵士，与布鲁德尔的弟弟做伴，倒是有些奇怪。"

===Original===
"A gentleman must serve his Queen, messire. As Cicero very justly observes——"

===Chinese===
"一位绅士理应为他的王后效劳，骁骑爵士。正如西塞罗（Cicero）极为公正地指出的那样——"

===Original===
"I am inclined to think that his political opinions are scarcely to our immediate purpose. This is a high matter, Messire Heleigh. To let the sorceress pass is, of course, out of the question; upon the other hand, I observe that you lack weapons of defence. Yet if you will have the kindness to assist me in unarming, your courtesy will place our commerce on more equal footing."

===Chinese===
"我倒觉得他的政治见解与我们眼前的事几乎无关。这可是件大事，黑利骁骑爵士。放走那个女巫自然绝无可能；但另一方面，我注意到您缺乏防身的武器。不过，如果您不介意帮我解除武装，您的礼貌将使我们的交易更加平等。"

===Original===
Osmund had turned very white. "I am no swordsman, messire——"

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德面色变得煞白。"我不是剑客，骁骑爵士——"

===Original===
"Now, this is not handsome of you," Camoys began. "I warn you that people will speak harshly of us if we lose this opportunity of gaining honor. And besides, the woman will be burned at the stake. Plainly, you owe it to all three of us to fight."

===Chinese===
"哎，这可不漂亮，"卡穆瓦说。"我警告你，如果我们错过了这个赢得荣誉的机会，人们会狠狠说我们的闲话。况且，这女人会被烧死在火刑柱上。很显然，为了我们三个人，你都该一战。"

===Original===
"—but I refer my cause to God. I am quite at your service."

===Chinese===
"——但我将此事交付上帝裁决。我听凭您的安排。"

===Original===
"No, my Osmund!" Dame Alianora then cried. "It means your death."

===Chinese===
"不，我的奥斯蒙德！"艾丽亚诺拉夫人喊道。"那意味着你会死。"

===Original===
He spread out his hands. "That is God's affair, madame."

===Chinese===
他摊开双手。"那是上帝的事，夫人。"

===Original===
"Are you not afraid?" she breathed.

===Chinese===
"你不怕吗？"她低声问。

===Original===
"Of course I am afraid," said Messire Heleigh, irritably.

===Chinese===
"我当然怕，"黑利骁骑爵士烦躁地说。

===Original===
After that he unarmed Camoys, and presently they faced each other in their tunics. So for the first time in the journey Osmund's long falchion saw daylight. He had thrown away his dagger, as Camoys had none.

===Chinese===
随后他解除了卡穆瓦的武装，不久二人穿着束腰外衣对峙。奥斯蒙德的长弯刀在旅途中第一次得见天日。他扔掉了自己的匕首，因为卡穆瓦身上没有。

===Original===
The combat was sufficiently curious. Camoys raised his left hand. "So help me God and His saints, I have upon me neither bone, stone, nor witchcraft wherethrough the power and the word of God might be diminished or the devil's power increased."

===Chinese===
这场搏斗颇为奇特。卡穆瓦举起左手。"上帝及其圣徒为我作证，我身上未携骨、石或巫术，以削弱上帝的大能与圣言，或增加魔鬼的力量。"

===Original===
Osmund made similar oath. "Judge Thou this woman's cause!" he cried, likewise.

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德立下类似的誓言。"主啊，请裁断这女子的案件！"他也同样喊道。

===Original===
Then Gui Camoys shouted, as a herald might have done, "*Laissez les aller, laissez les aller, laissez les aller, les bons combatants!*" and warily each moved toward the other.

===Chinese===
然后居伊·卡穆瓦像传令官那样高喊："*Laissez les aller, laissez les aller, laissez les aller, les bons combatants!*（放手一搏，放手一搏，放手一搏，勇士们！）"二人各自谨慎地向对方逼近。

===Original===
On a sudden Osmund attacked, desperately apprehensive of his own cowardice. Camoys lightly eluded him and slashed at Osmund's undefended thigh, drawing much blood. Osmund gasped. He flung away his sword, and in the instant catching Camoys under the arms, threw him to the ground. Messire Heleigh fell with his opponent, who in stumbling had lost his sword, and thus the two struggled unarmed, Osmund atop. But Camoys was the younger man, and Osmund's strength was ebbing rapidly by reason of his wound. Now Camoys' tethered horse, rearing with nervousness, tumbled his master's flat-topped helmet into the road. Osmund caught up this helmet and with it battered Camoys in the face, dealing severe blows.

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德突然发起进攻，因恐惧自己怯懦而拼尽全力。卡穆瓦轻巧地闪开，在奥斯蒙德毫无防备的大腿上砍了一刀，血流如注。奥斯蒙德倒抽一口气，扔掉佩剑，趁势搂住卡穆瓦双臂，将他摔在地上。黑利踉跄间跌倒，对手在绊倒时也丢了剑，于是二人徒手搏斗，奥斯蒙德骑在上面。但卡穆瓦更年轻，而奥斯蒙德因伤口失血，体力迅速衰竭。此时卡穆瓦那匹被拴着的战马因紧张而扬蹄，将主人的平顶头盔掀到了路上。奥斯蒙德抓起头盔朝卡穆瓦脸上猛砸，连击数下。

===Original===
"God!" Camoys cried, his face all blood.

===Chinese===
"上帝！"卡穆瓦喊道，满脸是血。

===Original===
"Do you acknowledge my quarrel just?" said Osmund, between horrid sobs.

===Chinese===
"你承认我的争端是正义的吗？"奥斯蒙德在可怕的啜泣中问道。

===Original===
"What choice have I?" said Gui Camoys, very sensibly.

===Chinese===
"我有什么选择？"居伊·卡穆瓦极为理智地说。

===Original===
So Osmund rose, blind with tears and shivering. The Queen bound up their wounds as best she might, but Camoys was much dissatisfied.

===Chinese===
于是奥斯蒙德站了起来，泪眼模糊，浑身发抖。王后尽力包扎了他们的伤口，但卡穆瓦大为不满。

===Original===
"For private purposes of His own, madame," he observed, "and doubtless for sufficient reasons, God has singularly favored your cause. I am neither a fool nor a pagan to question His decision, and you two may go your way unhampered. But I have had my head broken with my own helmet, and this I consider to be a proceeding very little conducive toward enhancing my reputation. Of your courtesy, messire, I must entreat another meeting."

===Chinese===
"出于祂自身的隐秘旨意，夫人，"他评论道，"无疑有充足的理由，上帝格外垂青您的事业。我不是傻瓜，也不是异教徒，不会质疑祂的裁决，你们二位可以不受阻拦地继续赶路。但我的脑袋被自己的头盔打破了，在我看来，这对提升我的声誉几乎毫无助益。恳请阁下，骁骑爵士，我必须请求再战一场。"

===Original===
Osmund shrank as if from a blow. Then, with a short laugh, he conceded that this was Camoys' right, and they fixed upon the following Saturday, with Poges Copse as the rendezvous.

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德像挨了一击似地退缩了一下。随后短促地笑了一声，承认这是卡穆瓦的权利，二人约定下周六在波日树林会合。

===Original===
"I would suggest that the combat be to the death," Gui Camoys said, "in consideration of the fact it was my own helmet. You must undoubtedly be aware, Messire Osmund, that such an affront is practically without any parallel."

===Chinese===
"考虑到打我的毕竟是我自己的头盔，"居伊·卡穆瓦说，"我建议决斗以一方死亡为终。奥斯蒙德骁骑爵士，您想必十分清楚，这样的羞辱几乎是史无前例的。"

===Original===
This, too, was agreed upon.

===Chinese===
这一点也被同意了。

===Original===
Then, after asking if they needed money, which was courteously declined, Gui Camoys rode away, and sang as he went. Osmund Heleigh remained motionless. He raised quivering hands to the sky.

===Chinese===
随后，在问过他们是否需要钱——被礼貌地谢绝了——之后，居伊·卡穆瓦策马而去，一路唱歌。奥斯蒙德·黑利一动不动。他将颤抖的双手举向天空。

===Original===
"Thou hast judged!" he cried. "Thou hast judged, O puissant Emperor of Heaven! Now pardon! Pardon us twain! Pardon for unjust stewards of Thy gifts! Thou hast loaned this woman dominion over England, with all instruments to aid Thy cause, and this trust she has abused. Thou hast loaned me life and manhood, agility and wit and strength, all instruments to aid Thy cause. Talents in a napkin, O God! Repentant we cry to Thee. Pardon for unjust stewards! Pardon for the ungirt loin, for the service shirked, for all good deeds undone! Pardon and grace, O King of kings！"

===Chinese===
"您已经裁决了！"他喊道。"您已经裁断了，哦，万能的天帝！现在请宽恕！宽恕我们两人！宽恕对您天赋的不忠管事！您将英格兰的统治权交托给这妇人，赐予一切助成您圣业的工具，她却辜负了这信任。您将生命与男子气概、敏捷与智慧与力量交托给我，一切助成您圣业的工具。埋没的天赋啊，上帝！我们悔罪，向您呼求。宽恕不忠的管家！宽恕懈怠的腰带、推却的侍奉、一切未行的善事！宽恕与恩典，万王之王啊！"

===Original===
Thus he prayed, while Gui Camoys sang, riding deeper into the tattered, yellowing forest. By an odd chance Camoys had lighted on that song made by Thibaut of Champagne, beginning *Signor, saciez, ki or ne s'en ira*, which denounces all halfhearted servitors of Heaven; and this he sang with a lilt gayer than his matter countenanced. Faintly there now came to Osmund and the Queen the sound of Camoys' singing, and they found it, in the circumstances, ominously apt.

===Chinese===
他就这样祈祷着，而居伊·卡穆瓦一路高歌，骑马深入那片凋敝枯黄的林中。碰巧卡穆瓦选中了香槟的蒂博（Thibaut of Champagne）所作的那首以*Signor, saciez, ki or ne s'en ira*（诸位须知，今日之事不会消逝）开头的歌，歌中斥责一切半心半意侍奉上天的人；他唱时的欢快曲调与歌词的沉重颇不相称。此时奥斯蒙德和王后隐约听到卡穆瓦的歌声，他们觉得在此情境下，这歌声不祥地恰如其分。

===Original===
Sang Camoys:

===Chinese===
卡穆瓦唱道：

===Original===
"Et vos, par qui je n'ci onques aïe,

						Descendez luit en infer le parfont."

===Chinese===
"而你们——我从未从你们那里得到过帮助的人啊——今夜便坠入地狱的最深处吧。"

===Original===
Dame Alianora shivered. But she was a capable woman, and so she said: "I may have made mistakes. But I am sure I never meant any harm, and I am sure, too, that God will be more sensible about it than are you poets."

===Chinese===
艾丽亚诺拉夫人打了个寒颤。但她是个果决的女人，于是说道："我也许犯了错。但我确信我从未有意造成伤害，我也确信，上帝对此事的判断会比你们这些诗人更明智。"

===Original===
They slept that night in Ousley Meadow, and the next afternoon came safely to Bristol. You may learn elsewhere with what rejoicing the royal army welcomed the Queen's arrival, how courage quickened at sight of the generous virago. In the ebullition Messire Heleigh was submerged, and Dame Alianora saw nothing more of him that day. Friday there were counsels, requisitions, orders signed, a memorial despatched to Pope Urban, chief of all a letter (this in the Queen's hand throughout) privily conveyed to the Lady Maude de Mortemer, who shortly afterward contrived Prince Edward's escape from her husband's gaolership. There was much sowing of a seed, in fine, that eventually flowered victory. There was, however, no sign of Osmund Heleigh, though by Dame Alianora's order he was sought.

===Chinese===
那天夜里他们在奥斯利草地（Ousley Meadow）露营，次日下午平安抵达布里斯托尔。你可以在别处读到王军以何等喜悦迎接王后的到来，看到那位慷慨的女中豪杰时士气如何高涨。在一片欢腾中，黑利骁骑爵士被淹没了，艾丽亚诺拉夫人那天再没见到他。星期五，议事、征调、签署命令、向教皇乌尔班（Pope Urban）递交请愿书，最重要的是——一封全程由王后亲笔的密信被秘密送达莫德·德·莫特梅尔夫人（Lady Maude de Mortemer）手中，后者不久便设法帮助爱德华王子从她丈夫的囚禁中逃脱。总之，播种已毕，最终将开出胜利之花。然而奥斯蒙德·黑利不见踪影，尽管艾丽亚诺拉夫人已下令搜寻。

===Original===
On Saturday at seven in the morning he came to her lodging, in complete armor. From the open helmet his wrinkled face, showing like a wizened nut in a shell, smiled upon her questionings.

===Chinese===
星期六早晨七时，他全身披甲来到她的住所。头盔敞开着，他那张皱纹密布的脸——像壳中一颗干瘪的坚果——朝她的追问微笑着。

===Original===
"I go to fight Gui Camoys, madame and Queen."

===Chinese===
"我去与居伊·卡穆瓦决斗，夫人兼王后。"

===Original===
Dame Alianora wrung her hands. "You go to your death."

===Chinese===
艾丽亚诺拉夫人绞着双手。"你这是去送死。"

===Original===
He answered: "That is true. Therefore I am come to bid you farewell."

===Chinese===
他答道："确实如此。因此我来向您辞行。"

===Original===
The Queen stared at him for a while; on a sudden she broke into a curious fit of deep but tearless sobbing, which bordered upon laughter, too.

===Chinese===
王后凝视了他许久；突然间，她发出一阵奇怪的、深沉而无泪的呜咽，那声音几乎像是在笑。

===Original===
"*Mon bel esper*," said Osmund Heleigh, gently, "what is there in all this worthy of your sorrow? The man will kill me; granted, for he is my junior by some fifteen years, and is in addition a skilled swordsman. I fail to see that this is lamentable. Back to Longaville I cannot go after recent happenings; there a rope's end awaits me. Here I must in any event shortly take to the sword, since a beleaguered army has very little need of ink-pots; and shortly I must be slain in some skirmish, dug under the ribs perhaps by a greasy fellow I have never seen. I prefer a clean death at a gentleman's hands."

===Chinese===
"*Mon bel esper*，"奥斯蒙德·黑利温柔地说，"这整件事有什么值得您悲伤的？那人会杀了我；的确如此，因为他比我年轻大约十五岁，而且还是一名老练的剑客。我看不出这有什么可悲的。出了最近这些事，我回不了朗阿维尔了；那里有一根绳索等着我。在这里我反正很快也要拿起剑了，因为一支被围困的军队几乎用不着墨水瓶；而且我很快就会在某场遭遇战中被杀死，说不定被一个我从未谋面的油腻家伙从肋下捅进去。我宁愿死在一个绅士的干净剑下。"

===Original===
"It is I who bring about your death!" she said. "You gave me gallant service, and I have requited you with death, and it is a great pity."

===Chinese===
"是我害了你的性命！"她说。"你为我提供了侠义的效劳，而我回报你以死亡，这是天大的遗憾。"

===Original===
"Indeed the debt is on the other side. The trivial services I rendered you were such as any gentleman must render a woman in distress. Naught else have I afforded you, madame, save very anciently a Sestina. Ho, a Sestina! And in return you have given me a Sestina of fairer make——a Sestina of days, six days of manly common living." His eyes were fervent.

===Chinese===
"事实上这笔债在另一方。我为您做的那些微不足道的事，不过是一位绅士对落难女子理应尽到的义务。除此之外，夫人，我给过您的只有一首久远的塞斯蒂纳。嗬，一首塞斯蒂纳！而您回赠了我一首更美的塞斯蒂纳——一首岁月的塞斯蒂纳，六天堂堂正正的日常生活。"他的双目炽热。

===Original===
She kissed him on either cheek. "Farewell, my champion!"

===Chinese===
她在他的双颊各亲了一下。"永别了，我的勇士！"

===Original===
"Ay, your champion. In the twilight of life old Osmund Heleigh rides forth to defend the quarrel of Alianora of Provence. Reign wisely, my Queen, so that hereafter men may not say I was slain in an evil cause. Do not, I pray you, shame my maiden venture at a man's work."

===Chinese===
"是的，您的勇士。在生命的暮色中，老奥斯蒙德·黑利策马出征，为普罗旺斯的艾丽亚诺拉而战。请睿智地统治吧，我的王后，使后人不能说我死于一场不义之举。恳请您，不要让我这第一次以男子之身行事的努力蒙羞。"

===Original===
"I will not shame you," the Queen proudly said; and then, with a change of voice: "O my Osmund! My Osmund, you have a folly that is divine, and I lack it."

===Chinese===
"我不会让你蒙羞，"王后骄傲地说；继而声音一变："噢，我的奥斯蒙德！我的奥斯蒙德，你有一种神圣的愚痴，而我缺少它。"

===Original===
He caught her by each wrist, and stood crushing both her hands to his lips, with fierce staring. "Wife of my King! wife of my King!" he babbled; and then put her from him, crying, "I have not failed you! Praise God, I have not failed you!"

===Chinese===
他抓住她的双手腕，将她的双手按在自己唇上，热切地凝视着。"我王的妻！我王的妻！"他喃喃地说；然后将她推开，喊道："我没有辜负您！赞美上帝，我没有辜负您！"

===Original===
From her window she saw him ride away, a rich flush of glitter and color. In new armor with a smart emblazoned surcoat the lean pedant sat conspicuously erect; and as he went he sang defiantly, taunting the weakness of his flesh.

===Chinese===
从窗口她看到他策马远去，一片闪耀的华彩。那消瘦的学究身着崭新的铠甲与绣饰华美的罩袍，坐得格外笔直；他一路高歌，以不屈的姿态嘲弄着肉体的软弱。

===Original===
Sang Osmund Heleigh:

===Chinese===
奥斯蒙德·黑利唱道：

===Original===
"Love sows, but lovers reap; and ye will see

						The loved eyes lighten, feel the loved lips cling

						Never again when in the grave ye be

						Incurious of your happiness in spring,

						And get no grace of Love, there, whither he

						That bartered life for love no love may bring."

===Chinese===
"爱播种，恋人收获；而你们将看见心爱之人的明眸闪耀，感受心爱之人的双唇依恋——一旦入了坟墓，你们便再也不能对春天的幸福无动于衷，在那里也得不到爱的恩泽——因为以生命换取爱情的人，到了爱无法带到之处，也得不到爱。"

===Original===
So he rode away and thus out of our history. But in the evening Gui Camoys came into Bristol under a flag of truce, and behind him heaved a litter wherein lay Osmund Heleigh's body.

===Chinese===
就这样他策马远去，从此退出了我们的故事。但到了傍晚，居伊·卡穆瓦打着休战旗帜进入布里斯托尔，他身后抬着一副担架，上面躺着奥斯蒙德·黑利的遗体。

===Original===
"For this man was frank and courteous," Camoys said to the Queen, "and in the matter of the reparation he owed me acted very handsomely. It is fitting that he should have honorable interment."

===Chinese===
"此人坦荡有礼，"卡穆瓦对王后说，"在偿还欠我之债这件事上，他做得极为漂亮。他理应获得体面的安葬。"

===Original===
"That he shall not lack," the Queen said, and gently unclasped from Osmund's wrinkled neck the thin gold chain, now locketless. "There was a portrait here," she said; "the portrait of a woman whom he loved in his youth, Messire Camoys. And all his life it lay above his heart."

===Chinese===
"这不会短缺的，"王后说着，轻轻从奥斯蒙德皱纹密布的脖颈上解下那条已无挂坠盒的细金链。"这里曾有一幅画像，"她说，"一位他年轻时深爱的女子的画像，卡穆瓦骁骑爵士。它一生都挂在他的心口。"

===Original===
Camoys answered stiffly: "I imagine this same locket to have been the object which Messire Heleigh flung into the river, shortly before we began our combat. I do not rob the dead, madame."

===Chinese===
卡穆瓦生硬地答道："我想那个挂坠盒便是黑利骁骑爵士在我们决斗前不久扔进河里的东西。我不抢死人的东西，夫人。"

===Original===
"Well," the Queen said, "he always did queer things, and so, I shall always wonder what sort of lady he picked out to love, but it is none of my affair."

===Chinese===
"好吧，"王后说，"他总是做些怪事，所以，我一直会好奇他选了什么样的女子去爱，但这不关我的事。"

===Original===
Afterward she set to work on requisitions in the King's name. But Osmund Heleigh she had interred at Ambresbury, commanding it to be written on his tomb that he died in the Queen's cause.

===Chinese===
此后她以国王的名义着手处理征调事宜。但她将奥斯蒙德·黑利安葬在安布里斯伯里（Ambresbury），命人在他的墓碑上刻下：他为王后之事业而死。

===Original===
How the same cause prospered (Nicolas concludes), how presently Dame Alianora reigned again in England and with what wisdom, and how in the end this great Queen died a nun at Ambresbury and all England wept therefor——this you may learn elsewhere. I have chosen to record six days of a long and eventful life; and (as Messire Heleigh might have done) I say modestly with him of old, *Majores majora sonent*. Nevertheless, I assert that many a forest was once a pocketful of acorns.

===Chinese===
这一事业后来如何兴旺（尼古拉斯写道），艾丽亚诺拉夫人如何不久后重掌英格兰并以何等智慧施政，以及这位伟大的王后最终如何在安布里斯伯里修道院以修女之身辞世、全英格兰为之哀恸——这些你可以在别处读到。我选择记录一段漫长而多事的人生中的六天；而且（正如黑利骁骑爵士或许会做的那样）我谦逊地引用古人之语：*Majores majora sonent*（让伟大的人物颂扬更伟大的事业）。尽管如此，我断言，每一片广袤的森林曾经都不过是满口袋的橡实。

## II. The Story of the Tenson / 第二章 争辩曲的故事

===Original===
"Plagues à Dieu ja la nueitz non falhis,
Ni’l mieus amicx lone de mi nos partis,
Ni la gayta jorn ni alba ne vis.
Oy Dieus! oy Dieus! de l’alba tan tost we!"

===Chinese===
“愿上帝保佑，黑夜不要过早消逝，
不要让最亲爱的朋友离我远去，
不要让我看到白昼或黎明。
啊上帝！啊上帝！黎明来得如此之快！”

===Original===
The second novel.﻿—Ellinor of Castile, being enamored of a handsome person, is in her flight from marital obligations assisted by her husband, and is in the end by him convinced of the rationality of all attendant circumstances.

===Chinese===
第二则故事。——卡斯蒂利亚的埃莉诺（Ellinor）爱上了英俊的人，她在逃避婚姻义务时得到了丈夫的帮助，最终被他说服，相信所有附带情形都是合理的。

===Original===
In the year of grace 1265 (Nicolas begins), about the festival of Saint Peter *ad Vincula*, the Prince de Gâtinais came to Burgos. Before this he had lodged for three months in the district of Ponthieu; and the object of his southern journey was to assure the tenth Alphonso, then ruling in Castile, that the latter’s sister Ellinor, now resident at Entréchat, was beyond any reasonable doubt the transcendent lady whose existence old romancers had anticipated, however cloudily, when they fabled in remote time concerning Queen Heleine of Sparta.

===Chinese===
公元1265年（尼古拉（Nicolas）纪年开始），大约在圣彼得锁链节（Saint Peter *ad Vincula*）前后，加蒂内亲王（Prince de Gâtinais）来到了布尔戈斯（Burgos）。此前他已在蓬蒂厄（Ponthieu）地区寄宿了三个月；他南下的目的是向当时统治卡斯蒂利亚的第十代阿方索（Alphonso）保证，后者现居昂特雷沙（Entréchat）的妹妹埃莉诺，毋庸置疑就是古代传奇作家在遥远年代编织斯巴达王后海伦（Heleine of Sparta）故事时，尽管朦胧却已预见的那位非凡女性。

===Original===
There was a postscript to this news. The world knew that the King of Leon and Castile desired to be King of Germany as well, and that at present a single vote in the Diet would decide between his claims and those of his competitor, Earl Richard of Cornwall. De Gâtinais chaffered fairly; he had a vote, Alphonso had a sister. So that, in effect﻿—ohé, in effect, he made no question that his Majesty understood!

===Chinese===
这则消息还有个附言。世人皆知莱昂（Leon）和卡斯蒂利亚的国王也想成为德意志（Germany）国王，而目前帝国议会（Diet）的一票就将在他和他的竞争者康沃尔伯爵理查（Earl Richard of Cornwall）之间做出裁决。德·加蒂内公平交易；他有一票，阿方索有一个妹妹。所以，实际上——哦，实际上，他毫不怀疑陛下会明白！

===Original===
The Astronomer twitched his beard and demanded if the fact that Ellinor had been a married woman these ten years past was not an obstacle to the plan which his fair cousin had proposed?

===Chinese===
天文学家（The Astronomer）捻着胡子问道，埃莉诺这十年来一直是个有夫之妇，这难道不是他那位美丽表亲所提计划的障碍吗？

===Original===
Here the Prince was accoutred cap-à-pie, and hauled out a paper. Dating from Viterbo, Clement, Bishop of Rome, servant to the servants of God, desirous of all health and apostolical blessing for his well-beloved son in Christ, stated that a compact between a boy of fifteen and a girl of ten was an affair of no particular moment; and that in consideration of the covenantors never having clapped eyes upon each other since the wedding-day﻿—even had not the precontract of marriage between the groom’s father and the bride’s mother rendered a consummation of the childish oath an obvious and a most heinous enormity﻿—why, that, in a sentence, and for all his coy verbosity, the new pontiff was perfectly amenable to reason.

===Chinese===
此时亲王全副武装，从怀中掏出一份文件。这份文件日期署自维泰博（Viterbo），由罗马主教克莱门特（Clement），上帝众仆之仆，向他在基督里亲爱的儿子致以一切健康和宗座祝福，并声明：一个十五岁男孩和一个十岁女孩之间的婚约并非什么特别重要的事；并且鉴于缔约双方自婚礼之日起就未曾谋面——即便没有新郎父亲与新娘母亲之间的婚前约定，使得履行这孩童誓约成为明显且极其严重的暴行——总之，一句话，尽管这位新教皇言辞闪烁，但他完全通情达理。

===Original===
So in a month it was settled. Alphonso would give his sister to de Gâtinais, and in exchange get the latter’s vote to make Alphonso King of Germany; and Gui Foulques of Sabionetta﻿—now Clement, fourth Pope to assume that name﻿—would annul the previous marriage, and in exchange get an armament to serve him against Manfred, the late and troublesome tyrant of Sicily and Apulia. The scheme promised to each one of them that which he in particular desired, and messengers were presently sent into Ponthieu.

===Chinese===
于是一个月内事情就定了下来。阿方索将把妹妹嫁给德·加蒂内，作为交换，德·加蒂内将投票支持阿方索成为德意志国王；而萨比奥内塔（Sabionetta）的居伊·富尔克（Gui Foulques）——如今是克莱门特，第四位采用此名的教皇——将宣布之前的婚姻无效，作为交换，他将获得一支军队用来对抗曼弗雷德（Manfred），那个已故的、制造麻烦的西西里（Sicily）和阿普利亚（Apulia）暴君。这个计划向每个人承诺了他们各自特别渴望的东西，信使很快就被派往蓬蒂厄。

===Original===
It is now time we put aside these Castilian matters and speak of other things. In England, Prince Edward had fought, and won, a shrewd battle at Evesham. People said, of course, that such behavior was less in the manner of his nominal father, King Henry, than reminiscent of Count Manuel of Poictesme, whose portraits certainly the Prince resembled to an embarrassing extent. Either way, the barons’ power was demolished, there would be no more internecine war; and spurred by the unaccustomed idleness, Prince Edward began to think of the foreign girl he had not seen since the day he wedded her. She would be a woman by this, and it was befitting that he claim his wife. He rode with Hawise Bulmer and her baby to Ambresbury, and at the gate of the nunnery they parted, with what agonies are immaterial to this history’s progression; the tale merely tells that, having thus decorously rid himself of his mistress, the Prince went into Lower Picardy alone, riding at adventure as he loved to do, and thus came to Entréchat, where his wife resided with her mother, the Countess Johane.

===Chinese===
现在是时候放下这些卡斯蒂利亚的事务，谈谈其他事情了。在英格兰（England），爱德华王子（Prince Edward）在伊夫舍姆（Evesham）打了一场漂亮仗并取得了胜利。人们当然说，这种行为与其名义上的父亲亨利国王（King Henry）的风格不太相符，反而让人想起普瓦图的曼努埃尔伯爵（Count Manuel of Poictesme），王子的肖像确实与他相似到令人尴尬的程度。无论如何，男爵们的权力被摧毁了，不会再有内战；在不习惯的闲暇刺激下，爱德华王子开始想起那个自婚礼之日起就未曾谋面的外国姑娘。她现在该是个成年女子了，他理应去认领他的妻子。他带着哈维斯·布尔默（Hawise Bulmer）和她的婴儿骑马前往安布里斯伯里（Ambresbury），在女修道院门口他们分别了，其中的痛苦与本历史的进展无关；故事只是说，王子就这样得体地摆脱了他的情妇，独自一人前往下皮卡第（Lower Picardy），像他喜欢的那样冒险骑行，于是来到了昂特雷沙，他的妻子和她的母亲约翰娜伯爵夫人（Countess Johane）居住在那里。

===Original===
In a wood near the castle he approached a company of Spaniards, four in number, their horses tethered while these men (Oviedans, as they told him) drank about a great stone which served them for a table. Being thirsty, he asked and was readily accorded hospitality, and these five fell into amicable discourse. One fellow asked his name and business in those parts, and the Prince gave each without hesitancy as he reached for the bottle, and afterward dropped it just in time to catch, cannily, with his naked left hand, the knife-blade with which the rascal had dug at the unguarded ribs. The Prince was astounded, but he was never a subtle man: here were four knaves who, for reasons unexplained﻿—but to them of undoubted cogency﻿—desired his death: manifestly there was here an actionable difference of opinion; so he had his sword out and killed the four of them.

===Chinese===
在城堡附近的一片树林里，他遇到了四个西班牙人，他们的马拴在一旁，这几个人（他们自称是奥维耶多人（Oviedans））正围着一块充当桌子的大石头喝酒。他口渴了，请求分享，对方欣然同意，于是五人友好地交谈起来。其中一人问他的名字和在此地的事务，王子毫不犹豫地一一回答，同时伸手去拿酒瓶，然后及时丢开酒瓶，用赤裸的左手机敏地抓住了那个无赖刺向他毫无防备肋骨的刀刃。王子大吃一惊，但他从来不是个心思缜密的人：这里显然有四个恶棍，出于未解释的原因——但对他们来说无疑具有充分的理由——想要他的命：显然这里存在着可以起诉的意见分歧；于是他拔出剑，把他们四个都杀了。

===Original===
Presently came to him an apple-cheeked boy, habited as a page, who, riding jauntily through the forest, lighted upon the Prince, now in bottomless vexation. The lad drew rein, and his lips outlined a whistle. At his feet were several dead men in various conditions of dismemberment. And seated among them, as if throned upon this boulder, was a gigantic and florid person, so tall that the heads of few men reached to his shoulder; a person of handsome exterior, high-featured and blond, having a narrow, small head, and vivid light blue eyes, and the chest of a stallion; a person whose left eyebrow had an odd oblique droop, so that the stupendous man appeared to be winking the information that he was in jest.

===Chinese===
不久，一个苹果脸蛋的男孩朝他走来，打扮得像个侍童，他正轻快地骑马穿过森林，遇见了这位此刻陷入无尽烦恼的王子。小伙子勒住马，嘴唇做出吹口哨的形状。他脚下是几个肢体残缺不全的死人。而坐在这群人中间，仿佛端坐在这块巨石王座上的，是一个魁梧而红润的人，如此高大以至于很少有人的头能到他的肩膀；一个外表英俊的人，五官轮廓分明，金发，头小而窄，有着生动的浅蓝色眼睛，和种马般的胸膛；一个左眉毛有奇怪斜垂的人，以至于这个巨汉仿佛在眨眼暗示他是在开玩笑。

===Original===
"Fair friend," said the page. "God give you joy! and why have you converted this forest into a shambles?"

===Chinese===
“亲爱的朋友，”侍童说。“愿上帝赐予你欢乐！你为什么把这片森林变成了屠宰场？”

===Original===
The Prince told him as much of the half-hour’s action as has been narrated. "I have perhaps been rather hasty," he considered, by way of peroration, "and it vexes me that I did not spare, say, one of these lank Spaniards, if only long enough to ascertain why, in the name of Termagaunt, they should have desired my destruction."

===Chinese===
王子向他讲述了这半小时战斗的大致经过，正如上面所述。“我也许有点太性急了，”他总结道，“让我烦恼的是我没有留下，比如说，一个瘦长的西班牙人，哪怕只是为了弄清楚，以塔玛加恩特（Termagaunt）的名义，他们为何想要我的命。”

===Original===
But midway in his tale the boy had dismounted with a gasp, and he was now inspecting the features of one carcass. "Felons, my Prince! You have slain some eight yards of felony which might have cheated the gallows had they got the Princess Ellinor safe to Burgos. Only two days ago this chalk-eyed fellow conveyed to her a letter."

===Chinese===
但故事讲到一半时，那男孩就喘着气下了马，此刻正检查着一具尸体的面容。“重罪犯，我的王子！您杀死了大约八码长的重罪犯，他们若是能把埃莉诺公主安全带到布尔戈斯，就可能逃脱绞刑。仅仅两天前，这个白眼珠的家伙还给她送了一封信。”

===Original===
Prince Edward said, "You appear, lad, to be somewhat overheels in the confidence of my wife."

===Chinese===
爱德华王子说：“小伙子，看来你对我妻子的信任有些过分了。”

===Original===
Now the boy arose and defiantly flung back his head in shrill laughter. "Your wife! Oh, God have mercy! Your wife, and for ten years left to her own devices! Why, look you, today you and your wife would not know each other were you two brought face to face."

===Chinese===
这时男孩站起来，挑衅地仰头发出尖利的笑声。“您的妻子！哦，上帝怜悯吧！您的妻子，十年来一直任她自生自灭！听着，今天您和您妻子即使面对面也认不出彼此。”

===Original===
Prince Edward said, "That is very near the truth." But, indeed, it was the absolute truth, and as it concerned him was already attested.

===Chinese===
爱德华王子说：“这非常接近事实。”但，事实上，这是绝对的真相，并且就他而言，这已经是明证。

===Original===
"Sire Edward," the boy then said, "your wife has wearied of this long waiting till you chose to whistle for her. Last summer the young Prince de Gâtinais came a-wooing﻿—and he is a handsome man." The page made known all which de Gâtinais and King Alphonso planned, the words jostling as they came in torrents, but so that one might understand. "I am her page, my lord. I was to follow her. These fellows were to be my escort, were to ward off possible pursuit. Cry haro, beau sire! Cry haro, and shout it lustily, for your wife in company with six other knaves is at large between here and Burgos﻿—that unreasonable wife who grew dissatisfied after a mere ten years of neglect."

===Chinese===
“爱德华陛下，”男孩接着说，“您的妻子已经厌倦了漫长的等待，等着您选择召唤她。去年夏天，年轻的加蒂内亲王来求婚——他是个英俊的人。”侍童将德·加蒂内和阿方索国王的所有计划和盘托出，话语如洪水般涌来，但还能让人听明白。“我是她的侍童，大人。我本该跟随她。这些家伙本该做我的护卫，阻挡可能的追捕。快喊吧，漂亮的老爷！大声喊吧，因为您的妻子正和另外六个恶棍一起，在从这里到布尔戈斯之间的某个地方逍遥法外——那个仅仅被忽视了十年就变得不讲理的妻子。”

===Original===
"I have been remiss," the Prince said, and one huge hand strained at his chin; "yes, perhaps I have been remiss. Yet it had appeared to me﻿—But as it is, I bid you mount, my lad!"

===Chinese===
“我疏忽了，”王子说，一只大手紧握着下巴；“是的，也许我疏忽了。然而在我看来——但事已至此，我命令你上马，小伙子！”

===Original===
The boy demanded, "And to what end?"

===Chinese===
男孩问道：“为了什么目的？”

===Original===
"Oy Dieus, messire! have I not slain your escort? Why, in common reason, equity demands that I afford you my protection so far as Burgos, messire, just as plainly as equity demands I slay de Gâtinais and fetch back my wife to England."

===Chinese===
“哦天哪，阁下！难道我不是杀了您的护卫吗？听着，按照常理，公平要求我保护您直到布尔戈斯，阁下，正如公平要求我杀掉德·加蒂内并把我的妻子带回英格兰一样明确。”

===Original===
The page wrung exquisite hands with a gesture which was but partially tinged with anguish, and presently began to laugh. Afterward these two rode southerly, in the direction of Castile.

===Chinese===
侍童拧着精致的手，手势中只有部分带着痛苦，随后开始大笑。之后两人向南骑行，朝着卡斯蒂利亚的方向。

===Original===
For it appeared to the intriguing little woman a diverting jest that in this fashion her husband should be the promoter of her evasion. It appeared to her more diverting when in two days’ space she had become fond of him. She found him rather slow of comprehension, and she was humiliated by the discovery that not an eyelash of the man was irritated by his wife’s decampment; he considered, to all appearances, that some property of his had been stolen, and he intended, quite without passion, to repossess himself of it, after, of course, punishing the thief.

===Chinese===
因为对这个诡计多端的小妇人来说，她的丈夫竟以这种方式成为她逃亡的推动者，似乎是个有趣的玩笑。而更有趣的是，两天之内她就喜欢上了他。她觉得他理解力相当迟钝，而让她感到屈辱的是，这个男人对他妻子的私奔竟没有丝毫不快；表面上看，他认为自己的某些财产被偷走了，他打算毫无激情地将其夺回，当然，在惩罚窃贼之后。

===Original===
This troubled the Princess somewhat; and often, riding by her stolid husband’s side, the girl’s heart raged at memory of the decade so newly overpast which had kept her always dependent on the charity of this or that ungracious patron﻿—on anyone who would take charge of her while the truant丈夫 fought out his endless squabbles in England. Slights enough she had borne during the period, and squalor, and physical hunger also she had known, who was the child of a king and a saint.2 But now she rode toward the dear southland; and presently she would be rid of this big man, when he had served her purpose; and afterward she meant to wheedle Alphonso, just as she had always wheedled him, and later still, she and Etienne would be very happy: in fine, tomorrow was to be a new day.

===Chinese===
这让公主有些烦恼；常常，骑在她那冷漠丈夫身旁，女孩的心因回忆起刚刚过去的十年而愤怒，这十年让她总是依赖这个或那个不友善的庇护者的施舍——依赖任何愿意在她那逃亡的丈夫在英格兰打着他那无休止的争吵时照顾她的人。在那段时期她受够了轻蔑，也经历过肮脏和肉体饥饿，她本是一个国王和圣徒的孩子。但现在她正骑马前往可爱的南方；不久当这个大个子男人为她达到目的后，她就将摆脱他；之后她打算像以往那样哄骗阿方索，再后来，她和艾蒂安（Etienne）会非常幸福：总之，明天将是新的一天。

===Original===
So these two rode southward, and always Prince Edward found this new page of his﻿—this Miguel de Rueda﻿—a jolly lad, who whistled and sang inapposite snatches of balladry, without any formal ending or beginning, descanting always with the delicate irrelevancy of a bird-trill.

===Chinese===
于是两人继续向南骑行，爱德华王子总觉得他这个新侍童——米格尔·德·鲁埃达（Miguel de Rueda）——是个快活的小伙子，他吹着口哨，唱着不合时宜的民谣片段，没有正式的开头或结尾，总是像鸟儿的啭鸣一样细腻地离题吟唱。

===Original===
Sang Miguel de Rueda:

"Man’s Love, that leads me day by day
Through many a screened and scented way,
Finds to assuage my thirst.
No love that may the old love slay,
None sweeter than the first.

"Fond heart of mine, that beats so fast
As this or that fair maid trips past,
Once, and with lesser stir
We viewed the grace of love, at last,
And turned idolater.

"Lad’s Love it was, that in the spring
When all things woke to blossoming
Was as a child that came
Laughing, and filled with wondering,
Nor knowing his own name﻿—"

===Chinese===
米格尔·德·鲁埃达唱道：

“人间的爱，日复一日引领我
穿过许多掩映着芬芳的路径，
它找到了解我饥渴的泉源。
没有爱能扼杀旧日的爱，
没有比初恋更甜蜜的爱。

“我那颗痴心，跳得如此之快，
当这位或那位美丽的少女走过，
曾经，以更轻微的悸动
我们最终目睹了爱的优雅，
并转而成为偶像崇拜者。

“那是少年之爱，在春天，
当万物苏醒，繁花盛开，
它如同一个孩子走来，
笑着，充满了惊奇，
却不知自己的名字——”

===Original===
"And still I would prefer to think," the big man interrupted, heavily, "that Sicily is not the only allure. I would prefer to think my wife so beautiful.﻿—And yet, as I remember her, she was nothing extraordinary."

===Chinese===
“我仍然宁愿认为，”大个子男人沉重地打断道，“西西里不是唯一的诱惑。我宁愿认为我的妻子如此美丽。——然而，我记得她时，她并无特别之处。”

===Original===
The page a little tartly said that people might forget a deal within a decade.

===Chinese===
侍童有点尖刻地说，人们在十年内可能会忘记很多事。

===Original===
The Prince continued his unriddling of the scheme hatched in Castile. "When Manfred is driven out of Sicily they will give the throne to de Gâtinais. He intends to get both a kingdom and a handsome wife by this neat affair. And in reason, England must support my Uncle Richard’s claim to the German crown, against El Sabio﻿—Why, my lad, I ride southward to prevent a war that would devastate half Europe."

===Chinese===
王子继续解读在卡斯蒂利亚策划的阴谋。“当曼弗雷德被赶出西西里时，他们会把王位交给德·加蒂内。他打算通过这件干净利落的事得到一个王国和一个美丽的妻子。而按理说，英格兰必须支持我叔叔理查（Richard）对德国皇位的主张，反对智者阿方索（El Sabio）——听着，小伙子，我南下是为了防止一场会摧毁半个欧洲的战争。”

===Original===
"You ride southward in the attempt to rob a miserable woman of her sole chance of happiness," Miguel de Rueda estimated.

===Chinese===
“你南下是为了试图剥夺一个可怜女人唯一的幸福机会，”米格尔·德·鲁埃达评价道。

===Original===
"That is undeniable, if she loves this thrifty Prince, as indeed I do not question my wife does. Yet our happiness here is a trivial matter, whereas war is a great disaster. You have not seen﻿—as I, my little Miguel, have often seen﻿—a man viewing his death-wound with a face of stupid wonder, a bewildered wretch in point to die in his lord’s quarrel and understanding never a word of it. Or a woman, say﻿—a woman’s twisted and naked body, the breasts yet horribly heaving, in the red ashes of some village, or the already dripping hoofs which will presently crush this body. Well, it is to prevent many such ugly spectacles hereabout that I ride southward."

===Chinese===
“这是不可否认的，如果她爱这位精打细算的王子，正如我确实不怀疑我的妻子所做的一样。然而，我们在这里的幸福是小事，而战争是巨大的灾难。你没有见过——而我，我的小米格尔，经常见过——一个男人看着自己的致命伤时脸上露出愚蠢的惊奇，一个困惑的可怜虫正要死在他主人的争执中却一句话也不懂。或者说，一个女人——一个女人扭曲赤裸的身体，乳房还在可怕地起伏，在某个村庄的红色灰烬中，或者那已经滴水的马蹄即将践踏这具尸体。听着，我南下正是为了防止附近发生许多这样丑陋的景象。”

===Original===
Miguel de Rueda shuddered. But, "She has her right to happiness," the page stubbornly said.

===Chinese===
米格尔·德·鲁埃达颤抖了一下。但是，“她有权获得幸福，”侍童固执地说。

===Original===
"She has only one right," the Prince retorted; "because it has pleased the Emperor of Heaven to appoint us twain to lofty stations, to entrust to us the five talents of the parable; whence is our debt to Him, being fivefold, so much the greater than that of common persons. Therefore the more is it our sole right, being fivefold, to serve God without faltering, and therefore is our happiness, or our unhappiness, the more an inconsiderable matter. For, as I have read in the Annals of the Romans﻿—" He launched upon the story of King Pompey and his daughter, whom a certain duke regarded with impure and improper emotions. "My little Miguel, that ancient king is our Heavenly Father, that only daughter is the rational soul of us, which is here delivered for protection to five soldiers﻿—that is, to the five senses﻿—to preserve it from the devil, the world, and the flesh. But, alas! the too-credulous soul, desirous of gazing upon the gaudy vapors of this world﻿—"

===Chinese===
“她只有一项权利，”王子反驳道；“因为天帝乐意任命我们二人担任崇高职位，将寓言中的五塔兰特托付给我们；因此我们对他的债务，既然增加了五倍，就比普通人的债务大得多。所以，我们五倍的权利是坚定不移地侍奉上帝，因此我们的幸福或不幸就更加无关紧要。因为，正如我在《罗马编年史》中读到的——”他开始讲述庞培国王（King Pompey）和他的女儿的故事，一位公爵对这位女儿产生了不纯洁和不正当的情感。“我的小米格尔，那位古代国王就是我们的天父，那个独生女就是我们理性的灵魂，它被托付给五个士兵——即五种感官——保护它免受魔鬼、世界和肉体的侵害。但是，唉！那过于轻信的灵魂，渴望凝视这个世界华丽的虚妄——”

===Original===
"You whine like a canting friar," the page complained; "and I can assure you that the Lady Ellinor was prompted rather than hindered by her God-given faculties of sight and hearing and so on when she fell in love with de Gâtinais. Of you two, he is, beyond any question, the handsomer and the more intelligent man, and it was God who bestowed on her sufficient wit to perceive the superiority of de Gâtinais. And what am I to deduce from this?"

===Chinese===
“你像虚伪的僧侣一样哀鸣，”侍童抱怨道；“而且我可以向您保证，埃莉诺女士在爱上德·加蒂内时，是被上帝赋予的视觉、听觉等能力所促使，而不是阻碍。在你们两人中，他毫无疑问是更英俊、更聪明的人，是上帝赐予她足够的智慧去认识德·加蒂内的优越性。我该从中推断出什么？”

===Original===
The Prince reflected. At last he said: "I have also read in these same Gestes how Seneca mentions that in poisoned bodies, on account of the malignancy and the coldness of the poison, no worm will engender; but if the body be smitten by lightning, in a few days the carcass will abound with vermin. My little Miguel, both men and women are at birth empoisoned by sin, and then they produce no worm﻿—that is, no virtue. But once they are struck with lightning﻿—that is, by the grace of God﻿—they are astonishingly fruitful in good works."

===Chinese===
王子沉思着。最后他说：“我也在这些《武功记》中读到，塞内卡（Seneca）提到在中毒的身体里，由于毒药的恶毒和寒冷，不会滋生蛆虫；但如果身体被闪电击中，几天内尸体就会爬满蛆虫。我的小米格尔，男人和女人都在出生时被罪孽毒害，因此他们不产生蛆虫——即不产生美德。但一旦他们被闪电击中——即被上帝的恩典击中——他们在善行方面就会惊人地多产。”

===Original===
The page began to laugh. "You are hopelessly absurd, my Prince, though you will never know it﻿—and I hate you a little﻿—and I envy you a great deal."

===Chinese===
侍童开始大笑。“您真是荒谬得无可救药，我的王子，尽管您自己永远不会知道——我有点恨您——但我非常羡慕您。”

===Original===
"Ah, but," Prince Edward said, in misapprehension, for the man was never quick-witted﻿—"but it is not for my own happiness that I ride southward."

===Chinese===
“啊，但是，”爱德华王子误解了，因为他从来不是个机敏的人——“但我南下不是为了我自己的幸福。”

===Original===
The page then said, "What is her name?"

===Chinese===
侍童接着说：“她叫什么名字？”

===Original===
Prince Edward answered, very fondly, "Hawise."

===Chinese===
爱德华王子非常深情地回答：“哈维斯（Hawise）。”

===Original===
"I hate her, too," said Miguel de Rueda; "and I think that the holy angels alone know how profoundly I envy her."

===Chinese===
“我也恨她，”米格尔·德·鲁埃达说；“而且我认为只有圣洁的天使才知道我多么深深地羡慕她。”

===Original===
In the afternoon of the same day they neared Ruffec, and at the ford found three brigands ready, two of whom the Prince slew, and the other fled.

===Chinese===
同一天下午，他们接近吕费克（Ruffec），在渡口发现三个强盗严阵以待，王子杀了其中两个，另一个逃跑了。

===Original===
Next night they supped at Manneville, and sat afterward in the little square, tree-chequered, that lay before their inn. Miguel had procured a lute from the innkeeper, and he strummed idly as these two debated together of great matters; about them was an immeasurable twilight, moonless, but tempered by many stars, and everywhere they could hear a agreeable whispering of leaves.

===Chinese===
第二天晚上他们在马讷维尔（Manneville）用过晚餐，然后坐在旅店前树影斑驳的小广场上。米格尔从旅店老板那里弄来一把鲁特琴，他随意拨弄着琴弦，两人讨论着大事；周围是无边无际的暮色，没有月亮，但被无数星辰柔和地照亮，到处都能听到树叶宜人的沙沙声。

===Original===
"Listen, my Prince," the boy said: "here is one view of the affair." And he began to chant, without rhyming, without raising his voice above the pitch of talk, while the lute monotonously accompanied his chanting.

===Chinese===
“听着，我的王子，”男孩说：“这是对这件事的一种看法。”于是他开始吟唱，没有押韵，没有把声音抬高到谈话音调以上，鲁特琴单调地伴奏着他的吟唱。

===Original===
Sang Miguel:

"Passeth a little while, and Irus the beggar and Menephtah the high king are at sorry unison, and Guenevere is a skull. Multitudinously we tread toward oblivion, as ants hasten toward sugar, and presently Time cometh with his broom. Multitudinously we tread dusty road toward oblivion; but yonder the sun shines upon a grass-plot, converting it into an emerald; and I am aweary of the trodden path.

"Vine-crowned is the fair peril that guards the grasses yonder, and her breasts are naked. 'Vanity of Vanities!' saith the beloved. But she whom I love seems very far away tonight, though I might be with her if I would. And she may not aid me now, for not even love is all-powerful. She is most dear of created women, and very wise, but she may never understand that at any time one grows aweary of the trodden path.

"At sight of my beloved, love closes over my heart like a flood. For the sake of my beloved I have striven, with a good endeavor, to my tiny uttermost. Pardie, I am not Priam at the head of his army! A little while and I will repent; tonight I cannot but remember that there are women whose lips are of a livelier tint, that life is short at best, that wine evokes in me some admiration for myself, and that I am aweary of the trodden path.

"She is very far from me tonight. Yonder in the Hörselberg they exult and make sweet songs, songs which are sweeter, immeasurably sweeter, than this song of mine, but in the trodden path I falter, for I am tired, tired in every fibre of me, and I am aweary of the trodden path."

===Chinese===
米格尔唱道：

“不久之后，乞丐伊鲁斯（Irus）和伟大的国王门涅普塔（Menephtah）悲哀地统一了，桂妮薇儿（Guenevere）成了一个骷髅。我们成群结队地走向遗忘，就像蚂蚁匆忙奔向糖，很快时间带着他的扫帚来了。我们成群结队地走在尘土飞扬的遗忘之路上；但那边阳光照耀着一片草地，将它变成了翡翠；而我厌倦了这踏出的小径。

“那边守护着草地的美丽危险头顶着藤蔓王冠，她的乳房裸露。‘虚空的虚空！’我心爱的人说。但我爱的人今晚似乎非常遥远，虽然如果我愿意，我可以和她在一起。而她现在无法帮助我，因为即使爱也不是全能的。她是我所创造的女人中最珍贵的，非常智慧，但她可能永远不明白，人随时都可能厌倦这踏出的小径。

“看见我心爱的人，爱就像洪水一样淹没我的心。为了我心爱的人，我已竭尽所能，尽我微薄之力。天哪，我不是率领军队的普里阿摩斯（Priam）！一会儿我就会忏悔；今晚我不得不记住，有些女人的嘴唇色彩更鲜艳，生命充其量短暂，葡萄酒在我心中唤起对自己的一些欣赏，而我厌倦了这踏出的小径。

“她今晚离我非常遥远。那边在赫尔塞尔山（Hörselberg）他们欢欣鼓舞，唱着甜蜜的歌，这些歌比我这首歌更甜美，甜美得无法估量，但在踏出的小径上我踌躇不前，因为我累了，每根神经都疲惫不堪，我厌倦了这踏出的小径。”

===Original===
Followed a silence. "Ignorance spoke there," the Prince said. "It is the song of a woman, or else of a boy who is very young. Give me the lute, my little Miguel." And presently the Prince, too, sang.

===Chinese===
接着是一阵沉默。“无知在说话，”王子说。“这是女人的歌，或者是非常年轻的男孩的歌。把鲁特琴给我，我的小米格尔。”不久王子也唱了起来。

===Original===
Sang the Prince:

"I was in a path, and I trod toward the citadel of the land’s Seigneur, and on either side were pleasant and forbidden meadows, having various names. And one trod with me who babbled of the brooding mountains and of the low-lying and adjacent clouds; of the west wind and of the budding fruit-trees. He debated the significance of these things, and he went astray to gather violets, while I walked in the trodden path.

"He babbled of genial wine and of the alert lips of women, of swinging censers and of the serene countenances of priests, and of the clear, lovely colors of bread and butter, and his heart was troubled by a world profuse in beauty. And he leaped a stile to share his allotted provision with a dying dog, and afterward, being hungry, a wall to pilfer apples, while I walked in the trodden path.

"He babbled of Autumn’s bankruptcy and of the age-long lying promises of Spring; and of his own desire to be at rest; and of running waters and of decaying leaves. He babbled of the far-off stars; and he debated whether they were the eyes of God or gases which burned, and he demonstrated, with logic, that neither existed. At times he stumbled as he stared about him and munched his apples, so that he was all bemired, but I walked in the trodden path.

"And the path led to the gateway of a citadel, and through the gateway. 'Let us not enter,' he said, 'for the citadel is vacant, and, moreover, I am in profound terror, and, besides, I have not as yet eaten all my apples.' And he wept aloud, but I was not afraid, for I had walked in the trodden path."

===Chinese===
王子唱道：
