The King said more lately: "And so she is at Stirling now? hobnob with my armed enemies, and cajoling that red lecher Robert Stewart?" He laughed, not overpleasantly. "Eh, yes, it needed a bold person to bring all your tidings! But you Brabanters are a very thoroughgoing people."

===Chinese===

国王后来又说道："所以她现在在斯特灵？与我的武装敌人勾搭在一起，还勾引那个好色的罗伯特·斯图尔特？"他笑了一声，不算好听。"嗯，不错，敢把这消息全带给我，需要一个胆大的人！你们布拉班特人真是事事做绝的民族。"

===Original===

The King rose and flung back his high head. "John, the loyal service you have done us and our esteem for your valour are so great that they may well serve you as an excuse. May shame fall on those who bear you any ill-will! You will now return home, and take your prisoner, the King of Scotland, and deliver him to my wife, to do with as she may elect. You will convey to her my entreaty—not my orders, John—that she come to me here at Calais. As remuneration for this evening's insolence, I assign lands as near your house as you can choose them to the value of £500 a year for you and for your heirs."

===Chinese===

国王站起身来，昂起他那高昂的头颅。"约翰，你对我们忠诚不二，我们对你英勇的器重也非同小可，足以成为你的辩词。让那些对你心怀恶意的人蒙羞吧！你现在回家去，带上你的俘虏——苏格兰国王——将他交给我的妻子，由她任意处置。你替我转达我的恳求——不是命令，约翰——请她到加莱来与我相会。至于今晚这番冒犯的报酬，我在你家附近拨给你每年五百英镑的领地，由你和你的后嗣世代享有。"

===Original===

You must know that John Copeland fell upon his knees before King Edward. "Sire—" he stammered.

But the King raised him. "No, no," he said, "you are the better man. Were there any equity in fate, John Copeland, your lady had loved you, not me. As it is, I must strive to prove not altogether unworthy of my fortune. But I make no large promises," he added, squinting horribly, "because the most generous person cannot render to love any more than that person happens to possess. So be off with you, John Copeland—go, my squire, and bring me back my Queen!"

===Chinese===

须知约翰·科佩兰双膝跪倒在爱德华国王面前。"陛下——"他结结巴巴地说。

但国王扶起了他。"不，不，"他说，"你才是更好的那个人。若命运尚存半点公平，约翰·科佩兰，你心爱的人本该爱你，而非爱我。既然如此，我必须努力证明自己不完全辜负了我的运气。但我不作任何承诺，"他补充道，斜视着，极其骇人，"因为最慷慨的人能给予爱情的，也不过是自己恰好拥有的东西。去吧，约翰·科佩兰——去吧，我的侍从，把我的王后给我带回来！"

===Original===

Presently he heard John Copeland singing without. And through that instant, they say, his youth returned to Edward Plantagenet, and all the scents and shadows and faint sounds of Valenciennes on that ancient night when a tall girl came to him, running, stumbling in her haste to bring him kingship. "She waddles now," he thought forlornly. "Still, I am blessed." But Copeland sang, and the Brabanter's heart was big with joy.

===Chinese===

不一会儿他听到帐外约翰·科佩兰唱起歌来。据说在那一瞬间，爱德华·金雀花（Edward Plantaget）的青春重回身上，瓦朗谢讷那个古老夜晚的所有芬芳、幽影与隐约声响纷至沓来——那晚一个高挑的少女向他跑来，踉踉跄跄，步履匆忙，为他带来了一个王国。"她如今已步履蹒跚了，"他凄然想道。"但我依然有福。"科佩兰还在歌唱，这位布拉班特人的心中充盈着喜悦。

===Original===

Sang John Copeland:

"Long I besought thee, nor vainly,

Daughter of Water and Air—

Charis! Idalia! Hortensis!

Hast thou not heard the prayer,

When the blood stood still with loving,

And the blood in me leapt like wine,

And I cried on thy name, Melaenis?—

That heard me, (the glory is thine!)

And let the heart of Atys,

At last, at last, be mine!

"Falsely they tell of thy dying,

Thou that art older than Death,

And never the Hörselberg hid thee,

Whatever the slanderer saith,

For the stars are as heralds forerunning,

When laughter and love combine

At twilight, in thy light, Melaenis—

That heard me, (the glory is thine!)

And let the heart of Atys,

At last, at last, be mine!"

===Chinese===

约翰·科佩兰唱道：

"我曾长久地祈求你，并非徒然，

水与空气的女儿——

卡里斯（Charis）！伊达利亚（Idalia）！霍尔滕西斯（Hortensis）！

你可曾听见那祈祷——

当血液因爱而凝滞，

当我的血液如酒般沸腾，

我呼唤你的名字，梅拉埃尼斯？——

你听到了，（荣耀归于你！）

让阿提斯的心，

终归、终归属于我！

"他们妄言你已死去，

你比死亡更古老，

赫瑟尔山（Hörselberg）从未将你掩藏，

无论诽谤者如何妄言，

因为星辰是先驱的使者，

当笑声与爱情在暮色中交融，

在你的光辉中，梅拉埃尼斯——

你听到了，（荣耀归于你！）

让阿提斯的心，

终归、终归属于我！"

## VI / 总督的故事

===Original===
"Je suis voix au désert criant

Que chascun soyt rectifiant

La voye de Sauveur; non suis,

Et accomplir je ne le puis."

===Chinese===
"我是旷野中呼喊的声音

让每人修正道路

通往救主之途；非我

且我亦无力完成。"

===Original===
The sixth novel.﻿—Anne of Bohemia has one sole friend, and by him plays the friend's part; and in doing so achieves their common anguish, as well as the confusion of statecraft and the poulticing of a great disease.

===Chinese===
第六部小说。——安妮（Anne）在世上只有一个朋友，而她以友人之身为他行事；由此成就了二人共同的苦痛，搅乱了国政，也敷治了沉疴。

===Original===
In the year of grace 1381 (Nicolas begins) was Dame Anne magnificently fetched from remote Bohemia, and at Westminster married to Sire Richard, the second monarch of that name to reign in England. This king, I must tell you, had succeeded while he was yet an infant, to the throne of his grandfather, the third King Edward, about whom I have told you in the story preceding this.

===Chinese===
主历一三八一年（尼古拉（Nicolas）如此记述），安妮夫人自遥远的波西米亚被隆重迎娶，于威斯敏斯特（Westminster）与理查大人（Richard）成婚。这位理查乃是以此名号君临英格兰的第二位国王。须知此王尚在襒褒之中，便已继其祖父——即我前篇所述的第三位爱德华（Edward）国王——之位登基。

===Original===
Queen Anne had presently noted a certain priest who went forbiddingly about her court, where he was accorded a provisional courtesy, and who went also into many hovels, where pitiable wrecks of humankind received his alms and ministrations.

===Chinese===
安妮王后不久便留意到一位举止可畏的教士，在她宫廷中出入，得享礼遇但皆有保留；此人亦常去往许多茅舍，那些可怜的人间残骸在那里接受他的施舍与照料。

===Original===
Queen Anne made inquiries. This young cleric was amanuensis to the Duke of Gloucester, she learned, and was notoriously a by-blow of the Duke's brother, dead Lionel of Clarence. She sent for this Edward Maudelain. When he came her first perception was, "How wonderful is his likeness to the King!" while the thought's commentary ran, unacknowledged, "Yes, as an eagle resembles a falcon!" For here, to the observant eye, was a more zealous person, already passion-wasted, and a far more dictatorial and stiff-necked person than the lazy and amiable King; also, this Maudelain's face and nose were somewhat too long and high: the priest was, in a word, the less comely of the pair by a very little, and to an immeasurable extent the more kinglike.

===Chinese===
王后暗中打探。这位年轻教士乃格洛斯特公爵（Duke of Gloucester）的文书，据说系公爵亡弟克莱伦斯的莱昂内尔（Lionel of Clarence）的私生子。她召来这位爱德华·莫德莱恩（Edward Maudelain）。他到来时，她的第一个念头是："他与国王何其相似！"而暗中默然的评注则是："不错，恰如鹰隼之别！"——因为在明眼人看来，此人远比那位慵懒温厚的国王更热忱，已被激情耗损，也更为专横倔强；况且莫德莱恩的面容与鼻梁略嫌长而高耸：一言以蔽之，论容貌，此人与国王仅稍逊一筹，论气度，却远为更具王者之象。

===Original===
"You are my cousin now, messire," the Queen told him, and innocently offered to his lips her own.

===Chinese===
"你现在是我的表亲了，先生，"王后对他说，天真地将自己的唇送上他的唇前。

===Original===
He never moved; but their glances crossed, and for that instant she saw the face of a man who has just stepped into a quicksand. She grew red, without knowing why. Then he spoke, composedly, of trivial matters.

===Chinese===
他纹丝未动；但二人目光交汇，那一刹那她看见了一个刚踏入流沙之人的面孔。她不觉涨红了脸。随即他不动声色地谈起琐事。

===Original===
Thus began the Queen's acquaintance with Edward Maudelain. She was by this time the loneliest woman in the island. Her husband granted her a bright and fresh perfection of form and color, but desiderated any appetizing tang, and lamented, in his phrase, a certain kinship to the impeccable loveliness of some female saint in a jaunty tapestry; bright as ice in sunshine, just so her beauty chilled you, he complained: moreover, this daughter of the Caesars had been fetched into England, chiefly, to breed him children, and this she had never done. Undoubtedly he had made a bad bargain﻿—he was too easygoing, people presumed upon it. His barons snatched their cue and esteemed Dame Anne to be negligible; whereas the clergy, finding that she obstinately read the Scriptures in the vulgar tongue, under the irrelevant plea of not comprehending Latin, began to denounce her from their pulpits as a heretic and as the evil woman prophesied by Ezekiel.

===Chinese===
由此开始了王后与爱德华·莫德莱恩的相识。此时她已是这座岛上最孤独的女人。她的丈夫赐予她明亮清新的形色之美，却缺乏令人垂涎的韵味，用他自己的话说，她与华美壁毯上某位完美圣女有几分相仿；那美貌如晴日冰霜，令人生寒。况且这位凯撒之女被迎入英格兰，主要是为他生育子嗣，而她从未做到。毫无疑问，他做了一笔糟糕的交易——他太过随和，人们便借此放肆。他的男爵们纷纷效尤，视安妮夫人于无物；而教士们发现她固执地以不通拉丁文为借口，以俗语阅读《圣经》，便开始在布道坛上谴责她为异端，是以西结所预言的那个邪恶妇人。

===Original===
It was the nature of this desolate child to crave affection, as a necessary, and pitifully she tried to purchase it through almsgiving. In the attempt she could have found no coadjutor more ready than Edward Maudelain. Giving was with these two a sort of obsession, though always he gave in a half scorn of his fellow creatures which was not more than half concealed. This bastard was charitable and pious because he knew his soul, conceived in double sin, to be doubly evil, and therefore doubly in need of redemption through good works.

===Chinese===
这孤寂天性使然，她渴望亲情如渴望水，并卑微地试图以施舍换取。在这项尝试中，她找不到比爱德华·莫德莱恩更热忱的帮手。施予于二人而言近乎一种执念，尽管他始终以半是轻蔑、几乎不加掩饰的态度对待同类。这私生子行善且虔诚，是因为他深知自己那在双重罪孽中孕育的灵魂本就双重邪恶，因而加倍需要以善功赎罪。

===Original===
Now in and about the Queen's lonely rooms the woman and the priest met daily to discuss now this or that point of theology, or now (to cite a single instance) Gammer Tudway's obstinate sciatica. Considerate persons found something of the pathetic in their preoccupation by these matters while, so clamantly, the dissension between the young King and his uncles gathered to a head. The King's uncles meant to continue governing England, with the King as their ward, as long as they could; he meant to relieve himself of this guardianship, and them of their heads, as soon as he was able. War seemed inevitable, the air was thick with portents; and was this, then, an appropriate time, the judicious demanded of high Heaven, for the Queen of imperilled England to concern herself about a peasant's toothache?

===Chinese===
于是王后那孤寂的居所内外，这位女子与教士日日相见，时而论及某项神学要义，时而——仅举一例——论及老妇特德韦（Tudway）顽固的坐骨神经痛。通情达理之人觉得他们沉湎于此等琐事颇为可悲，而此时年轻国王与其叔父们之间日趋激化的纷争已濒临爆发。国王的叔父们意在尽可能长久地以国王为傀儡继续治理英格兰；国王则意在尽快摆脱他们的监护，连同他们的头颅。战争似已不可避免，空气中弥漫着不祥的预兆；明理之人不禁向上天发问：对于岌岌可危的英格兰王后而言，此时关心一个农夫的牙痛，果真合宜么？

===Original===
Long afterward was Edward Maudelain to remember this quiet and amiable period of his life, and to wonder over the man that he had been through this queer while. Embittered and suspicious she had found him, noted for the carping tongue he lacked both power and inclination to bridle; and she had, against his nature, made Maudelain see that every person is at bottom lovable, and that human vices are but the stains of a traveller midway in a dusty journey; and had incited the priest no longer to do good for his soul's health, but simply for his fellow's benefit.

===Chinese===
多年以后，爱德华·莫德莱恩仍会忆起这段宁静而温和的日子，惊异于自己在那段奇异时光里曾是怎样的人。她发现他苦涩多疑，以挑剔之舌著称，既无力也不愿加以约束；而她竟违背其本性，让莫德莱恩看到每个人骨子里都是可爱的，人的恶习不过是风尘旅途中沾染的污渍；她激励这教士不再为灵魂的得救而行善，而仅仅是为了他人的福祉。

===Original===
In place of that monstrous passion which had at first view of her possessed the priest, now, like a sheltered taper, glowed an adoration which made him yearn, in defiance of common sense, to suffer somehow for this beautiful and gracious comrade; though very often pity for her loneliness and knowledge that she dared trust no one save him would throttle Maudelain like two assassins, and would move the hot-blooded young man to a rapture of self-contempt and exultation.

===Chinese===
取代先前初见时攫住教士的那股狰狞激情的，如今如一支被护持的烛焰，燃着一种崇拜之情——令他不顾理智，甘愿为这位美丽而优雅的同伴承受些什么；尽管对她孤寂的怜惜和深知她除他之外无人可信这两个念头，常如两名刺客般扼住莫德莱恩，令这热血青年在自轻的狂喜与得意中激动不已。

===Original===
Now Maudelain made excellent songs, it was a matter of common report. Yet but once in their close friendship did the Queen command him to make a song for her. This had been at Dover, about vespers, in the starved and tiny garden overlooking the English Channel, upon which her apartments faced; and the priest had fingered his lute for an appreciable while before he sang, more harshly than was his custom.

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩善作歌谣，这是众所周知的事。然而在他们亲密的友谊中，王后仅一次命他为自己作歌。那是在多佛尔（Dover），暮祷时分，在俯瞰英吉利海峡的荒僻小园中，她的寓所正对海峡；教士拨弄鲁特琴许久，方才开口歌唱，声调比平日更为粗厉。

===Original===
Sang Maudelain:

"Ave Maria! now cry we so

That see night wake and daylight go.

"Mother and Maid, in nothing incomplete,

This night that gathers is more light and fleet

Than twilight trod alway with stumbling feet,

*Agentes semper uno animo*.

"Ever we touch the prize we dare not take!

Ever we know that thirst we dare not slake!

Yet ever to a dreamed-of goal we make﻿—

*Est tui coeli in palatio!*

"Long, long the road, and set with many a snare;

And to how small sure knowledge are we heir

That blindly tread, with twilight everywhere!

*Volo in toto; sed non valeo!*

"Long, long the road, and very frail are we

That may not lightly curb mortality,

Nor lightly tread together steadfastly,

*Et parvum carmen unum facio:*

"Mater, ora filium,

Ut post hoc exilium

Nobis donet gaudium

Beatorum omnium!"

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩唱道：

"万福玛利亚！吾等今呼唤，
夜色方醒，白昼将逝。
圣母与童贞女，无处不圆满，
今夜之光，较暮色之踉跄步履更迅捷——
*Agentes semper uno animo*。

吾等触碰那不敢取之奖赏！
吾等知晓那不敢解之渴！
却始终向着梦中之境进发——
*Est tui coeli in palatio!*

路漫漫，路漫漫，沿途陷阱密布；
吾等所继承的确知何其微小，
盲目行走，四野皆是暮色——
*Volo in toto; sed non valeo!*

路漫漫，路漫漫，吾等何其脆弱，
不能轻易克制必死之躯，
亦不能轻易同行于恒常之途——
*Et parvum carmen unum facio:*
母亲，为你的儿子祈求，
*Ut post hoc exilium*
*Ut post hoc exilium*
*Ut post hoc exilium*
*Beatorum omnium!*"

===Original===
Dame Anne had risen. She said nothing. She stayed in this posture for a lengthy while, one hand yet clasping each breast. Then she laughed, and began to speak of Long Simon's recent fever. Was there no method of establishing him in another cottage? No, the priest said, the peasants, like the cattle, were always deeded with the land, and Simon could not lawfully be taken away from his owner.

===Chinese===
安妮夫人已站起身来。她一言不发。她就这样伫立了许久，双手仍各捂在胸前。接着她笑了，开始谈起老西蒙（Simon）最近的热病。难道没有办法把他安置到另一间茅舍去吗？不行，教士说，农民如同牲畜，总是随土地一并归属于主人，西蒙不能合法地从他的主人身边被带走。

===Original===
One day, about the hour of prime, in that season of the year when fields smell of young grass, the Duke of Gloucester sent for Edward Maudelain. The court was then at Windsor. The priest came quickly to his patron. He found the Duke in company with the King's other uncle Edmund of York and bland Harry of Derby, who was John of Gaunt's oldest son, and in consequence the King's cousin. Each was a proud and handsome man: Derby alone (who was afterward King of England) had inherited the squint that distinguished this family. Today Gloucester was gnawing at his finger nails, big York seemed half-asleep, and the Earl of Derby appeared patiently to await something as yet ineffably remote.

===Chinese===
一天，在初祷时分，正值田野弥漫新草气息的时节，格洛斯特公爵召见爱德华·莫德莱恩。彼时宫廷在温莎（Windsor）。教士迅速赶来见他的庇护人。他发现公爵正与国王的另一位叔父约克的埃德蒙（Edmund of York）以及温厚的德比的哈利（Harry of Derby）同在——后者是约翰·冈特（John of Gaunt）的长子，因此也是国王的表亲。三人皆是骄傲而英俊的男子：唯有德比（后来成为英格兰国王）继承了这一家族特有的斜视。今日格洛斯特在啃指甲，大个子约克似半睡半醒，而德比伯爵则耐心地等待着某件尚且不可名状之事。

===Original===
"Sit down!" snarled Gloucester. His lean and evil countenance was that of a tired devil. The priest obeyed, wondering that so high an honor should be accorded him in the view of three great noblemen. Then Gloucester said, in his sharp way: "Edward, you know, as England knows, the King's intention toward us three and our adherents. It has come to our demolishment or his. I confess a preference in the matter. I have consulted with the Pope concerning the advisability of taking the crown into my own hands. Edmund here does not want it, and my brother John is already achieving one in Spain. Eh, in imagination I was already King of England, and I had dreamed﻿—Well! today the prosaic courier arrived. Urban﻿—the Neapolitan swine!﻿—dares give me no assistance. It is decreed I shall never reign in these islands. And I had dreamed﻿—Meanwhile, de Vere and de la Pole are at the King day and night, urging revolt. As matters go, within a week or two, the three heads before you will be embellishing Temple Bar. You, of course, they will only hang."

===Chinese===
"坐下！"格洛斯特厉声道。他瘦削而阴鸷的面容宛如一个疲惫的魔鬼。教士依言坐下，对三位大贵族竟给予他如此殊荣感到惊异。随后格洛斯特以他那尖锐的腔调说："爱德华，你知道，正如英格兰所知，国王对我们三人及其追随者的意图。结局非我毁灭他，即他毁灭我。坦白说，我对此事有所偏好。我已就将王冠收入自己手中之事向教廷咨询。埃德蒙不想要它，我的兄弟约翰已在西班牙谋取了一顶。嗯，在想象中我已是英格兰国王，我曾梦想——罢了！今天那平庸的信使到了。乌尔班——那不勒斯的猪猡！——竟拒绝给我援助。我注定不能在这座岛国王临。我曾梦想——与此同时，德维尔（de Vere）与德拉波尔（de la Pole）日夜纠缠国王，怂恿叛乱。照此局势，不出一两周，你们三位面前的这三颗头颅将装点坦普尔巴（Temple Bar）。你们当然，只会被绞死。"

===Original===
"We must avoid England, then, my noble patron," the priest considered.

===Chinese===
"那么我们必须离开英格兰，我尊贵的庇护人，"教士思忖道。

===Original===
Angrily the Duke struck a clenched fist upon the table. "By the Cross! we remain in England, you and I and all of us. Others avoid. The Pope and the Emperor will have none of me. They plead for the Black Prince's heir, for the legitimate heir. Dompnedex! they shall have him!"

===Chinese===
公爵愤怒地以拳击桌。"以十字架起誓！我们留在英格兰，你我以及所有人。别人可以走。教皇与皇帝都不肯接纳我。他们为黑太子（Black Prince）的后嗣求情，为合法继承人求情。该死的！那就让他们如愿吧！"

===Original===
Maudelain recoiled, for he thought this twitching man insane.

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩向后退去，因为他以为这浑身痉挛的人已疯了。

===Original===
"Besides, the King intends to take from me my fief at Sudbury," said the Duke of York, "in order to give it to de Vere. That is both absurd and monstrous and abominable."

===Chinese===
"况且，国王意图夺走我在萨德伯里（Sudbury）的采邑，"约克公爵说，"以赐予德维尔。这既荒谬，又骇人，更可憎。"

===Original===
Openly Gloucester sneered. "Listen!" he rapped out toward Maudelain; "when they were drawing up the Great Peace at Brétigny, it happened, as is notorious, that the Black Prince, my brother, wooed in this town the Demoiselle Alixe Riczi, whom in the outcome he abducted. It is not so generally known, however, that, finding this sister of the Vicomte de Montbrison a girl of obdurate virtue, my brother had prefaced the action by marriage."

===Chinese===
格洛斯特公然嗤笑。"听着！"他转向莫德莱恩厉声说；"当年在布列塔尼签署《大和约》之际，众所周知，我的兄弟黑太子在这座城镇向阿里克丝·里奇小姐（Demoiselle Alixe Riczi）求爱，最终将她劫走。然而不那么广为人知的是，我兄弟发现蒙布里松子爵（Vicomte de Montbrison）的这位姐妹贞烈不屈，遂先以婚姻为铺垫。"

===Original===
"And what have I to do with all this?" said Edward Maudelain.

===Chinese===
"这与我何干？"爱德华·莫德莱恩说。

===Original===
Gloucester retorted: "More than you think. For this Alixe was conveyed to Chertsey, here in England, where at the year's end she died in childbirth. A little before this time had Sir Thomas Holland seen his last day﻿—the husband of that Joane of Kent whom throughout life my brother loved most marvellously. The disposition of the late Queen-Mother is tolerably well known. I make no comment save that to her moulding my brother was as so much wax. In fine, the two lovers were presently married, and their son reigns today in England. The abandoned son of Alixe Riczi was reared by the Cistercians at Chertsey, where some years ago I found you."

===Chinese===
格洛斯特反驳道："关系远超你想象。因为这位阿里克丝被送往英格兰境内的切特西（Chertsey），在年末分娩时死去。此前不久，托马斯·霍兰爵士（Sir Thomas Holland）寿终——此人正是那位肯特的琼安（Joane of Kent）的丈夫，我兄弟终生对她怀有至为奇妙的爱慕。先王太后的倾向已是众所周知。我不置评论，仅说她对我兄弟的塑造，如同蜡在模中。总之，这对恋人不久便结了婚，他们的儿子如今在英格兰为王。阿里克丝·里奇所遗弃的幼子由切特西的熙笃会士抚养，数年前我在那里找到了你。"

===Original===
He spoke with a stifled voice, wrenching forth each sentence; and now with a stiff forefinger flipped a paper across the table. "*In extremis* my brother did more than confess. He signed﻿—your Majesty," said Gloucester. The Duke on a sudden flung out his hands, like a wizard whose necromancy fails, and the palms were bloodied where his nails had cut the flesh.

===Chinese===
他声音压抑，一字一句地吐出；接着以僵硬的手指将一份文件弹过桌面。"*临终之际*，我兄弟所做的远不止忏悔。他签署了——陛下，"格洛斯特说。公爵猛地摊开双手，仿佛一个法术失灵的巫师，掌心已被指甲割出血来。

===Original===
"Moreover, my daughter was born at Sudbury," said the Duke of York.

===Chinese===
"此外，我的女儿生于萨德伯里，"约克公爵补充道。

===Original===
And of Maudelain's face I cannot tell you. He made pretence to read the paper carefully, but his eyes roved, and he knew that he stood among wolves. The room was oddly shaped, with eight equal sides: the ceiling was of a light and brilliant blue, powdered with many golden stars, and the walls were hung with smart tapestries which commemorated the exploits of Theseus. "Then I am King," this Maudelain said aloud, "of France and England, and Lord of Ireland, and Duke of Aquitaine! I perceive that Heaven loves a jest." He wheeled upon Gloucester and spoke with singular irrelevance, "And what is to be done with the present Queen?"

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩的面色，我无法向你描述。他佯装细读那份文件，眼神却四处游移，他知道正置身于狼群之中。房间形状奇特，呈等边八角；天花板是明亮的天蓝色，缀满金色星辰，墙壁上挂着精美的挂毯，描绘忒修斯（Theseus）的功绩。"那么我就是国王，"莫德莱恩朗声说道，"法兰西与英格兰的国王，爱尔兰的领主，阿基坦（Aquitaine）的公爵！我看上天也爱开个玩笑。"他转向格洛斯特，全然答非所问地说："至于现在的王后，该如何处置？"

===Original===
Again the Duke shrugged. "I had not thought of the dumb wench. We have many convents."

===Chinese===
公爵又耸了耸肩。"我未想过那哑巴妞儿。我们有很多修道院。"

===Original===
Now Maudelain twisted the paper between his long, wet fingers and appeared to meditate.

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩在潮湿的长指间捻着那份文件，似乎在沉思。

===Original===
"It would be advisable, your Grace," observed the Earl of Derby, suavely, and breaking his silence for the first time, "that you yourself should wed Dame Anne, once the Apostolic See has granted the necessary dispensation. Treading too close upon the fighting requisite to bring about the dethronement and death of our nominal lord the so-called King, a war with Bohemia, which would be only too apt to follow this noble lady's assassination, would be highly inconvenient, and, lacking that, we would have to pay back her dowry."

===Chinese===
"大人，"德比伯爵圆滑地开口，打破了他此前的沉默，"明智的做法是，一旦宗座授予必要的特许，您亲自迎娶安妮夫人。在为推翻并处死我们名义上的君主——那位所谓的国王——而必须进行的战争之后，紧接着与波西米亚开战——暗杀这位高贵的女士极易引来这样的战争——将极不方便；况且若不如此，我们还得退还她的嫁妆。"

===Original===
Then these three princes rose and knelt before the priest; they were clad in long bright garments, and they glittered with gold and many jewels. He standing among them shuddered in his sombre robe. "Hail, King of England!" cried these three.

===Chinese===
于是三位王子起身跪在教士面前；他们身穿华贵的长袍，金碧辉煌，珠光宝气。他站在他们中间，裹着暗色法衣，瑟瑟发抖。"万岁，英格兰国王！"三人齐声高呼。

===Original===
"Hail, ye that are my kinsmen!" he answered; "hail, ye that spring of an accursed race, as I! And woe to England for that hour wherein Manuel of Poictesme held traffic with the Sorceress of Provence, and the devil's son begot an heir for England! Of ice and of lust and of hellfire are all we sprung; old records attest it; and fickle and cold and ravenous and without shame are all our race until the end. Of your brother's dishonor ye make merchandise today, and today fratricide whispers me, and leers, and, Heaven help me! I attend. O God of Gods! wilt Thou dare bid a man live stainless, having aforetime filled his veins with such a venom? Then haro, will I cry from Thy deepest hell.﻿ ﻿… Oh, now let the adulterous Redeemer of Poictesme rejoice in his tall fires, to note that his descendants know of what wood to make a crutch! You are very wise, my kinsmen. Take your measures, messieurs who are my kinsmen! Though were I of any other race, with what expedition would I now kill you, I that recognize within me the strength to do it! Then would I slay you! without any animosity, would I slay you then, just as I would kill as many splendid snakes!"

===Chinese===
"万岁，我的亲族！"他应道；"万岁，你们这些与我一样出自被诅咒血脉的后裔！英格兰当悲号，因为那一日——当普瓦图的曼努埃尔（Manuel of Poictesme）与普罗旺斯的女巫（Sorceress of Provence）媾合，那恶魔之子为英格兰孕育了继承人！我们皆源于冰霜、淫欲与地狱之火；古籍为证；而我们全族，自始至终，皆是轻浮、冷酷、贪饕且不知羞耻之辈。你们今日以兄长的耻辱为买卖，今日弑亲的念头在我耳边低语、谄笑，而——天助我也！——我在倾听。哦，万神之神！您竟敢要求一个人活得一尘不染，此前却往他的血管里灌满了此等毒液？那么我要从您最深的地狱发出控诉！……哦，现在就让那普瓦图的通奸救赎者在他的高火中欢欣吧，看看他的后裔懂得用什么木头来做拐杖！你们真是聪明，我的亲族。采取你们的措施吧，先生们——你们是我的亲族！若我并非此族血脉，此刻我何等迅捷地杀掉你们——我认出了自己身上有此力量！那么我便要杀你们！毫无怨恨地杀你们，就像杀几条华美的毒蛇一样！"

===Original===
He went away, laughing horribly. Gloucester drummed upon the table, his brows contracted. But the lean Duke said nothing; big York seemed to drowse; and Henry of Derby smiled as he sounded a gong for that scribe who would draw up the necessary letters. The Earl's time was not yet come, but it was nearing.

===Chinese===
他离去时发出可怕的狂笑。格洛斯特以指敲桌，双眉紧锁。瘦削的公爵一言不发；大个子约克似乎在打盹；德比的亨利（Henry of Derby）微笑着敲响铜锣，唤来那位起草必要文书的书记。伯爵的时机尚未到来，但它正在逼近。

===Original===
In the antechamber the priest encountered two men-at-arms dragging a dead body from the castle. The Duke of Kent, Maudelain was informed, had taken a fancy to a peasant girl, and in remonstrance her misguided father had actually tugged at his Grace's sleeve.

===Chinese===
在前厅，教士遇见两名武士拖着一具尸体从城堡中走出。肯特公爵（Duke of Kent）——莫德莱恩被告知——看上了一位农家女，而她那误入歧途的父亲在劝阻时竟拉扯了大人的衣袖。

===Original===
Maudelain went into the park of Windsor, where he walked for a long while alone. It was a fine day in the middle spring; and now he seemed to understand for the first time how fair was his England. For all England was his fief, held in vassalage to God and to no man alive, his heart now sang; allwhither his empire spread, opulent in grain and metal and every revenue of the earth, and in stalwart men (his chattels), and in strong orderly cities, where the windows would be adorned with scarlet hangings, and women (with golden hair and red lax lips) would presently admire as King Edward rode slowly by at the head of a resplendent retinue. And always the King would bow, graciously and without haste, to his shouting people.﻿ ﻿… He laughed to find himself already at rehearsal of the gesture.

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩步入温莎公园，独自漫步良久。那是仲春的一个好天气；此刻他似乎第一次领悟到英格兰是多么美丽。他的心在歌唱：全英格兰皆是他的采邑，臣服于上帝而非在世的任何人；他的帝国处处延展，谷物、矿产和大地的一切物产皆丰饶，还有健壮的子民（他的财产），以及强盛有序的城邦——那里的窗户装饰着猩红的帷幔，妇人们（金发、红唇慵懒）不久便会在骑着灿烂随从队伍缓缓经过的爱德华国王面前发出赞叹。而国王始终会优雅而不急不徐地向欢呼的子民鞠躬。……他笑自己竟已开始排演这手势。

===Original===
It was strange, though, that in this glorious fief of his so many persons should, as yet, live day by day as cattle live, suspicious of all other moving things (with reason), and roused from their incurious and filthy apathy only when some glittering baron, like a resistless eagle, swept uncomfortably near as he passed on some by-errand of the more bright and windy upper-world. East and north they had gone yearly, for so many centuries, these dumb peasants, to fight out their master's uncomprehended quarrel, and to manure with their carcasses the soil of France and of Scotland. Give these serfs a king, now, who (being absolute), might dare to deal in perfect equity with rich and poor, who with his advent would bring Peace into England as his bride, as Trygaeus did very anciently in Athens﻿—"And then," the priest paraphrased, "may England recover all the blessings she has lost, and everywhere the glitter of active steel will cease." For everywhere men would crack a rustic jest or two, unhurriedly. Virid fields would heave brownly under their ploughs; they would find that with practice it was almost as easy to chuckle as it was to cringe.

===Chinese===
然而奇怪的是，在这片辉煌的采邑上，竟有如此多的人至今仍如牛羊般度日，对一切其他活动之物心存戒心（这有其理由），只在某个如不可抗拒之鹰隼般掠过的华贵男爵——因某桩更为明亮与喧嚣的上界事务途经此处——迫近时，才从他们漠然而污秽的麻木中惊醒。多少个世纪以来，这些沉默的农民年复一年东征北讨，为他们不理解的主人之纷争而战，以尸骸肥沃法兰西与苏格兰的土壤。给这些农奴一位国王吧——一位绝对的王，敢于对贫富一秉公允的王；随他降临，将和平如新娘般带入英格兰，正如特赖俄斯（Trygaeus）在远古雅典所做的那样——"如此，"教士转述道，"英格兰或许能重获她失去的一切福祉，到处闪亮的刀兵之光也将消弭。"因为人人都会不慌不忙地说一两个乡间笑话。葱绿的田野会在犁下翻起褐色的泥土；他们会发现，练习之后，发笑几乎与屈膝一样容易。

===Original===
Meanwhile on every side the nobles tyrannized in their degree, well clothed and nourished, but at bottom equally comfortless in condition. As illuminate by lightning Maudelain saw the many factions of his barons squabbling for gross pleasures, like wolves over a corpse, and blindly dealing death to one another to secure at least one more delicious gulp before that inevitable mangling by the teeth of some burlier colleague. The complete misery of England showed before Maudelain like a winter landscape. The thing was questionless. He must tread henceforward without fear among frenzied beasts, and to their ultimate welfare. On a sudden Maudelain knew himself to be invincible and fine, and hesitancy ebbed.

===Chinese===
与此同时，贵族们在各自的等级中暴虐横行，衣食丰足，但骨子里同样处境凄凉。莫德莱恩如闪电照亮般看到他的男爵们分成诸多派系，为粗鄙的享乐彼此争抢，如饿狼争食死尸，盲目地向同类施以死命，只为在被某位更强壮的同僚不可避免地撕碎之前，至少再吞一口甘美的肉。英格兰的全部苦难在莫德莱恩面前展现，如冬日的风景。这毋庸置疑。从此他必须无畏地行走于癫狂的野兽之间，为它们终极的福祉而行。忽然间，莫德莱恩知道自己是无敌且高贵的，犹豫退潮了。

===Original===
True, Richard, poor fool, must die. Squarely the priest faced that stark and hideous circumstance; to spare Richard was beyond his power, and the boy was his brother; yes, this oncoming King Edward would be a fratricide, and after death would be irrevocably damned. To burn, and eternally to burn, and, worst of all, to know that the torment was eternal! ay, it would be hard; but, at the cost of Richard's ignoble life and of Edward's inconsiderable soul, to win so many men to manhood was not a bargain to be refused.

===Chinese===
诚然，理查，可怜的傻瓜，必须死。教士直面这严酷而骇人的处境；饶恕理查非他能力所及，而这少年是他的兄弟；不错，这位即将登基的爱德华国王将成为弑亲者，死后必将万劫不复。焚烧，永远焚烧，而最可怕的是，知道那折磨是永恒的！是的，那将何其艰难；然而，以理查卑微的生命和爱德华微不足道的灵魂为代价，赢得如此多的人走向成年，这笔交易不容拒绝。

===Original===
The tale tells that Maudelain went toward the little garden which adjoined Dame Anne's apartments. He found the Queen there, alone, as nowadays she was for the most part, and he paused to wonder at her bright and singular beauty. How vaguely odd was this beauty, he reflected, too; how alien in its effect to that of any other woman in sturdy England, and how associable it was, somehow, with every wild and gracious denizen of the woods which blossomed yonder.

===Chinese===
故事告诉我们，莫德莱恩向安妮夫人寓所旁的小园走去。他在那里找到了王后，独自一人——如今她多半都是独处——他驻足惊叹于她那明亮而卓异的美。他想，这美貌多么奇异地陌生，与英格兰其他女子的美何其不同，又如何奇异地与远处那片林中每一只狂野而优雅的生灵联系在一起。

===Original===
In this place the world was all sunlight, temperate but undiluted. They had met in a wide, unshaded plot of grass, too short to ripple, which everywhere glowed steadily, like a gem. Right and left, birds sang as if in a contest. The sky was cloudless, a faint and radiant blue throughout, save where the sun stayed as yet in the zenith, so that the Queen's brows cast honey-colored shadows upon either cheek. The priest was greatly troubled by the proud and heatless brilliancies, the shrill joys, of every object within the radius of his senses.

===Chinese===
此处阳光普照，温和而不减。二人在一片宽阔无荫的草地上相遇，草太短而无法起伏，到处如宝石般稳定地闪光。左右两侧，鸟儿仿佛在竞赛般歌唱。天空无云，通体是淡而灿烂的蓝，唯独太阳仍在天顶的位置，使得王后的双颊各投下蜂蜜色的阴影。教士深受感官范围内每一物象那骄傲而无热度的光辉、那尖锐的欢乐所困扰。

===Original===
She was splendidly clothed, in a kirtle of very bright green, tinted like the verdancy of young ferns in sunlight, and wore over all a gown of white, cut open on each side as far as the hips. This garment was embroidered with golden leopards and was trimmed with ermine. About her yellow hair was a chaplet of gold, wherein emeralds glowed. Her blue eyes were as large and shining and changeable (he thought) as two oceans in midsummer; and Maudelain stood motionless and seemed to himself but to revere, as the Earl Ixion did, some bright unstable wisp of cloud, while somehow all elation departed from him as water does from a wetted sponge compressed. He laughed discordantly.

===Chinese===
她穿着华丽，一件极为明亮的绿色长裙，色泽如阳光下的新蕨，外面套着一件白色长袍，两侧开襟至髋部。这件外袍绣着金色的豹纹，镶着白鼬皮。金发上戴着一顶金冠，其中翡翠闪耀。她的蓝眼睛如仲夏的两片大洋般广阔、明亮且变幻莫测（他想）；莫德莱恩一动不动地站着，在他看来自己仿佛在崇拜——如伊克西翁伯爵（Earl Ixion）所做——某个明亮而不稳定的云片，而不知何故，所有的欢欣都如湿海绵被挤压般从他身上流走了。他不谐调地笑了。

===Original===
"Wait﻿—! O my only friend﻿—!" said Maudelain. Then in a level voice he told her all, unhurriedly and without any apparent emotion.

===Chinese===
"等等——！哦，我唯一的朋友——！"莫德莱恩说。然后他以平稳的声音将一切告诉她，不慌不忙，不露任何明显的情绪。

===Original===
She had breathed once, with a deep inhalation. She had screened her countenance from his gaze the while you might have counted fifty. Presently she said: "This means more war, for de Vere and Tressilian and de la Pole and Bramber and others of the barons know that the King's fall signifies their ruin. Many thousands die tomorrow."

===Chinese===
她深吸了一口气。她掩面不让他看的时间，大约够你数到五十。随后她说："这意味着更多的战争，因为德维尔、特雷西里安（Tressilian）、德拉波尔、布拉姆伯（Bramber）以及其他男爵都明白，国王的倒台意味着他们的覆灭。明日将有成千上万人死去。"

===Original===
He answered, "It means a war which will make me King of England, and will make you my wife."

===Chinese===
他答道："这意味着一场战争——它将使我成为英格兰国王，也使我娶你为妻。"

===Original===
"In that war the nobles will ride abroad with banners and gay surcoats, and will kill and ravish in the pauses of their songs; while daily in that war the naked peasants will kill the one the other, without knowing why."

===Chinese===
"在那场战争中，贵族们将打着旗帜、穿着华美的战袍四出征战，在歌声的间隙里杀戮与奸淫；而每日在那场战争里，赤裸的农民将不知缘由地彼此杀戮。"

===Original===
His thought had forerun hers. "Yes, some must die, so that in the end I may be King, and the general happiness may rest at my disposal. The adventure of this world is wonderful, and it goes otherwise than under the strict tutelage of reason."

===Chinese===
他的想法先于她。"是的，必须有人死去，以便最终我能登上王位，大众的福祉能由我裁决。这世间的冒险何其奇妙，它的发展并非循着理性的严格指引。"

===Original===
"It would not be yours, but Gloucester's and his barons'. Friend, they would set you on the throne to be their puppet and to move only as they pulled the strings. Thwart them in their maraudings and they will fling you aside, as the barons have pulled down every king that dared oppose them. No, they desire to live pleasantly, to have fish on Fridays, and white bread and the finest wine the whole year through, and there is not enough for all, say they. Can you alone contend against them? and conquer them? for not unless you can do this may I dare bid you reign."

===Chinese===
"那福祉不会属于你，而属于格洛斯特和他的男爵们。朋友，他们会把你扶上王位，让你做他们的傀儡，只在他们拉线时才动。你在劫掠中与他们作对，他们便会把你扔到一边——正如男爵们历次推翻胆敢反抗他们的国王一样。不，他们想要过得舒适，要周五有鱼，要全年有白面包和最好的酒，而这些不够所有人分，他们说。你一个人能对抗他们吗？能征服他们吗？因为除非你能做到，我才有胆量请你为王。"

===Original===
The sun had grown too bright, too merciless, but as always she drew the truth from him. "I could not venture to oppose in anything the barons who supported my cause: for if I did, I would not endure a fortnight. Heaven help us, nor you nor I nor anyone may transform through any personal force this bitter world, this piercing, cruel place of frost and sun. Charity and Truth are excommunicate, and a king is only an adorned and fearful person who leads wolves toward their quarry, lest, lacking it, they turn and devour him. Everywhere the powerful labor to put one another out of worship, and each to stand the higher with the other's corpse as his pedestal; and Lechery and Greed and Hatred sway these proud and inconsiderate fools as winds blow at will the gay leaves of autumn. We walk among shining vapors, we aspire to overpass a mountain of unstable sparkling sand! We two alone in all the scuffling world! Oh, it is horrible, and I think that Satan plans the jest! We dream for a while of refashioning this bright desolation, and know that we alone can do it! we are as demigods, you and I, in those gallant dreams! and at the end we can but poultice some dirty rascal!"

===Chinese===
阳光变得过于明亮，过于无情，但一如往常，她从他口中引出了真话。"我不敢在任何事上反对支持我的那些男爵：因为若我那样做，我活不过两周。天助我们，无论你我还是任何人都无法凭个人之力改变这个苦涩的世界——这个冷酷的、冰与火相煎的所在。慈善与真理已被逐出教门，而国王不过是一个装饰华丽的可怖人物，他引着狼群走向猎物，唯恐狼群失去它便转身吞噬他。到处是有权势者相互排斥、以对方尸体为基座各自高升的情景；而淫欲、贪婪与仇恨操纵着这些骄傲而无知的愚人，如风随意吹动秋日的彩叶。我们行走在辉煌的雾气中，我们渴望攀越一座不稳定的闪光沙山！在这纷争的世间，唯有我们两人！哦，何其恐怖，我想是撒旦在设计这场玩笑！我们一度梦想重塑这片辉煌的荒芜，知道唯有我们能做到！在那些英勇的梦境中，你我宛如半神！而到头来，我们不过能敷治某个卑劣的无赖！"

===Original===
The Queen answered sadly: "Once and only once did God tread this tangible world, for a very little while, and, look you, to what trivial matters He devoted that brief space! Only to chat with fishermen, and to talk with light women, and to consort with rascals, and at last to die between two cutpurses, ignominiously! If Christ Himself achieved so little that seemed great and admirable, how should we two hope to do any more?"

===Chinese===
王后悲伤地回答："上帝仅一次、仅一次踏足这可触之世，逗留了极短的时间，而你看，祂将那短暂的时刻花在了何等琐碎之事上！仅与渔夫闲谈，与轻浮女子叙话，与恶棍交往，最后死在两个盗贼之间，何其卑辱！倘若基督本人成就的伟大与可敬之事不过如此，你我二人又怎能指望做得更多？"

===Original===
He answered: "It is true. Of anise and of cumin the Master gets His tithe﻿—" Maudelain broke off with a yapping laugh. "Puf! Heaven is wiser than we. I am King of England. It is my heritage."

===Chinese===
他答道："诚然。茴香与芫荽，主人尚取其什一——"莫德莱恩以一声犬吠般的笑打断。"呸！上天比我们明智。我是英格兰国王。这是我的世袭。"

===Original===
"It means war. Many will die, thousands will die, and to no betterment of affairs."

===Chinese===
"这意味着战争。许多人会死，成千上万的人会死，而情势不会因此好转。"

===Original===
"I am King of England. I am Heaven's satrap here, and answerable to Heaven alone. It is my heritage." And now his large and cruel eyes were aflame as he regarded her.

===Chinese===
"我是英格兰国王。我是上天在此的总督（satrap），仅对上天负责。这是我的世袭。"此刻他那双大而残忍的眼睛燃烧着看向她。

===Original===
And visibly beneath their glare the woman changed. "My friend, must I not love you any longer? You would be content with happiness? Then I am jealous of that happiness! for you are the one friend that I have had, and so dear to me﻿—Look you!" she said, with a light, wistful laugh, "there have been times when I was afraid of everything you touched, and I hated everything you looked at. I would not have you stained; I desired to pass my whole life between the four walls of some dingy and eternal gaol, forever alone with you, lest you become like other men. I would in that period have been the very bread you eat, the least perfume which delights you, the clod you touch in crushing it, and I have often loathed some pleasure I derived from life because I might not transfer it to you undiminished. For I wanted somehow to make you happy to my own anguish.﻿ ﻿… It was wicked, I suppose, for the imagining of it made me happy, too."

===Chinese===
而在那注视下，女子显然变了。"我的朋友，我不能再爱你了吗？你会满足于幸福？那么我嫉妒那幸福！因为你是我仅有的朋友，对我如此珍贵——你听我说，"她带着一丝轻柔而怅惘的笑说，"曾经有段时期，我害怕你触碰的一切，憎恶你目视的一切。我不愿你被玷污；我渴望在某座阴暗而永恒的牢狱的四壁之间度过一生，永远与你独处，唯恐你变得与其他男人一样。在那段时光里，我愿做你吃的面包，令你愉悦的最淡的香气，你踩碎时触碰的泥土，而我常常厌恶自己从生活中获得的某种快乐，因为我无法将它毫无减损地传递给你。因为我想以某种方式令你快乐，哪怕令我自己痛苦。……这大概是邪恶的，因为想到这些也令我快乐。"

===Original===
Now while he listened to this dear and tranquil speaking, Edward Maudelain's raised hands had fallen like so much lead, and remembering his own nature, he longed for annihilation, before she had appraised his vileness. He said:

===Chinese===
此刻，当爱德华·莫德莱恩倾听这亲切而宁静的话语时，他抬起的双手如铅般垂落；想起自己的本性，在她估量他的卑劣之前，他便已渴望毁灭。他说：

===Original===
"With reason Augustine crieth out against the lust of the eyes. 'For pleasure seeketh objects beautiful, melodious, fragrant, savory, and soft; but this disease those contrary as well, not for the sake of suffering annoyance, but out of the lust of making trial of them!' Ah! ah! too curiously I planned my own damnation, too presumptuously I had esteemed my soul a worthy scapegoat, and I had gilded my enormity with many lies. Yet indeed, indeed, I had believed brave things, I had planned a not ignoble bargain﻿—! Ey, say, is it not laughable, madame?﻿—as my birthright Heaven accords me a penny, and with that only penny I must presently be seeking to bribe Heaven."

===Chinese===
"难怪奥古斯丁（Augustine）要高声痛斥肉欲之眼。'因为快感追逐美丽、悦耳、芬芳、可口与柔滑之物；但这疾病也追逐相反之物，并非为了忍受烦扰，而出于试探它们的欲望！'啊！啊！我过于好奇地为自己设计了那万劫不复，过于自负地以为自己的灵魂配做替罪羊，用诸多谎言为自己的罪孽镀金。然而确实，确实，我曾相信过英勇之事，我曾策划过一笔不失高贵的交易——嘿，说啊，这不可笑么，夫人？——上天赐予我的世袭不过一枚便士，而我须以这唯一的便士去贿赂上天。"

===Original===
Then he said: "Yet are we indeed God's satraps, as but now I cried in my vainglory, and we hold within our palms the destiny of many peoples. Depardieux! God is wiser than we are. Still, Satan offers no unhandsome bribes﻿—bribes that are tangible and sure. For Satan, too, is wiser than we are."

===Chinese===
然后他说："然而我们确是上帝的总督，正如方才我在虚荣中所呼喊的那样，我们掌握着诸多民族的命运。天哪！上天比我们明智。然而，撒旦开出的贿赂也不难看——那些切实而可靠的贿赂。因为撒旦也比我们明智。"

===Original===
They stood like effigies, lit by the broad, unsparing splendor of the morning, but again their kindling eyes had met, and again the man shuddered. "Decide! oh, decide very quickly, my only friend!" he said, "for throughout I am all filth!"

===Chinese===
他们如雕像般站立，沐浴在广博而无情的晨光中，但他们那再次点燃的目光又一次交汇，那男人再次战栗。"决断！哦，快快决断，我唯一的朋友！"他说，"因为我自始至终皆是污秽！"

===Original===
Closer she drew to him, and laid one hand upon each shoulder. "O my only friend!" she breathed, with red lax lips which were very near to his, "through these six years I have ranked your friendship as the chief of all my honors! and I pray God with an entire heart that I may die so soon as I have done what I must do today!"

===Chinese===
她更靠近他，将双手放在他的双肩上。"哦，我唯一的朋友！"她呼吸急促地说，红唇慵懒而距他极近，"这六年来，我一直将你的友谊列为我一切荣誉之首！我全心祈祷，在我完成今日必须做的事之后立刻死去！"

===Original===
Now Maudelain was trying to smile, but he could not quite manage it. "God save King Richard!" said the priest. "For by the cowardice and greed and ignorance of little men is Salomon himself confounded, and by them is Hercules lightly unhorsed. Were I Leviathan, whose bones were long ago picked clean by pismires, I could perform nothing against the will of many human pismires. Therefore do you pronounce my doom."

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩试图微笑，但未能如愿。"上帝保佑理查国王！"教士说。"因为正是那些渺小之人的怯懦、贪婪与无知挫败了所罗门（Salomon），正是他们轻易地使赫拉克勒斯（Hercules）落马。即便我是利维坦（Leviathan），那骨头早已被蚁群啃净的巨兽，我也无法违逆众多人类蚁群的意愿行事。因此请你宣判我的命运。"

===Original===
"O King," then said Dame Anne, "I bid you go forever from the court and live forever a landless man, friendless, and without even any name. Otherwise, you can in no way escape being made an instrument to bring about the misery and death of many thousands. This doom I dare adjudge and to pronounce, because we are royal and God's satraps, you and I."

===Chinese===
"哦，国王，"安妮夫人说，"我命你永远离开宫廷，永远做一个没有封地的人，没有朋友，甚至没有名字。否则，你将无法避免被利用为工具，造成成千上万人的苦难与死亡。我敢于宣判并宣告此命运，因为你是王族，是上帝的总督，你和我。"

===Original===
Twice or thrice his dry lips moved before he spoke. He was aware of innumerable birds that carolled with a piercing and intolerable sweetness. "O Queen!" he hoarsely said, "O fellow satrap! Heaven has many fiefs. A fair province is wasted and accords to Heaven no revenue. So wastes beauty, and a shrewd wit, and an illimitable charity, which of their pride go in fetters and achieve no increase. Today the young King junkets with his flatterers, and but rarely thinks of England. You have that beauty by which men are lightly conquered, and the mere sight of which may well cause a man's voice to tremble as my voice trembles now, and through desire of which﻿—But I tread afield! Of that beauty you have made no profit. O daughter of the Caesars, I bid you now gird either loin for an unlovely traffic. Old Legion must be fought with fire. True that the age is sick, true that we may not cure, we can but salve the hurt﻿—" His hand had torn open his sombre gown, and the man's bared breast shone in the sunlight, and on his breast heaved sleek and glittering beads of sweat. Twice he cried the Queen's name. In a while he said: "I bid you weave incessantly such snares of brain and body as may lure King Richard to be swayed by you, until against his will you daily guide this shallow-hearted fool to some commendable action. I bid you live as other folk do hereabouts. Coax! beg! cheat! wheedle! lie!" he barked like a teased dog, "and play the prostitute for him that wears my crown, till you achieve in part the task which is denied me. This doom I dare adjudge and to pronounce, because we are royal and God's satraps, you and I."

===Chinese===
他的干裂的嘴唇动了两三次才开口。他听到无数鸟儿以尖锐而不可忍受的甜美歌唱。"哦，王后！"他嘶哑地说，"哦，我的同僚总督！上天有许多采邑。一块美好的省份荒废了，不给上天带来任何收益。美貌如此荒废，精明的才智如此荒废，无限的慈悲也如此荒废——它们因骄傲而戴着镣铐，不能增殖。今日年轻的国王与他的谄媚者们纵酒作乐，难得想到英格兰。你拥有那令人轻易折服的美貌，光是看见它便足以使一个男人的声音颤抖，如同我此刻颤抖的声音，而因渴望它——但我离题了！你未曾利用那美貌谋利。哦，凯撒之女，我命你现在束紧腰带，去做那不体面的交易。对付古老军团（Legion）须以火攻。诚然这时代病了，诚然我们无法治愈，我们只能敷治创伤——"他猛然撕开暗色法衣，那人袒露的胸膛在阳光下闪烁，汗珠如珠，滑落起伏。他两次呼唤王后的名字。过了一会儿他说："我命你编织无休无止的心机与肉体之罗网，诱使理查国王受你摆布，直到你日复一日地引导这浅薄之徒做出某种可敬之举，即便违背他的本意。我命你像周围其他人那样生活。哄骗！乞求！欺骗！花言巧语！撒谎！"他如一条被戏弄的狗般吠叫，"为戴着我的王冠的人充当娼妓，直到你完成我被剥夺的那份任务。我敢于宣判并宣告此命运，因为你是王族，是上帝的总督，你和我。"

===Original===
She answered with a tiny, wordless sound. But presently, "I take my doom," the Queen proudly said. "I shall be lonely now, my only friend, and yet﻿—it does not matter," the Queen said, with a little shiver. "No, nothing will ever greatly matter now, I think, now that I may not ever see you any more, my dearest."

===Chinese===
她以一声微弱的、无言的声音回应。但随即，"我接受我的命运，"王后骄傲地说。"我现在将要孤独了，我唯一的朋友，然而——这不重要，"王后微微发颤地说。"不，从今往后没有什么会真正重要了，我想，因为我永远不能再见到你了，我最亲爱的。"

===Original===
Her eyes had filled with tears; she was unhappy, and, as always, this knowledge roused in Maudelain a sort of frenzied pity and a hatred, quite illogical, of all other things existent. She was unhappy, that only he comprehended: and for her to be made unhappy was unjust.

===Chinese===
她眼中噙满了泪水；她不快乐，而莫德莱恩一如往常，这认知在他心中激起了一种狂热的怜悯，以及对一切其他存在之物的全然非理性的憎恨。她不快乐，唯有他明白；而令她不快乐是不公正的。

===Original===
So he stood thus for an appreciable silence, staying motionless save that behind his back his fingers were bruising one another. Everywhere was this or that bright color and an incessant melody. It was unbearable. Then it was over; the ordered progress of all happenings was apparent, simple, and natural; and contentment came into his heart like a flight of linnets over level fields at dawn. He left her, and as he went he sang.

===Chinese===
他就这样站了许久，一动不动，唯有背后的手指在相互拧紧。到处是或此或彼的明亮色彩与无尽的旋律。令人无法忍受。然而一切结束了；一切事态的有序进程显得明显、简单而自然；安宁如黎明飞过平原的红雀群一般涌入他的心田。他离开了她，边走边唱。

===Original===
Sang Maudelain:

"Christ save us all, as well He can,

*A solis ortus cardine!*

For He is both God and man,

*Qui natus est de virgine,*

And we but part of His wide plan

That sing, and heartily sing we,

'*Gloria Tibi, Domine!*'

"Between a heifer and an ass

*Enixa est puerpera;*

In ragged woollen clad He was

*Qui régnât super aethera,*

And patiently may we then pass

That sing, and heartily sing we,

'*Gloria Tibi, Domine!*' "

===Chinese===
莫德莱恩唱道：

"基督拯救我等，尽其能，
*A solis ortus cardine!*
因祂既是神又是人，
*Qui natus est de virgine,*
我等仅是祂宏大计划的一部分，
歌唱，我等纵情歌唱，
'Gloria Tibi, Domine!'

在母牛与驴之间，
*Enixa est puerpera;*
祂身着粗毛衣而来，
*Qui régnât super aethera,*
而我等当耐心度过，
歌唱，我等纵情歌唱，
'Gloria Tibi, Domine!'"

===Original===
The Queen shivered in the glad sunlight. "I am, it must be, pitiably weak," she said at last, "because I cannot sing as he does. And, since I am not very wise, were he to return even now﻿—But he will not return. He will never return," the Queen repeated, carefully. "It is strange I cannot comprehend that he will never return! Ah, Mother of God!" she cried, with a steadier voice, "grant that I may weep! nay, of thy infinite mercy let me presently find the heart to weep!" And about the Queen of England many birds sang joyously.

===Chinese===
王后在欢悦的阳光中微微发颤。"我必定是可怜地软弱，"她终于说，"因为我不能像他那样歌唱。而且，既然我不甚聪慧，倘若他此刻回来——但他不会回来。他永远不会回来，"王后小心地重复道。"奇怪的是我竟不能理解他永远不会回来了！啊，圣母！"她以更稳定的声音喊道，"恩准我哭泣吧！不，以你无限的仁慈，让我此刻找到哭泣的勇气吧！"英格兰王后周围，许多鸟儿欢快地歌唱。

===Original===
She sent for the King that evening, after supper, and they may well have talked of many matters, for he did not return to his own apartments that night. Next day the English barons held a council, and in the midst of it King Richard demanded to be told his age.

===Chinese===
当晚晚膳之后，她派人去请国王，他们想必谈了许多事，因为那夜他没有回到自己的寓所。次日英格兰男爵们举行了一次会议，期间理查国王要求被告知自己的年龄。

===Original===
"Your Grace is in your twenty-second year," said the uneasy Gloucester, who was now with reason troubled, since he had been vainly seeking everywhere for the evanished Maudelain.

===Chinese===
"陛下正值二十二岁，"神色不安的格洛斯特答道。他此时确有理由忧心，因为他一直在四处寻找消失的莫德莱恩，一无所获。

===Original===
"Then I have been under tutors and governors longer than any other ward in my dominion. My lords, I thank you for your past services, but I need them no more." They had no check handy, and Gloucester in particular foreread his death-warrant, but of necessity he shouted with the others, "Hail, King of England!"

===Chinese===
"那么我受教于导师与监护人的时间，比我治下任何一位受监护人都长。诸位大人，我感谢你们过去的效力，但我不再需要了。"他们一时语塞，格洛斯特尤其预感到了自己的死刑令，但他不得不与其他人一同高喊："万岁，英格兰国王！"

===Original===
That afternoon the King's assumption of all royal responsibility was commemorated by a tournament, over which Dame Anne presided. Sixty of her ladies led as many knights by silver chains into the tilting-grounds at Smithfield, and it was remarked that the Queen appeared unusually mirthful. The King was in high good humor, a pattern of conjugal devotion; and the royal pair retired at dusk to the Bishop of London's palace at Saint Paul's, where was held a merry banquet, with dancing both before and after supper.

===Chinese===
那天下午，国王全面承担王权之事由一场锦标赛来纪念，安妮夫人主持。她的六十位侍女各牵着一位骑士的银链步入史密斯菲尔德（Smithfield）的比武场，人们注意到王后显得异常欢愉。国王兴致极高，是忠贞不渝的典范；黄昏时分，王室夫妇退往圣保罗（Saint Paul's）的伦敦主教宫，那里举行了欢乐的晚宴，餐前餐后皆有舞蹈。

## VII / 第七章

===Original===
The Story of the Heritage

"Pour vous je suis en prison mise,

En ceste chambre à voulte grise,

Et traineray ma triste vie

Sans que jamais mon cueur varie,

Car toujours seray vostre amye."

The seventh novel.—Isabel of Valois, being forsaken by all others, is befriended by a priest, who in chief through a child's innocence, contrives and executes a laudable imposture, and wins thereby to death.

===Chinese===
遗产的故事

Pour vous je suis en prison mise,

En ceste chambre à voulte grise,

Et traineray ma triste vie

Sans que jamais mon cueur varie,

Car toujours seray vostre amye.

第七则故事——瓦卢瓦的伊莎贝尔（Isabel of Valois）遭众人遗弃，一神父施以援手，此神父主要借一个孩童的天真，策划并实施了一桩值得称颂的骗局，最终以此赢得赴死之途。

===Original===
In the year of grace 1399 (Nicolas begins) dwelt in a hut near Caer Dathyl in Arvon, as he had dwelt for some five years, a gaunt hermit, notoriously consecrate, whom neighboring Welshmen revered as the Blessed Evrawc. There had been a time when people called him Edward Maudelain, but this period he dared not often remember.

For though in macerations of the flesh, in fasting, and in hour-long prayers he spent his days, this holy man was much troubled by devils. He got little rest because of them. Sometimes would come into his hut Belphegor in the likeness of a butler, and whisper, "Sire, had you been King, as was your right, you had drunk today not water but the wines of Spain and Hungary." Or Asmodeus saying, "Sire, had you been King, as was your right, you had lain now not upon the bare earth but on cushions of silk."

===Chinese===
一三九九年（尼古拉如此开始），在阿尔文（Arvon）卡尔·达西尔（Caer Dathyl）附近的一间小屋里，住着一位形容枯槁的隐士——他在此已住了约五年——此人以苦行闻名，附近的威尔士人尊称他为神圣的埃夫劳克（The Blessed Evrawc）。曾有一个时期，人们叫他爱德华·莫德莱恩（Edward Maudelain），但这段过往他不敢常常忆起。

因为尽管他以肉体苦修、斋戒和长达数小时的祈祷打发日子，这位圣人却深受恶魔侵扰。他因这些恶魔而难得安眠。有时别西卜（Belphegor）会化作管家的模样进入他的小屋，低声说："阁下，倘若您依权利成为国王，今日您饮的便不是水，而是西班牙与匈牙利的美酒。"或者阿斯蒙蒂斯（Asmodeus）说："阁下，倘若您依权利成为国王，此刻您卧的便不是光秃秃的地面，而是丝绸靠垫。"

===Original===
One day in early spring, they say, the spirit called Orvendile sent the likeness of a fair woman with yellow hair and large blue eyes. She wore a massive crown which seemed too heavy for her frailness to sustain. Soft tranquil eyes had lifted from her book. "You are my cousin now, messire," this phantom had appeared to say.

That was the worst, and Maudelain began to fear he was a little mad because even this he had resisted with many aves.

===Chinese===
据说在早春某日，那个名叫奥文迪尔（Orvendile）的精灵派遣了一个幻象前来——一位美丽的金发女子，生着一双大大的蓝眼睛。她头戴一顶沉重的冠冕，似乎以她那纤弱的身躯难以承受。她从书上抬起温柔而宁静的目光。"阁下，您现在是我的表亲了，"这幻象似乎如此说道。

这是最糟糕的一次，莫德莱恩开始担心自己已有些疯了，因为他对这个也以连串的圣母经抵抗住了。

===Original===
There came also to his hut, through a sullen snowstorm, upon the afternoon of All Soul's day, a horseman in a long cloak of black. He tethered his black horse and he came noiselessly through the doorway of the hut, and upon his breast and shoulders the snow was white as the bleached bones of those women that died in Merlin's youth.

"Greetings in God's name, Messire Edward Maudelain," the stranger said.

Since the newcomer spoke intrepidly of holy things a cheerier Maudelain knew that this at least was no demon. "Greetings!" he answered. "But I am Evrawc. You name a man long dead."

===Chinese===
万灵节那天下午，穿过一场沉闷的暴风雪，一个骑马人来到了他的小屋。此人身披黑色长斗篷。他拴好黑马，悄无声息地穿过小屋门口，胸膛与肩头上积雪皑皑，白如梅林年轻时代那些死去女子的枯骨。

"以上帝之名向您致意，爱德华·莫德莱恩阁下，"陌生人说。

来人言辞大胆地谈及神圣之物，心情稍安的莫德莱恩知道这至少不是恶魔。"致意！"他答道。"但我叫埃夫劳克。您说的那个人早已死了。"

===Original===
"But it is from a certain Bohemian woman I come. What matter, then, if the dead receive me?" And thus speaking, the stranger dropped his cloak.

He was clad, as you now saw, in flame-colored satin, which shimmered with each movement like a high flame. He had the appearance of a tall, lean youngster, with crisp, curling, very dark red hair. He now regarded Maudelain. He displayed peculiarly wide-set brown eyes; and their gaze was tender, and the tears somehow had come to Maudelain's eyes because of his great love for this tall stranger. "Eh, from the dead to the dead I travel, as ever," said the newcomer, "with a message and a token. My message runs, 'Time is, O fellow satrap!' and my token is this."

===Chinese===
"但我来自一位波希米亚女子那里。那么，纵然死者接待我，又有何妨？"说罢，陌生人脱下了斗篷。

此刻您看到，他身着火焰色的缎子，每动一下便如高扬的火焰般熠熠生辉。他看上去是个瘦高的年轻人，一头浓密卷曲的深红褐色头发。此刻他注视着莫德莱恩。他那双分得特别开的棕色眼睛流露着温柔的目光，而泪水不知怎的涌上了莫德莱恩的眼眶，因为他对这位高大的陌生人怀有深切的爱。"唉，我一如既往，从死者到死者，"来人说，"带来一个口信和一件信物。我的口信是：'时候到了，同僚总督！'我的信物是这个。"

===Original===
In this packet, wrapped with white parchment and tied with a golden cord, was only a lock of hair. It lay like a little yellow serpent in Maudelain's palm. "And yet five years ago," he mused, "this hair was turned to dust. God keep us all!" Then he saw the tall lean emissary puffed out like a candle-flame; and upon the floor he saw the huddled cloak waver and spread like ink, and he saw the white parchment slowly dwindle, as snow melts under the open sun. But in his hand remained the lock of yellow hair.

"O my only friend," said Maudelain, "I may not comprehend, but I know that by no unhallowed art have you won back to me." Hair by hair he scattered upon the floor that which he held. "*Time is!* and I have not need of any token to spur my memory." He prized up a corner of the hearthstone, took out a small leather bag, and that day purchased a horse and a sword.

===Chinese===
这小包用白羊皮纸包裹，以金绳系着，里面只有一绺头发。它躺在莫德莱恩的掌心，像一条小黄蛇。"然而五年前，"他沉思道，"这头发已化为尘土。上帝保佑我们所有人！"接着他看见那瘦高的使者像烛焰一样扑地熄灭；他看见地上蜷缩的斗篷如墨水般荡漾散开，看见白羊皮纸慢慢消融，如同雪在烈日下融化。但那绺黄发仍留在他的手中。

"噢，我唯一的朋友，"莫德莱恩说，"我或许无法理解，但我确知你并非凭借任何亵渎的法术回到我身边的。"他一缕一缕地将手中的发丝撒落在地。"*时候到了！*我不需要任何信物来唤醒我的记忆。"他撬起壁炉石的一角，取出一个小皮袋，当天便买了一匹马和一把剑。

===Original===
At dawn the Blessed Evrawc rode eastward in secular apparel. Two weeks later he came to Sunninghill; and it happened that the same morning the Earl of Salisbury, who had excellent reason to consider…

Follows a lacuna of fourteen pages. Maudelain's successful imposture of his half-brother, Richard the Second, so strangely favored by their physical resemblance, and the subsequent fiasco at Circencester, are now, however, tolerably well known to students of history.

===Chinese===
黎明时分，神圣的埃夫劳克身着世俗服装策马东行。两周后他抵达桑宁希尔（Sunninghill）；恰在同一天早晨，索尔兹伯里伯爵（Earl of Salisbury）——他有充分的理由认为……

以下缺失十四页。莫德莱恩成功冒充了他的同父异母弟弟理查二世（Richard the Second），两人的外貌奇异地相似，而此后在瑟伦塞斯特（Circencester）的惨败，如今在历史学者看来已大致为人所知。

===Original===
In one way or another, Maudelain contrived to take the place of his now dethroned brother, and therewith also the punishment designed for Richard. It would seem evident, from the Argument of the story in hand, that Nicolas de Caen attributes a large part of this mysterious business to the co-operancy of Isabel of Valois, King Richard's eleven year old wife. And (should one have a taste for the deductive) the foregoing name of Orvendile, when compared with "The Story of the Scabbard," would certainly hint that Owain Glyndwyr had a finger in the affair.

===Chinese===
无论如何，莫德莱恩设法取代了他那已被废黜的兄弟的位置，同时也承受了原本为理查准备的惩罚。从本故事的纲要来看，尼古拉·德·卡昂（Nicolas de Caen）似乎将这桩神秘事件的大部分归功于瓦卢瓦的伊莎贝尔（Isabel of Valois）——理查国王年仅十一岁的妻子——的协助。（若有兴趣推演）上文出现的奥文迪尔之名，与《剑鞘的故事》（The Story of the Scabbard）对照，无疑暗示欧文·格兰道尔（Owain Glyndwyr）也插手了此事。

===Original===
It is impossible to divine by what method, according to Nicolas, this Edward Maudelain was substituted for his younger brother. Nicolas, if you are to believe his "Epilogue," had the best of reasons for knowing that the prisoner locked up in Pontefract Castle in the February of 1400, after Harry of Derby had seized the crown of England, was not Richard Plantagenet: as is attested, also, by the remaining fragment of this same "Story of the Heritage."

===Chinese===
按照尼古拉的说法，这个爱德华·莫德莱恩是用什么方法被替换成他弟弟的，已无从推测。如果你相信尼古拉的"跋"，他有最充分的理由知道——一四〇〇年二月，德比的哈里（Harry of Derby）夺取英格兰王冠之后，关在蓬特弗拉克特城堡（Pontefract Castle）中的那个囚犯，并非理查·金雀花（Richard Plantagenet）：这同一则"遗产的故事"残存的片段也为此提供了佐证。

===Original===
… and eight men-at-arms followed him.

Quickly Maudelain rose from the table, pushing his tall chair aside, and as he did this, one of the soldiers closed the door securely. "Nay, eat your fill, Sire Richard," said Piers Exton, "since you will not ever eat again."

"Is it so?" the trapped man answered quietly. "Then indeed you come in a good hour." Once only he smote upon his breast. "Mea culpa! O Eternal Father, do Thou shrive me very quickly of all those sins I have committed, both in thought and deed, for now the time is very short."

===Chinese===
……八名武装侍从尾随其后。

莫德莱恩迅速从桌旁站起，推开高背椅；就在他这样做的时候，一名士兵牢牢地关上了门。"不必了，吃个够吧，理查陛下，"皮尔斯·埃克斯顿（Piers Exton）说，"因为您再也吃不到了。"

"当真？"被困之人平静地答道。"那你们确实来得正是时候。"他只捶了一次胸膛。"Mea culpa（是我的罪过）！哦，永恒的天父，求您速速赦免我所犯的一切罪过，无论是思想上的还是行为上的，因为现在时间所剩无几了。"

===Original===
And Exton spat upon the dusty floor. "Foh, they had told me I would find a king here. I discover only a cat that whines."

"Then 'ware his claws!" As a viper leaps Maudelain sprang upon the nearest fellow and wrested away his halberd. "Then 'ware his claws, my men! For I come of an accursed race. And now let some of you lament that hour wherein the devil's son begot an heir for England! For of ice and of lust and of hellfire are all we sprung; old records attest it; and fickle and cold and ravenous and without fear are all our race until the end. Hah, until the end! O God of Gods!" this Maudelain cried, with a great voice, "wilt Thou dare bid a man die patiently, having aforetime filled his veins with such a venom? For I lack the grace to die as all Thy saints have died, without one carnal blow struck in my own defence. I lack the grace, my Father, for even at the last the devil's blood You gave me is not quelled. I dare atone for that old sin done by my father in the flesh, but yet I must atone as befits the race of Oriander!"

===Chinese===
埃克斯顿朝满是灰尘的地上啐了一口。"呸，他们跟我说这里有个国王。我只发现一只喵喵叫的猫。"

"那就小心他的爪子！"莫德莱恩像毒蛇一样纵身而起，扑向最近的那人，夺过他的长戟。"那就小心他的爪子，弟兄们！因为我出身于一个被诅咒的种族。现在让你们中的一些人去哀悼那个时辰吧——魔鬼的儿子为英格兰诞下了继承人！因为我们都生于冰、欲与地狱之火；古老的文献为证；我们整个种族直到终结都是反复无常、冷酷贪婪、无所畏惧的。哈，直到终结！哦，万神之神！"莫德莱恩以洪亮的声音喊道，"您岂敢让一个人在血管中早已灌满如此毒液之后，仍要他忍耐地死去？因为我缺乏像您所有圣徒那样死去的恩典——毫无肉身的自卫之击。我缺乏这份恩典，我的天父，因为即便到了最后，您赋予我的魔鬼之血仍未平息。我敢为我父亲所犯的肉身旧罪赎罪，但我必须以奥里安德（Oriander）家族应有的方式赎罪！"

===Original===
Then it was he and not they who pressed to the attack. Their meeting was a bloody business, for in that dark and crowded room Maudelain raged among his nine antagonists like an angered lion among wolves.

They struck at random and cursed shrilly, for they were now half-afraid of this prey they had entrapped; so that presently he was all hacked and bleeding, though as yet he had no mortal wound. Four of these men he had killed by this time, and Piers Exton also lay at his feet.

===Chinese===
于是是他而不是他们发起了进攻。这场交锋是一场血腥之事，在那间昏暗拥挤的房间里，莫德莱恩在他九个对手中间暴怒如狂，如同一头被激怒的雄狮在群狼之中。

他们乱砍乱劈，尖声咒骂，因为他们此刻已对这个陷阱中的猎物产生了几分惧怕；所以他很快就浑身刀伤、鲜血淋漓，但尚未受致命伤。此时他已杀死了四个人，皮尔斯·埃克斯顿也倒在他的脚下。

===Original===
Then the other four drew back a little. "Are ye tired so soon?" said Maudelain, and he laughed terribly. "What, even you! Why, look ye, my bold veterans, I never killed before today, and I am not breathed as yet."

Thus he boasted, exultant in his strength. But the other men saw that behind him Piers Exton had crawled into the chair from which (they thought) King Richard had just risen, and they saw Exton standing erect in this chair, with both arms raised. They saw this Exton strike the King with his poleaxe, from behind, once only, and they knew no more was needed.

===Chinese===
于是余下四人稍稍退后。"你们这么快就累了？"莫德莱恩说，发出可怕的笑声。"怎么，连你们也这样！嘿，看看吧，我勇敢的老兵们，我今天之前从未杀过人，现在还不曾喘不过气来呢。"

他就这样自夸着，为自己的力量而得意。但其他人看到，他身后皮尔斯·埃克斯顿已爬进了那把椅子——他们以为理查国王刚才就是从那把椅子上站起来的——他们看到埃克斯顿直直地站在这把椅子上，双臂高举。他们看到埃克斯顿从背后用长柄战斧砍了国王一下，只此一下，他们知道无需更多了。

===Original===
"By God!" said one of them in the ensuing stillness, and it was he who bled the most, "that was a felon's blow."

But the dying man who lay before them made as though to smile. "I charge you all to witness," he faintly said, "how willingly I render to Caesar's daughter that which was ever hers."

Then Exton fretted, as if with a little trace of shame: "Who would have thought the rascal had remembered that first wife of his so long? Caesar's daughter, saith he! and dares *in extremis* to pervert Holy Scripture like any Wycliffite! Well, he is as dead as that first Caesar now, and our gracious King, I think, will sleep the better for it. And yet—God only knows! for they are an odd race, even as he said—these men that have old Manuel's blood in them."

===Chinese===
"天哪！"在随后的寂静中，他们中失血最多的那个说道，"那一击是死罪之击。"

但躺在他们面前的垂死之人似乎露出一丝微笑。"我要求你们所有人作证，"他微弱地说，"我是多么心甘情愿地将那从来就属于凯撒之女的东西归还给她。"

埃克斯顿烦躁起来，似乎带着几分羞愧："谁会想到这个无赖竟记了那么久他的第一个妻子？凯撒之女，他说！在临终之际还胆敢像任何威克里夫派（Wycliffite）教徒一样篡改《圣经》！好吧，他现在已和那个最初的凯撒一样死了，而我们仁慈的国王——我想——会因此睡得更安稳。然而——只有上帝知道！因为他们是一个古怪的种族，正如他自己所说——这些血管中流着老曼努埃尔血液的人。"

## VIII / 剑鞘的故事

===Original===
“Ainsi il avait trouvé sa mie
							
							Si belle qu’on put souhaiter.
							
							N’avoit cure d’ailleurs plaider,
							
							Fors qu’avec lui manoir et estre.
							
							Bien est Amour puissant et maistre.”

===Chinese===
“于是他找到了他的爱人
							
							如此美丽，令人心驰神往。
							
							他不再愿与他人争论，
							
							只想与她同居共处。
							
							爱神如此强大而主宰一切。”

===Original===
The eighth novel.﻿—Branwen of Wales gets a king’s love unwittingly, and in all innocence convinces him of the littleness of his kingdom; so that he besieges and in due course occupies another realm as yet unmapped.

===Chinese===
第八个故事。——威尔士的布兰温（Branwen）无意间赢得了国王的爱，又在全然无邪中让他认识到自己王国的渺小；于是他围攻并最终占领了另一片尚未绘制地图的疆域。

===Original===
In the year of grace 1400 (Nicolas begins) King Richard, the second monarch of that name to rule in England, wrenched his own existence, and nothing more, from the close wiles of his cousin, Harry of Derby, who was now sometimes called Henry of Lancaster, and sometimes Bolingbroke. The circumstances of this evasion having been recorded in the preceding tale, it suffices here to record that this Henry was presently crowned King of England in Richard’s place. All persons, saving only Owain Glyndwyr and Henry of Lancaster, believed King Richard dead at that period when Richard attended his own funeral, as a proceeding taking to the fancy, and, among many others, saw the body of Edward Maudelain interred with every regal ceremony in the chapel at Langley Bower. Then alone Sire Richard crossed the seas, and at thirty-three set out to inspect a transformed and gratefully untrammelling world wherein not a foot of land belonged to him.

===Chinese===
在公元1400年（尼古拉斯如是说），英格兰的理查（Richard）国王——历史上第二位以此名号统治英格兰的君主——从其表亲德比的哈里（Harry of Derby）的重重诡计中挣脱出来，除了他自己的性命外一无所获。这位哈里此时有时被称为亨利（Henry）·兰开斯特，有时又叫博林布鲁克（Bolingbroke）。这场逃脱的详情在前一篇故事中已有记述，此处只需记录：亨利很快加冕为英格兰国王，取代了理查的位置。除了欧文·格林杜尔（Owain Glyndwyr）和亨利·兰开斯特本人，所有人都以为理查国王已经死了——那时理查却参加自己的葬礼，视之为有趣的仪式，并在众人之中目睹爱德华·莫德莱恩（Edward Maudelain）的遗体以全套王室礼仪安葬在兰利堡（Langley Bower）的礼拜堂里。此后，理查爵士独自渡海，在三十三岁之年启程，去检视一个已然变样、令人欣然解放的世界，而那世界上没有一寸土地属于他。

===Original===
Holland was the surname he assumed, the name of his half-brothers; and to detail his Asian wanderings would be tedious and unprofitable. But at the end of each four months would come to him a certain messenger from Glyndwyr, supposed by Richard to be the imp Orvendile, who notoriously ran every day around the world upon the Welshman’s business. It was in the Isle of Taprobane, where the pismires are as great as hounds, and mine and store the gold of which the inhabitants afterward rob them through a very cunning device, that this emissary brought the letter which read simply, “Now is England fit pasture for the White Hart.” Presently Richard Holland was in Wales, and then he rode to Sycharth.

===Chinese===
他化名霍兰（Holland），取自他同父异母兄弟的名字；若要详述他在亚洲的游历，不免冗长乏味。但每隔四个月，总会有一名格林杜尔的信使前来，理查以为是那精灵奥文迪尔（Orvendile），此人天天绕世界一圈为威尔士人奔走，是出了名的。就在这托普罗巴纳岛（Taprobane）——那里的蚂蚁大如猎犬，挖掘并储存黄金，而岛民后来用一种极为狡猾的装置盗取这些黄金——这名使者送来的信上只有一句话：“英格兰如今已是白鹿的牧场了。”不久，理查·霍兰便来到了威尔士，随后骑马前往锡查斯（Sycharth）。

===Original===
There, after salutation, Glyndwyr gave an account of his long stewardship. It was a puzzling record of obscure and tireless machinations with which we have no immediate concern: in brief, the barons who had ousted King Log had been the very first to find their squinting King Stork intolerable; and Northumberland, Worcester, Douglas, Mortimer, and so on, were already pledged and in open revolt. “By the God I do not altogether serve,” Owain ended, “you have but to declare yourself, sire, and within the moment England is yours.”

===Chinese===
在那里，双方见礼之后，格林杜尔汇报了他漫长的管理事务。这是一份令人困惑的记录，满是晦暗而不懈的阴谋，与我们并无直接关联：简言之，那些驱逐了洛格国王（King Log）的男爵们，最先发现他们斜眼的鹳鸟国王（King Stork）令人难以忍受；诺森伯兰（Northumberland）、伍斯特（Worcester）、道格拉斯（Douglas）、莫蒂默（Mortimer）等人早已结盟，公开反叛。“以我所不全然侍奉的神起誓，”欧文（Owain）总结道，“您只需宣布复位，陛下，顷刻之间英格兰便归您所有。”

===Original===
Richard spoke with narrowed eyes. “You forget that while Henry of Lancaster lives no other man can ever hope to reign tranquilly in these islands. Come then! the hour strikes; and we will coax the devil for once in a way to serve God.”

===Chinese===
理查眯起眼睛说道。“你忘了，只要亨利·兰开斯特活着，旁人就休想在这片群岛上安稳称王。来吧！时辰已到；我们不妨哄骗魔鬼，哪怕就这一回，让它为上帝效力。”

===Original===
“Oh, but there is a boundary appointed,” Glyndwyr moodily returned. “You, too, forget that in cold blood this Henry stabbed my best-loved son. But I do not forget this, and I have tried divers methods which we need not speak of﻿—I who can at will corrupt the air, and cause sickness and storms, raise heavy mists, and create plagues and fires and shipwrecks; yet the life itself I cannot take. For there is a boundary appointed, sire, and beyond that frontier the Master of our Sabbaths cannot serve us even though he would.”

===Chinese===
“可是，有一道命定的界限，”格林杜尔阴郁地回答。“你也忘了，亨利冷酷地刺杀了我最心爱的儿子。但我没有忘记，我也试过种种手段，我们不必细说——我能随意败坏空气，引发疾病和风暴，升起浓雾，制造瘟疫、火灾和海难；但我无法取走他的性命。因为有一道命定的界限，陛下，在那道界限之外，我们安息日的主人（the Master of our Sabbaths）即便愿意也无法为我们效力。”

===Original===
Richard crossed himself. “You horribly mistake my meaning. Your practices are your own affair, and in them I decline to dabble. I merely design to trap a tiger with his appropriate bait. For you have a fief at Caer Idion, I think?﻿—Very well! I intend to herd your sheep there, for a week or two, after the honorable example of Apollo. It is your part to see that Henry knows I am living disguised and defenceless at Caer Idion.”

===Chinese===
理查划了个十字。“你完全误解了我的意思。你的那些法术是你自己的事，我无意掺和。我只是想用适当的诱饵来诱捕一只老虎。你在凯尔·伊迪恩（Caer Idion）有块封地，对吧？——很好！我打算效仿阿波罗（Apollo）的光荣先例，去那里给你牧羊一两周。你的任务是确保亨利知道我正乔装无备地住在凯尔·伊迪恩。”

===Original===
The gaunt Welshman chuckled. “Yes, squinting Henry of Lancaster would cross the world, much less the Severn, to make quite sure of Richard’s death. He would come in his own person with at most some twenty trustworthy followers. I will have a hundred there; and certain aging scores will then be settled in that place.” Glyndwyr meditated afterward, very evilly. “Sire,” he said without prelude, “I do not recognize Richard of Bordeaux. You have garnered much in travelling!”

===Chinese===
那个瘦削的威尔士人咯咯笑了。“是啊，那个斜眼的亨利·兰开斯特会漂洋过海，更别说区区塞文河（Severn），只为确认理查确实死了。他会亲自前来，最多带上二十个可靠随从。我会在那里安排一百人；一些陈年旧账将在那里清算。”格林杜尔随后阴险地盘算起来。“陛下，”他突兀地说，“我不认识波尔多的理查。你旅行途中收获颇丰啊！”

===Original===
“Why, look you,” Richard returned, “I have garnered so much that I do not greatly care whether this scheme succeed or no. With age I begin to contend even more indomitably that a wise man will consider nothing very seriously. You barons here believe it an affair of importance who may chance to be the King of England, say, this time next year; you take sides between Henry and me. I tell you frankly that neither of us, that no man in the world, by reason of innate limitations, can ever rule otherwise than abominably, or, ruling, can create anything save discord. Nor can I see how this matters either, since the discomfort of an ant-village is not, after all, a planet-wrecking disaster. No, Owain, if the planets do indeed sing together, it is, depend upon it, to the burden of ‘Fools All.’ For I am as liberally endowed as most people; and when I consider my abilities, my performances, my instincts, and so on, quite aloofly, as I would appraise those of another person, I can only shrug: and to conceive that common sense, much less Omnipotence, would ever concern itself about the actions of a creature so entirely futile is, to me at least, impossible.”

===Chinese===
“唉，你看，”理查答道，“我收获如此之多，以至于我对这计划成功与否并不十分在意。随着年岁增长，我愈发固执地主张：明智之人不会过于认真地看待任何事。你们这些男爵认为明年此时谁可能碰巧当上英格兰国王是件大事；你们在我和亨利之间选边站。我坦率地告诉你，我们两人，世上所有人，皆因天生的局限，统治方式无不令人厌恶，或者即便统治，也只会制造纷争。我也不明白这有什么关系，毕竟蚁村的不适毕竟不是毁天灭地的大灾祸。不，欧文，如果诸行星确实在齐声歌唱，相信我，唱的一定是‘愚人尽是愚人’。因为我天赋与常人不相上下；当我以旁观者的心态审视自己的能力、成就、本能等等，如同评估他人时，我只能耸肩：若认为常识——更不用说全能者——会关注一个如此全然无用的生物的行动，这对我而言至少是不可能的。”

===Original===
“I have known the thought,” said Owain﻿—“though rarely since I found the Englishwoman that was afterward my wife, and never since my son, my Gruffyd, was murdered by a jesting man. He was more like me than the others, people said.﻿ ﻿… You are as yet the empty scabbard, powerless alike for help or hurt. Ey, hate or love must be the sword, sire, that informs us here, and then, if only for a little while, we are as gods.”

===Chinese===
“我曾有过这种念头，”欧文说——“不过自从我遇见后来成为我妻子的那位英格兰女子后就很少了，自从我的儿子格鲁菲德（Gruffyd）被一个开玩笑的人谋害后就再也没有了。人们说他比其他几个更像我。……你现在还是一只空剑鞘，既无力相助，也无力伤人。唉，陛下，恨或爱才是那把剑，让我们在此显形，然后，哪怕只是片刻，我们便如同神明。”

===Original===
“Pardie! I have loved as often as Salomon, and in fourteen kingdoms.”

===Chinese===
“天哪！我爱的次数和所罗门（Salomon）一样多，而且遍及十四个王国。”

===Original===
“We of Cymry have a saying, sire, that when a man loves par amours the second time he may safely assume that he has never been in love at all.”

===Chinese===
“我们坎布里亚（Cymry）人有句俗话，陛下：一个人第二次坠入爱河时，他大可放心，他从未真正爱过。”

===Original===
“﻿—And I hate Henry of Lancaster as I do the devil.”

===Chinese===
“——我恨亨利·兰开斯特如同恨魔鬼。”

===Original===
“I greatly fear,” said Owain with a sigh, “lest it may be your irreparable malady to hate nothing, not even that which you dislike. No, you consider things with both eyes open, with an unmanly rationality: whereas Sire Henry views all matters with that heroic squint which came into your family from Poictesme.”

===Chinese===
“我深恐，”欧文叹道，“你的不治之症乃是恨无所有，连你厌恶的东西也不恨。不，你用双眼直视事物，带着不合男儿气概的理性：而亨利爵士却用那种英雄式的斜视看待一切，这种斜视是从波伊克特姆（Poictesme）传入你们家族的。”

===Original===
“Be off with your dusty scandals!” said Richard, laughing.

===Chinese===
“带着你那些陈腐的丑闻滚一边去！”理查笑着说。

===Original===
So then Glyndwyr rode south to besiege and burn the town of Caerdyf, while at Caer Idion Richard Holland abode tranquilly for some three weeks. There was in this place only Caradawc (the former shepherd), his wife Alundyne, and their sole daughter Branwen. They gladly perceived Sire Richard was no more a peasant than he was a curmudgeon; as Caradawc observed: “It is perfectly apparent that the robe of Padarn Beisrudd, which refuses to adjust itself to any save highborn persons, would fit him as a glove does the hand; but we will ask no questions, since it is not wholesome to dispute the orderings of Owain Glyndwyr.”

===Chinese===
于是格林杜尔骑马南下，围攻并焚毁了卡迪夫（Caerdyf）镇，而理查·霍兰则在凯尔·伊迪恩安然住了大约三周。那里只有卡拉道克（Caradawc）（从前的牧羊人）、他的妻子阿伦丁（Alundyne）以及他们唯一的女儿布兰温（Branwen）。他们欣然察觉理查爵士既非农夫也非吝啬鬼；正如卡拉道克所言：“显然，帕达恩·贝斯鲁德（Padarn Beisrudd）的长袍——那件只适合高贵之人的长袍——穿在他身上如同手套贴手；但我们不会多问，因为质疑欧文·格林杜尔的安排是不妥当的。”

===Original===
Now day by day would Richard Holland drive the flocks to pasture near the Severn, and loll there in the shade, and make songs to his lute. He grew to love this leisured life of bright and open spaces; and its long solitudes, grateful with the warm odors of growing things and with poignant bird-noises; and the tranquillity of these meadows, that were always void of hurry, bedrugged the man through many fruitless and contented hours.

===Chinese===
理查·霍兰每日将羊群赶到塞文河边的草场放牧，自己则在树荫下闲卧，用鲁特琴创作歌曲。他渐渐爱上了这悠然自在的生活：开阔明亮的空间；悠长的独处时光，弥漫着植物生长的温暖气息和鸟儿清越的鸣叫；这些草场的宁静——始终不含匆忙——令这男人在许多虚度而满足的时光中沉醉。

===Original===
Each day at noon Branwen would bring his dinner, and she would sometimes chat with him while he ate. After supper he would discourse to Branwen of remote kingdoms, through which, as aimlessly as a wind veers, he had ridden at adventure, among sedate and alien peoples who adjudged him a madman; and she, in turn, would tell him curious tales from the *Red Book of Hergest*﻿—telling of Gwalchmai, and Peredur, and Geraint, in each one of which fine heroes she had presently discerned an inadequate forerunnership of Richard’s existence.

===Chinese===
每天正午，布兰温都会送来午餐，有时一边陪他吃饭一边聊天。晚餐后，他会向布兰温讲述那些遥远的王国——他曾像风儿般随意漫游其间，探访那些沉静而异乡的民族，他们视他为疯子；而她则回以《赫格斯特红书》（Red Book of Hergest）中的奇闻——讲格瓦尔赫迈（Gwalchmai）、佩雷杜尔（Peredur）和杰兰特（Geraint），在这几位出色英雄身上，她都隐约看到了理查生涯的不充分先驱。

===Original===
This Branwen was a fair wench, slender and hardy. She had the bold demeanor of a child who is ignorant of evil and in consequence of suspicion. Happily, though, had she been named for that unhappy lady of old, the wife of King Matholwch, for this Branwen, too, had a white, thin, wistful face, like that of an empress on a silver coin which is a little worn. Her eyes were large and brilliant, colored like clear emeralds, and her abundant hair was so much cornfloss, only it was more brightly yellow and was of immeasurably finer texture. In full sunlight her cheeks were frosted like the surface of a peach, but the underlying cool pink of them was rather that of a cloud just after sunset, Richard decided. In all, a taking morsel! though her shapely hands were hard with labor, and she rarely laughed; for, as if in recompense, her heart was tender, and she rarely ceased to smile as though she were thinking of some peculiar and wonderful secret which she intended, in due time, to share with you and with nobody else. Branwen had many lovers, and preferred among them young Gwyllem ap Llyr, a portly lad, who was handsome enough, though he had tiny and piggish eyes, and who sang divinely.

===Chinese===
这位布兰温是个漂亮姑娘，苗条而结实。她有着不知邪恶与猜疑为何物的孩童般的大胆神态。不过，她幸好以那位古代不幸女士——马索尔赫（Matholwch）国王之妻——的名字命名，因为这位布兰温同样有一张苍白、清瘦、忧郁的脸，宛如一枚略有磨损的银币上女皇的肖像。她的眼睛大而明亮，如清澈的祖母绿般翠绿，浓密的头发恰似玉米绒，只是更亮黄，质地也细致得无可比拟。在充足的阳光下，她的双颊如同桃子表面般覆着细霜，但那底层的冷粉色更像日落后的云彩，理查如此判断。总之，是个可人儿！尽管她匀称的双手因劳作而粗糙，她也难得发笑；因为，仿佛补偿似的，她心地温柔，而且几乎总是带着微笑，仿佛心中藏着某个奇妙而特别的秘密，打算在合适的时候与你、且只与你分享。布兰温有许多爱慕者，其中她偏爱年轻的格威勒姆·阿普·利尔（Gwyllem ap Llyr），一个丰满的小伙子，相貌尚可，虽有双小而如猪的眼睛，且歌声极美。

===Original===
One day this Gwyllem came to Richard with two quarterstaves. “Saxon,” he said, “you appear a stout man. Take your pick of these, then, and have at you.”

===Chinese===
一天，这个格威勒姆拿着两根长棍来找理查。“撒克逊人（Saxon），”他说，“你看上去是个结实汉子。挑一根吧，咱们来较量较量。”

===Original===
“Such are not the weapons I would have named,” Richard answered: “yet in reason, Messire Gwyllem, I can deny you nothing that means nothing to me.”

===Chinese===
“这些不是我会选的武器，”理查回答：“不过讲道理，格威勒姆爵士（Messire Gwyllem），凡是于我无关紧要之事，我无法拒绝你。”

===Original===
With that they laid aside their coats and fell to exercise. In these unaccustomed bouts Richard was soundly drubbed, as he had anticipated, but he found himself the stronger man of the two, and he managed somehow to avoid an absolute overthrow. By what method he contrived this he never ascertained.

===Chinese===
说罢，他们脱下外衣，开始练习。在这类不习惯的搏斗中，理查如预料般被狠揍了一顿，但他发觉自己其实是两人中更强壮的那一个，而且设法避免了彻底落败。至于他是如何做到的，他自己始终没弄明白。

===Original===
“I have forgotten what we are fighting about,” he observed, after ten minutes of heroic thumps and hangings; “or, to be perfectly exact, I never knew. But we will fight no more in this place. Come and go with me to Welshpool, Messire Gwyllem, and there we will fight to a conclusion over good sack and claret.”

===Chinese===
“我已经忘了我们为何而战，”经过十分钟英勇的击打与擒抱后，他评论道：“或者确切地说，我从来就不知道。但咱们不在这里打了。跟我去威尔士浦（Welshpool），格威勒姆爵士，我们在那里一边喝着上好的萨克酒（sack）和波尔多红酒（claret），一边决出胜负。”

===Original===
“Content!” cried Gwyllem; “but only if you yield me Branwen.”

===Chinese===
“乐意之至！”格威勒姆叫道：“但你得把布兰温让给我。”

===Original===
“Have we indeed wasted a whole half-hour in squabbling over a woman?” Richard demanded; “like two children in a worldwide toyshop over any one particular toy? Then devil take me if I am not heartily ashamed of my folly! Though, look you, Gwyllem, I would speak naught save commendation of these delicate and livelily-tinted creatures so long as one is able to approach them in a becoming spirit of levity: it is only their not infrequent misuse which I would condemn; and in my opinion the person who elects to build a shrine for any one of them has only himself to blame if his chosen goddess will accept no burnt-offering except his honor and happiness. Yet since time’s youth have many fine men been addicted to this insane practice, as, for example, were Hercules and Merlin to their illimitable sorrow; and, indeed, the more I reconsider the old gallantries of Salomon, and of other venerable and sagacious potentates, the more profoundly am I ashamed of my sex.”

===Chinese===
“我们真的为一个女人白白浪费了整整半小时吗？”理查质问：“就像两个孩子在遍布全球的玩具店里为某一特定玩具争执？那么让魔鬼带走我吧，我真是为自己的愚蠢感到由衷惭愧！不过听着，格威勒姆，只要能以适当的轻浮态度接近这些娇嫩而活泼的生灵，我只会赞美她们：我谴责的只是她们不常被滥用；在我看来，任何选择为她们中任何一位修建神殿的人，若他选中的女神除了他的荣誉与幸福之外不接受任何祭品，那只能怪他自己。然而，自远古以来，许多优秀男子都沉迷于这种疯狂之举，例如赫拉克勒斯（Hercules）和梅林（Merlin），带来了无尽的悲伤；事实上，我越是回想所罗门及其他可敬而睿智的君王们昔日的风流韵事，就越是为自己的性别感到深深的羞愧。”

===Original===
Gwyllem said: “This lazy gabbling of yours is all very fine. Perhaps it is also reasonable. Only when you love you do not reason.”

===Chinese===
格威勒姆说：“你这番懒洋洋的喋喋不休说得好听。也许也很有道理。只是当你爱的时候，你是不会讲道理的。”

===Original===
“I was endeavoring to prove that,” said Richard gently. Then they went to Welshpool, ride and tie on Gwyllem’s horse. Tongue loosened by the claret, Gwyllem raved aloud of Branwen, like a babbling faun, while to each rapture Richard affably assented. In his heart he likened the boy to Dionysos at Naxos, and could find no blame for Ariadne. Moreover, the room was comfortably dark and cool, for thick vines hung about the windows, rustling and tapping pleasantly, and Richard was content.

===Chinese===
“我正试图证明这一点，”理查温和地说。随后他们去了威尔士浦，骑着格威勒姆的马，轮换骑行。红酒打开了话匣子，格威勒姆像只喋喋不休的林中牧神般高声赞美布兰温，而理查则友善地附和每一声赞叹。他内心将这小伙子比作纳克索斯（Naxos）的狄俄尼索斯（Dionysos），对阿里阿德涅（Ariadne）也无从苛责。况且，房间里幽暗舒适，因为浓密的藤蔓垂挂窗边，愉快地沙沙作响、轻敲窗棂，理查心满意足。

===Original===
“She does not love me?” Gwyllem cried. “It is well enough. I do not come to her as one merchant to another, since love was never bartered. Listen, Saxon!” He caught up Richard’s lute. The strings shrieked beneath Gwyllem’s fingers as he fashioned his rude song.

===Chinese===
“她不爱我？”格威勒姆叫道。“这样也好。我并非以商贾之态对她，因为爱情从不交易。听着，撒克逊人！”他抓起理查的鲁特琴。琴弦在格威勒姆的手指下尖叫，他随即创作出粗陋的歌谣。

===Original===
Sang Gwyllem:

===Chinese===
格威勒姆唱道：

===Original===
“Love me or love me not, it is enough
						
						That I have loved you, seeing my whole life is
						
						Uplifted and made glad by the glory of Love﻿—
						
						My life that was a scroll bescrawled and blurred
						
						With tavern-catches, which that pity of his
						
						Erased, and wrote instead one lonely word,
						
						O Branwen!

===Chinese===
“爱我或不爱我，这都无所谓
						
						只要我曾爱过你，我整个生命便因爱的荣光
						
						——我的生命原是一卷潦草模糊的羊皮纸
						
						上面满是酒馆小调，而他的怜悯将其擦去
						
						只写下孤零零的一个词，
						
						哦，布兰温！

===Original===
“I have accorded you incessant praise
						
						And song and service, dear, because of this;
						
						And always I have dreamed incessantly
						
						Who always dreamed, when in oncoming days
						
						This man or that shall love you, and at last
						
						This man or that shall win you, it must be
						
						That, loving him, you will have pity on me
						
						When happiness engenders memory
						
						And long thoughts, nor unkindly, of the past,
						
						O Branwen!

===Chinese===
“我不停地赞美你、歌唱你、侍奉你，亲爱的，皆因此故；
						
						我总在不停地梦想，梦想着未来某日
						
						这个人或那个人会爱上你，最终
						
						这个人或那个人会赢得你，那时
						
						你爱着他，必会怜悯我
						
						当幸福唤起记忆
						
						以及对往事悠长而温和的思绪，
						
						哦，布兰温！

===Original===
“Of this I know not surely, who am sure
						
						That I shall always love you while I live,
						
						And that, when I am dead, with naught to give
						
						Of song or service, Love will yet endure,
						
						And yet retain his last prerogative,
						
						When I lie still, and sleep out centuries,
						
						With dreams of you and the exceeding love
						
						I bore you, and am glad dreaming thereof,
						
						And give God thanks for all, and so find peace,
						
						O Branwen!”

===Chinese===
“对此我并不确定，但我确信
						
						只要活着，我将永远爱你，
						
						而当我死去，不再能献上
						
						歌曲或服务，爱仍将持续，
						
						并保有他最后的特权，
						
						当我静静躺卧，沉睡数百年，
						
						梦中是你和我曾对你怀抱的
						
						深沉爱意，我因此而欣喜，
						
						并为一切感谢上帝，由此得享安宁，
						
						哦，布兰温！”

===Original===
“Now, were I to get as tipsy as that,” Richard enviously thought, midway in a return to his stolid sheep, “I would simply go to sleep and wake up with a headache. And were I to fall as many fathoms deep in love as this Gwyllem ventures, or, rather, as he hurls himself with a splurge, I would perform﻿—I wonder, now, what miracle?”

===Chinese===
“现在，若我也能醉成那样，”理查在返回他那迟钝的羊群途中嫉妒地想，“我只会睡去，醒来时头痛。若我也能像这个格威勒姆般爱得如此之深，或者说，像他那样奋不顾身地投身爱情，我会施展——我想知道，会是什么奇迹？”

===Original===
For he was, though vaguely, discontent.
